    regent: { s: 'nar', t: '{regentHand}', d: { flag: 'petra_regent', standing: { selenne: -40 }, stage: { act2_traitor: 2 }, done: 'act2_traitor', take: 'letters' }, n: 'otto' },
    bribe: { s: 'petra', t: '"There it is." She smiles, and it reaches her eyes, which is worse. "Fifteen hundred marks, a seat at my table, and House Selenne\'s gratitude. The letters go in that brazier. The Prior takes the blame; he\'s dead, he won\'t mind."', o: [
      { t: 'Burn the letters. Take the money.', d: { marks: 1500, standing: { selenne: 50, lantern: -25, choir: -15 }, affinity: { maud: -20, anselm: -15 }, flag: 'petra_bribed', stage: { act2_traitor: 2 }, done: 'act2_traitor', take: 'letters' }, g: 'burned' },
      { t: '"No. I wanted to know your price. Now I know." Expose her.', g: 'expose' },
    ] },
    burned: { s: 'petra', t: 'The letters curl in the brazier. Petra counts out your marks herself, slowly, so you can watch. "Welcome to the Council, darling. Everyone here has a price. Now so do you."\n\nWalking down the steps, the money is very heavy.' },
    otto: { s: 'ottoline', t: '{ottoLine}' },
  } };

  // ---------------- SILVER TRIAL ----------------
  S.hale_silver = { start: 'a', nodes: {
    a: { s: 'hale', t: '"Silver." Hale says it the way other people say a lover\'s name. "Bronze is when your essence learns to speak. Silver is when it starts to argue back." Brother Anselm is with him, which is unusual; the Choir doesn\'t usually attend.\n\n"The Trial of the Unmaking," Anselm says. "The light will take you apart and keep only what fights to stay. Most people find it... clarifying."', o: [
      { t: 'Step into the circle.', d: { fight: 'trial_silver', win: 'pass', lose: 'fail' } },
      { t: '"Not yet."', g: 'end' },
    ] },
    pass: { s: 'sys', t: '[Breakthrough.]\n[You come apart. You come back. You come back more.]\n[You are Silver-rank. Your abilities have learned new tricks without asking.]', d: { breakthrough: true }, n: 'change' },
    change: { s: 'nar', t: 'Anselm makes the sign of the dawn without seeming to notice he\'s doing it. Hale is writing so fast his pen tears the paper.\n\n{lastChange}', n: 'trait' },
    trait: { s: 'sys', t: '[Your essences offer you a shape to grow into. Choose one. It is permanent.]', pickTrait: true, n: 'done' },
    done: { s: 'hale', t: '"Silver," Hale breathes. "In a season. From Lampwick. The Magisterium will want to dissect you. Metaphorically. Mostly." Anselm puts a hand on his shoulder, and he stops.', d: { standing: { magisterium: 15, choir: 5 } } },
    fail: { s: 'anselm', t: '"The light kept less than you hoped," Anselm says, helping you up. "That\'s all. Rest. Come back whole."', d: { heal: 0.5 } },
  } };

  // ---------------- ACT II FINALE ----------------
  S.act2_end = { start: 'a', nodes: {
    a: { s: 'nar', t: 'The Lodge, late. Maud has a bottle out, which happens twice a year. Tonight there are glasses for you, and for {companionName}, and one more that nobody touches, for Lampwick.', n: 'b' },
    b: { s: 'maud', t: '{act2MaudLine}', n: 'c' },
    c: { s: 'sys', t: '[Act II complete.]\n[The Ashen Circle is broken in Calderhal. Not everywhere: the Prior\'s letters mention other cloisters, other cities, other storms.]\n[Gold-rank is a long way off. You\'ve got time. Probably.]', d: { flag: 'act2_done' } },
  } };

  // ---------------- small events ----------------
  S.ev_wreck_cache = { start: 'a', nodes: {
    a: { s: 'nar', t: 'In the hold of a capsized merchantman: a wreckers\' stash. Bronze cores in a pickle jar, which is a choice.', o: [
      { t: 'Take it.', d: { bcores: 2, marks: 60 }, g: 'end' },
      { t: '[Traverse] Climb to the crow\'s nest first.', if: { social: 'Traverse' }, d: { bcores: 3, marks: 60, items: { elixir: 1 } }, g: 'nest' },
      { t: 'Leave it for the Landing.', d: { affinity: { tarn: 8 }, standing: { lantern: 3 } }, g: 'left' },
    ] },
    nest: { s: 'nar', t: 'The crow\'s nest holds the wreckers\' lookout kit: a spyglass, an elixir, and a second jar of cores. You take the lot, and the jar from the hold.' },
    left: { s: 'nar', t: 'You send word to Tarn. That night the whole Landing eats well, and someone carves your initials into the bridge rail. Badly.' },
  } };
  S.ev_ash_pilgrim = { start: 'a', nodes: {
    a: { s: 'nar', t: 'A pilgrim in grey kneels in the ash, praying toward the Cloister. She looks up as you pass. "Do you feel it? The world, trying to heal?"', o: [
      { t: '"I feel it trying to burn."', g: 'b' },
      { t: '[Comfort] Kneel beside her and talk.', if: { social: 'Comfort' }, d: { standing: { choir: 4 }, flag: 'pilgrim_turned' }, g: 'c' },
    ] },
    b: { s: 'nar', t: 'She smiles at you with great pity and goes back to praying.' },
    c: { s: 'nar', t: 'You talk for an hour. About Lampwick. About Jory\'s pie. At the end she stands, brushes the ash off her knees, and walks back toward the Landing instead of the Cloister.' },
  } };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const U = EB.U, K = EB.K, W = EB.W;
  const G_ = (EB.G = {});
  const C = () => EB.content;
  const STONE_PRICE = { common: 60, uncommon: 110, rare: 220, legendary: 500 };
  const ESS_PRICE = { common: 280, uncommon: 520, rare: 1100, legendary: 3000 };
  G_.STONE_PRICE = STONE_PRICE; G_.ESS_PRICE = ESS_PRICE;
  G_.TUNE = { enemyHp: 0.59, enemyPow: 0.77, elitePow: 0.83, bossPow: 1.0, heroPow: 1.1, eliteHp: 0.75, bossHp: 0.64 };

  // ---------- state ----------
  G_.newGame = function (name, origin, temper, seed) {
    const o = W.ORIGINS[origin];
    const g = {
      v: 1, name: name || 'Wren', origin, temper, seed: seed || (Date.now() % 1e9), hardcore: false,
      day: 1, rank: -1, attrs: { might: 10, swiftness: 10, spirit: 10, resilience: 10 },
      essences: [], confluence: null, abilities: {}, hp: null, mana: null, sta: null,
      marks: o.marks, inv: Object.assign({}, o.items), stones: [], essenceItems: [],
      gear: { weapon: o.gear[0] || null, armor: o.gear[1] || null, charm: null },
      flags: {}, standing: {}, npc: {}, suspicion: o.suspicion || 0, quests: {},
      loc: 'prologue', node: 'gate', hollowRoom: 0, hollowCleared: {}, familiars: {},
      coresToday: {}, readings: 0, journal: [], stats: { fights: 0, wins: 0, losses: 0 },
      scene: null, rumour: 0, explored: {}, pendingFight: null,
      traits: [], changes: [], scars: 0, burned: [], strainUntil: 0,
    };
    for (const k in o.attrs) g.attrs[k] += o.attrs[k];
    for (const f in W.FACTIONS) g.standing[f] = 0;
    for (const f in o.standing || {}) g.standing[f] += o.standing[f];
    for (const n in W.NPCS) g.npc[n] = { aff: 0, trust: 0 };
    G_.startQuest(g, 'main_bloom');
    G_.note(g, 'You were born, you lived on Lampwick Lane, and then the sky opened.');
    return g;
  };
  G_.rng = (g, salt) => U.makeRng((g.seed + g.day * 7919 + (salt || 0) * 104729) >>> 0);
  G_.note = function (g, msg) { g.journal.unshift({ day: g.day, msg }); if (g.journal.length > 80) g.journal.pop(); };

  // ---------- save / load ----------
  const KEY = 'essencebound.save.v1';
  G_.save = function (g, slot) {
    if (!g || g.dead) return false;
    try { const data = JSON.stringify(g); localStorage.setItem(KEY + (slot || ''), data); if (!slot) G_.pushAuto(g, data); return true; } catch (e) { return false; }
  };
  // Autosave history: a snapshot whenever the day, place or fight count changes (last 6 kept)
  G_._autoSig = null;
  G_.pushAuto = function (g, data) {
    if (g.hardcore) return;
    if (g.scene || g.loc === 'prologue') return;
    const sig = [g.day, g.loc, g.node, g.dungeon, g.stats.fights, g.rank].join('|');
    if (sig === G_._autoSig) return;
    G_._autoSig = sig;
    try {
      const list = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY + '.auto') || '[]');
      if (list[0] && list[0].sig === sig) return;
      list.unshift({ sig, t: Date.now(), info: G_.saveInfo(g), data });
      localStorage.setItem(KEY + '.auto', JSON.stringify(list.slice(0, 6)));
    } catch (e) {}
  };
  G_.autoList = function () { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY + '.auto') || '[]'); } catch (e) { return []; } };
  G_.saveInfo = function (g) {
    const where = g.loc === 'town' ? 'Calderhal' : g.loc === 'hollow' ? (W.DUNGEONS && W.DUNGEONS[g.dungeon || 'glass'] ? W.DUNGEONS[g.dungeon || 'glass'].name : 'a Hollow') : g.loc === 'verge' && W.MAP[g.node] ? W.MAP[g.node].name : 'Lampwick Lane';
    return { name: g.name, day: g.day, rank: W.RANKS[g.rank] || 'Normal', where };
  };
  G_.slotInfo = function (n) { const g = G_.load('.slot' + n); return g ? Object.assign(G_.saveInfo(g), { t: g.savedAt || null }) : null; };
  G_.saveSlot = function (g, n) { g.savedAt = Date.now(); return G_.save(g, '.slot' + n); };
  G_.loadSlot = (n) => G_.load('.slot' + n);
  G_.load = function (slot) {
    try { const s = localStorage.getItem(KEY + (slot || '')); return s ? JSON.parse(s) : null; } catch (e) { return null; }
  };
  // Hardcore: a dead hero's save is deleted and remembered in the Hall of the Fallen.
  G_.buryHero = function (g) {
    try {
      localStorage.removeItem(KEY);
      const fallen = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY + '.fallen') || '[]');
      const rec = { name: g.name, origin: g.origin, rank: G_.rankName(g), day: g.day, enc: g.dead && g.dead.enc, where: g.dead && g.dead.where, fights: g.stats.fights, wins: g.stats.wins, path: g.path ? (EB.W.PATHS[g.path.id] || {}).short : null, pathRank: g.path && EB.W.PATHS[g.path.id] ? EB.W.PATHS[g.path.id].ranks[g.path.rank] : null, essences: G_.allEssences(g).map((e) => G_.ess(e.id).name), t: Date.now() };
      fallen.unshift(rec); localStorage.setItem(KEY + '.fallen', JSON.stringify(fallen.slice(0, 30)));
      return rec;
    } catch (e) { return null; }
  };
  G_.fallen = () => { try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(KEY + '.fallen') || '[]'); } catch (e) { return []; } };
  G_.hasSave = () => { try { return !!localStorage.getItem(KEY); } catch (e) { return false; } };
  G_.exportCode = (g) => btoa(unescape(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(g))));
  G_.importCode = function (code) { try { const g = JSON.parse(decodeURIComponent(escape(atob(code.trim())))); return g && g.v && !g.hardcore && !g.dead ? g : null; } catch (e) { return null; } };

  // ---------- quests / standing ----------
  G_.startQuest = function (g, id) { if (!g.quests[id]) { g.quests[id] = { stage: 0, done: false }; G_.note(g, 'New: ' + W.QUESTS[id].name); return true; } return false; };
  G_.setStage = function (g, id, stage) { const q = g.quests[id] || (g.quests[id] = { stage: 0, done: false }); if (stage > q.stage) q.stage = stage; };
  G_.finishQuest = function (g, id) { const q = g.quests[id] || (g.quests[id] = { stage: 0 }); if (!q.done) { q.done = true; G_.note(g, 'Completed: ' + W.QUESTS[id].name); } };
  G_.questActive = (g, id) => g.quests[id] && !g.quests[id].done;
  G_.tier = function (v) { let t = 'Hostile'; for (const [min, name] of W.TIERS) if (v >= min) t = name; return t; };
  G_.addStanding = function (g, f, n) { g.standing[f] = U.clamp((g.standing[f] || 0) + n, -100, 100); };

  // ---------- essences & abilities ----------
  G_.ess = (id) => C().essences[id];
  G_.allEssences = (g) => g.essences.concat(g.confluence ? [g.confluence] : []);
  G_.abilityIds = function (g) {
    const out = [];
    for (const e of G_.allEssences(g)) {
      const E = G_.ess(e.id);
      out.push(E.starter);
      for (const s of e.stones) if (E.byStone[s]) out.push(E.byStone[s]);
    }
    return out;
  };
  G_.abilityState = function (g, id) { return g.abilities[id] || (g.abilities[id] = { train: 0, rank: Math.max(0, g.rank) }); };
  G_.rankName = (g) => W.RANKS[g.rank] || 'Normal';
  G_.fullSet = (g) => g.essences.length >= 4 && !!g.confluence;
  G_.strained = (g) => (g.strainUntil || 0) > g.day;
  G_.traitMod = function (g, k) { let s = G_.pathMod ? G_.pathMod(g, k) : 0; for (const t of g.traits || []) { const T = W.TRAITS[t]; if (T && T.mods && T.mods[k]) s += T.mods[k]; } return s; };
  G_.hasTaboo = (g) => G_.allEssences(g).some((e) => G_.ess(e.id).taboo > 0);
  G_.socialTags = function (g) {
    const s = new Set();
    for (const id of G_.abilityIds(g)) { const a = C().abilities[id]; if (a && a.social) s.add(a.social.tag); }
    if (g.origin === 'hunter') { s.add('Track'); s.add('Forage'); }
    if (g.origin === 'foundling') { s.add('Faith'); s.add('Comfort'); }
    if (g.origin === 'dropout') s.add('Lore');
    if (g.origin === 'smuggler') { s.add('Contacts'); s.add('Sneak'); }
    if (g.origin === 'heir') { s.add('Persuade'); s.add('Etiquette'); }
    if (g.origin === 'dockhand') { s.add('Labour'); s.add('Carouse'); }
    const ut = G_.utilities(g);
    if (ut.scout) s.add('Scout'); if (ut.traverse) s.add('Traverse'); if (ut.waypoint) s.add('Waypoint');
    return s;
  };
  // Out-of-combat utilities granted by your abilities: {scout: [ability names], ...}
  G_.utilities = function (g) {
    const out = {};
    const add = (u, src) => { (out[u] = out[u] || []).includes(src) || out[u].push(src); };
    for (const id of G_.abilityIds(g)) {
      const a = C().abilities[id]; if (!a) continue;
      for (const u of W.UTIL_BY_STONE[a.stone] || []) add(u, a.name);
      if (a.social) for (const u of W.UTIL_BY_TAG[a.social.tag] || []) add(u, a.name);
    }
    if (g.origin === 'hunter') add('scout', 'Hedge-Hunter training');
    return out;
  };
  G_.hasUtil = (g, u) => !!G_.utilities(g)[u];
  G_.essenceTags = function (g) { const t = new Set(); for (const e of G_.allEssences(g)) for (const k in G_.ess(e.id).tags) t.add(k); return t; };

  G_.attrs = function (g) {
    const a = Object.assign({}, g.attrs);
    for (const e of G_.allEssences(g)) { const aff = G_.ess(e.id).affinity || {}; for (const k in aff) a[k] = (a[k] || 0) + aff[k]; }
    for (const k in a) a[k] += (W.RANK_ATTR[g.rank] || 0) - (g.scars || 0);
    for (const k in a) a[k] = Math.max(3, a[k]);
    return a;
  };
  G_.maxHp = (g) => { const a = G_.attrs(g); return Math.round((40 + 5 * a.resilience) * (1 + G_.gearMod(g, 'max_health') + G_.traitMod(g, 'max_health') - (G_.strained(g) ? 0.15 : 0))); };
  G_.maxMana = (g) => Math.round((30 + 4 * G_.attrs(g).spirit) * (G_.strained(g) ? 0.85 : 1));
  G_.maxSta = (g) => Math.round(30 + 4 * G_.attrs(g).resilience);
  G_.gearMod = function (g, k) { let s = 0; for (const slot of ['weapon', 'armor', 'charm']) { const it = W.ITEMS[g.gear[slot]]; if (it && it.mods && it.mods[k]) s += it.mods[k]; } return s; };
  G_.fullHeal = function (g) { g.hp = G_.maxHp(g); g.mana = G_.maxMana(g); g.sta = G_.maxSta(g); };
  G_.commandCap = (g) => Math.floor(G_.attrs(g).spirit / 7) + 1 + Math.max(0, g.rank);

  // Prologue essence choices: 3 commons weighted by origin attributes, sometimes one uncommon.
  G_.prologueChoices = function (g) {
    const rng = G_.rng(g, 3);
    const o = W.ORIGINS[g.origin];
    const pool = Object.values(C().essences).filter((e) => e.rarity === 'common');
    const score = (e) => { let s = rng() * 2; for (const k in e.affinity || {}) s += (o.attrs[k] || 0) * 0.6; return s; };
    const sorted = pool.map((e) => [score(e), e]).sort((a, b) => b[0] - a[0]).map((x) => x[1]);
    const out = [];
    for (const e of sorted) { if (out.length >= 3) break; if (out.some((x) => Object.keys(x.tags)[0] === Object.keys(e.tags)[0])) continue; out.push(e); }
    if (rng() < 0.35) { const unc = Object.values(C().essences).filter((e) => e.rarity === 'uncommon'); out[2] = unc[Math.floor(rng() * unc.length)]; }
    return out.map((e) => e.id);
  };

  G_.absorbEssence = function (g, id, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    if (G_.allEssences(g).some((e) => e.id === id)) return { error: 'You already have that essence.' };
    if ((g.burned || []).includes(id)) return { error: 'That essence was torn out of you once. It won\'t take again.' };
    if (g.essences.length >= 4) return { error: 'You can hold four essences, plus the confluence.' };
    g.essences.push({ id, stones: [] });
    const E = G_.ess(id);
    G_.abilityState(g, E.starter);
    if (E.taboo) g.suspicion = U.clamp(g.suspicion + 10 * E.taboo, 0, 100);
    if (!opts.free) g.day++;
    G_.note(g, `Absorbed the ${E.name} essence.`);
    const res = { ability: C().abilities[E.starter] };
    if (g.essences.length === 4 && !g.confluence) {
      const cid = G_.confluenceOf(g);
      g.confluence = { id: cid, stones: [] };
      G_.abilityState(g, G_.ess(cid).starter);
      G_.note(g, `Confluence: the four essences produced ${G_.ess(cid).name}.`);
      res.confluence = cid;
      if (g.rank < 0) res.iron = G_.ironAwakening(g);
    }
    if (g.hp != null) { g.hp = Math.min(g.hp, G_.maxHp(g)); }
    return res;
  };

  // Confluence: the essence (not already held) whose tags best match the four, with rarity as tie-breaker, then affinity.
  G_.confluenceOf = function (g) {
    const tags = {}; const aff = {};
    for (const e of g.essences) { const E = G_.ess(e.id); for (const k in E.tags) tags[k] = (tags[k] || 0) + E.tags[k]; for (const k in E.affinity || {}) aff[k] = (aff[k] || 0) + E.affinity[k]; }
    const held = new Set(g.essences.map((e) => e.id));
    const rar = { common: 0, uncommon: 1, rare: 2, legendary: 3 };
    let best = null, bs = -1;
    for (const E of Object.values(C().essences)) {
      if (held.has(E.id) || E.divine || E.astral) continue;
      let s = 0;
      for (const k in E.tags) s += Math.min(tags[k] || 0, 4) * E.tags[k];
      for (const k in E.affinity || {}) s += (aff[k] || 0) * 0.2;
      s += rar[E.rarity] * 0.5;
      if (s > bs) { bs = s; best = E.id; }
    }
    return best;
  };
  G_.confluencePreview = (g, extraId) => { const t = { essences: g.essences.concat([{ id: extraId, stones: [] }]) }; return G_.confluenceOf(t); };

  // ---------- stones ----------
  G_.stoneKeys = () => Object.values(C().stones).map((s) => s.key);
  G_.stoneDef = (key) => Object.values(C().stones).find((s) => s.key === key);
  G_.addStone = function (g, key) {
    if (key === 'random') { const r = G_.rng(g, g.stones.length + 11 + g.stats.fights); const pool = G_.stoneKeys().filter((k) => G_.stoneDef(k).rarity !== 'legendary'); key = pool[Math.floor(r() * pool.length)]; }
    g.stones.push({ key, uid: U.uid('s') + ':' + Date.now() % 100000 });
    return key;
  };
  // Resonance reading: what the stone becomes in each essence
  G_.reading = function (g, key) {
    return G_.allEssences(g).map((e, i) => {
      const E = G_.ess(e.id);
      if (key === 'volatile') return { i, essence: E.name, note: 'Unstable: the result isn\'t settled until absorbed.' };
      if (e.stones.length >= 4) return { i, essence: E.name, note: 'Full (4 stones).' };
      if (e.stones.includes(key)) return { i, essence: E.name, note: 'Already has this stone.' };
      const ab = C().abilities[E.byStone[key]];
      return { i, essence: E.name, ability: ab };
    });
  };
  G_.absorbStone = function (g, uid, essIdx) {
    const st = g.stones.find((s) => s.uid === uid);
    if (!st) return { error: 'No such stone.' };
    const e = G_.allEssences(g)[essIdx];
    if (!e) return { error: 'No such essence.' };
    if (e.stones.length >= 4) return { error: 'That essence is full.' };
    let key = st.key;
    if (key === 'volatile') {
      const r = G_.rng(g, 99 + g.stones.length);
      const pool = G_.stoneKeys().filter((k) => !e.stones.includes(k));
      key = pool[Math.floor(r() * pool.length)];
    }
    if (e.stones.includes(key)) return { error: 'That essence already has this stone.' };
    e.stones.push(key);
    g.stones = g.stones.filter((s) => s !== st);
    const id = G_.ess(e.id).byStone[key];
    G_.abilityState(g, id);
    const ab = C().abilities[id];
    G_.note(g, `Awakened ${ab.name} (${G_.ess(e.id).name} × ${G_.stoneDef(key).name}).`);
    if (ab.type === 'familiar') { const t = EB.B.familiarTemplate(ab); if (t) g.familiars[t] = g.familiars[t] || { stance: 'aggressive' }; }
    return { ability: ab, key };
  };

  // ---------- training ----------
  // Combat training: earned progress, no downside. It also seasons core-fed progress.
  G_.train = function (g, id, amt) {
    const s = G_.abilityState(g, id);
    if (s.rank > Math.max(0, g.rank)) return 0;
    const before = s.train;
    s.train = Math.min(100, s.train + amt);
    return s.train - before;
  };
  // ---------- seasoning: core-fed growth is real but hollow until it's been used in earnest ----------
  // cored: training points from cores at this rank. hollow: unseasoned growth carried over from a breakthrough.
  G_.unseasoned = function (g, id) { const s = G_.abilityState(g, id); return Math.min(100, (s.cored || 0) + (s.hollow || 0)); };
  G_.seasonPenalty = function (g, id) {
    const u = G_.unseasoned(g, id) / 100;
    return { u, power: 1 - 0.25 * u, cost: 1 + 0.2 * u, misfire: 0.12 * u };
  };
  // Using an ability for real works the hollowness out: carried-over growth first, then this rank's cored share.
  G_.season = function (g, id, amt) {
    const s = G_.abilityState(g, id);
    let left = amt, done = 0;
    const take = (k) => { const t = Math.min(s[k] || 0, left); s[k] = (s[k] || 0) - t; left -= t; done += t; };
    take('hollow'); take('cored');
    return done;
  };
  // Iron cores teach Iron abilities well and Bronze ones barely; Bronze cores teach both.
  G_.coreValue = function (g, id, type) {
    const n = g.coresToday[id] || 0;
    const rank = G_.abilityState(g, id).rank;
    const base = type === 'bcore' ? (rank >= 1 ? 13 : 20) : (rank >= 1 ? 3 : 14);
    return Math.max(1, Math.round(base * Math.pow(0.72, n)));
  };
  G_.spendCore = function (g, id, type) {
    type = type || 'core';
    if ((g.inv[type] || 0) <= 0) return { error: 'No cores of that kind.' };
    const s = G_.abilityState(g, id);
    if (s.train >= 100) return { error: 'Already fully trained.' };
    const v = G_.coreValue(g, id, type);
    G_.give(g, type, -1);
    const room = 100 - s.train;
    s.cored = (s.cored || 0) + Math.min(v, room);
    g.coresToday[id] = (g.coresToday[id] || 0) + 1;
    return { gained: G_.train(g, id, v) };
  };
  G_.trialReady = (g) => (g.rank === 0 || g.rank === 1) && G_.fullSet(g) && G_.abilityIds(g).every((id) => G_.abilityState(g, id).train >= 100);
  // The essence tag that shapes a physical change: strongest tag on the given essences not used before
  G_.changeFor = function (g, essIds) {
    const score = {};
    for (const id of essIds) { const E = G_.ess(id); for (const k in E.tags) score[k] = (score[k] || 0) + E.tags[k]; }
    const used = new Set((g.changes || []).map((c) => c.tag));
    const tag = Object.keys(score).filter((t) => W.CHANGES[t] && !used.has(t)).sort((a, b) => score[b] - score[a])[0] || Object.keys(score)[0];
    const c = { tag, text: W.CHANGES[tag] || 'Something in you has settled into a new shape.', rank: g.rank, day: g.day };
    g.changes.push(c);
    return c;
  };
  G_.ironAwakening = function (g) {
    g.rank = 0;
    for (const id of G_.abilityIds(g)) G_.abilityState(g, id);
    if (!g.traits.includes('iron_body')) g.traits.push('iron_body');
    const change = G_.changeFor(g, [g.confluence.id]);
    G_.fullHeal(g);
    G_.setStage(g, 'main_iron', 1); G_.finishQuest(g, 'main_iron');
    G_.startQuest(g, 'main_bronze');
    G_.note(g, 'The full set settled: Iron-rank. ' + change.text);
    return { change, trait: W.TRAITS.iron_body, gain: W.RANK_ATTR[0] - W.RANK_ATTR[-1] };
  };
  // Bronze trait options: the three whose attribute is strongest for you
  G_.bronzeChoices = function (g) {
    const a = G_.attrs(g);
    return Object.entries(W.TRAITS).filter(([, T]) => T.rank === g.rank).sort((x, y) => a[y[1].attr] - a[x[1].attr]).slice(0, 3).map(([id]) => id);
  };
  G_.takeTrait = function (g, id) { if (!g.traits.includes(id)) g.traits.push(id); G_.fullHeal(g); G_.note(g, 'Breakthrough trait: ' + W.TRAITS[id].name + '.'); };
  G_.breakthrough = function (g) {
    g.rank = g.rank + 1;
    g.pendingTrait = true;
    g.lastChange = G_.changeFor(g, G_.allEssences(g).map((e) => e.id));
    for (const id of G_.abilityIds(g)) { const s = G_.abilityState(g, id); s.hollow = Math.round(((s.hollow || 0) + (s.cored || 0)) * 0.6); s.cored = 0; s.rank = g.rank; s.train = 0; }
    G_.fullHeal(g);
    const q = g.rank === 1 ? 'main_bronze' : 'main_silver';
    G_.setStage(g, q, 2); G_.finishQuest(g, q);
    if (g.rank === 1) G_.startQuest(g, 'main_silver');
    G_.note(g, `Broke through to ${W.RANKS[g.rank]}-rank.`);
  };
  G_.camp = function (g) { g.day++; g.coresToday = {}; G_.fullHeal(g); };
  // Run one fight with the essence in control (auto-explore). Returns the battle summary.
  G_.autoFight = function (g, encId) {
    const b = G_.makeBattle(g, encId);
    b.autoPlayer = true;
    let n = 0; while (!b.over && n++ < 900) EB.B.stepOne(b);
    if (!b.over) { b.over = true; b.winner = 'fled'; }
    return G_.resolveBattle(g, b);
  };
  // Where active quests point on the map
  G_.QUEST_NODES = {
    c_wolves: { 0: 'ash' }, c_moths: { 0: 'ash' }, c_mill: { 0: 'mill' }, c_cass: { 0: 'road' },
    main_glass: { 1: 'scar', 2: 'hollow', 3: 'hollow' }, s_ledger: { 0: 'mill' }, s_crate: { 0: 'mill' },
    act2_embers: { 2: 'causeway', 3: 'light' }, act2_cloister: { 0: 'cloister', 1: 'cloister' },
    c_wraiths: { 0: 'flats' }, c_gulls: { 0: 'flats' }, c_tide: { 0: 'wrecks' }, s_landing: { 0: 'landing' },
  };
  G_.questMarkers = function (g) {
    const out = [];
    for (const q in g.quests) {
      const st = g.quests[q]; if (st.done) continue;
      const node = (G_.QUEST_NODES[q] || {})[st.stage];
      if (!node || !W.MAP[node]) continue;
      if (q === 's_crate' && !G_.count(g, 'crate')) continue;
      out.push({ quest: q, name: W.QUESTS[q].name, node, text: W.QUESTS[q].stages[st.stage] });
    }
    return out;
  };
  G_.rest = function (g) {
    g.day++; g.coresToday = {}; G_.fullHeal(g); g.suspicion = Math.max(0, g.suspicion - 2);
  };

  // ---------- the Society stipend: a choice of essences for your first contract ----------
  G_.stipendChoices = function (g) {
    const rng = G_.rng(g, 41);
    const pool = Object.values(C().essences).filter((e) => e.rarity === 'common' && !e.taboo && !G_.allEssences(g).some((x) => x.id === e.id) && !g.essenceItems.includes(e.id));
    const out = [];
    while (out.length < 3 && pool.length) out.push(pool.splice(Math.floor(rng() * pool.length), 1)[0].id);
    return out;
  };

  // ---------- extraction: essences and stones are semi-permanent ----------
  G_.EXTRACT = {
    stone: { magisterium: { marks: 120, days: 1, risk: 0 }, undercroft: { marks: 50, days: 1, risk: 0.35 } },
    essence: { magisterium: { marks: 400, days: 3 } },
  };
  // What happens if you pull a stone: preview text
  G_.stonePreview = function (g, essIdx, key, where) {
    const c = G_.EXTRACT.stone[where];
    const e = G_.allEssences(g)[essIdx]; const E = G_.ess(e.id);
    const ab = C().abilities[E.byStone[key]];
    return { cost: c, lines: [
      `${ab.name} is lost, and the ${G_.stoneDef(key).name} is destroyed in the process.`,
      `Every other ${E.name} ability loses 25% training.`,
      `Essence strain for 3 days: -15% max health and mana.`,
      `Costs ${c.marks} marks and ${c.days} day.`,
      c.risk ? `Back-alley risk: ${Math.round(c.risk * 100)}% chance of a permanent Essence Scar (-1 to every attribute).` : null,
    ].filter(Boolean) };
  };
  G_.extractStone = function (g, essIdx, key, where) {
    const c = G_.EXTRACT.stone[where];
    if (g.marks < c.marks) return { error: `That costs ${c.marks} marks.` };
    const e = G_.allEssences(g)[essIdx]; if (!e || !e.stones.includes(key)) return { error: 'No such stone.' };
    const E = G_.ess(e.id); const lostId = E.byStone[key];
    g.marks -= c.marks;
    e.stones = e.stones.filter((s) => s !== key);
    delete g.abilities[lostId];
    for (const id of [E.starter].concat(e.stones.map((s) => E.byStone[s]))) { const s = G_.abilityState(g, id); s.train = Math.max(0, s.train - 25); }
    const A = C().abilities[lostId];
    if (A.type === 'familiar') { const t = EB.B.familiarTemplate(A); if (t) delete g.familiars[t]; }
    g.day += c.days; g.coresToday = {};
    g.strainUntil = Math.max(g.strainUntil || 0, g.day + 3);
    let scarred = false;
    if (c.risk && G_.rng(g, 7 + g.scars).call(null) < c.risk) { g.scars++; scarred = true; }
    g.hp = Math.min(g.hp, G_.maxHp(g)); g.mana = Math.min(g.mana, G_.maxMana(g));
    G_.note(g, `Had ${A.name} extracted${scarred ? ', and it left a scar' : ''}.`);
    return { lost: A, scarred };
  };
  G_.essencePreview = function (g, essIdx) {
    const c = G_.EXTRACT.essence.magisterium;
    const e = G_.allEssences(g)[essIdx]; const E = G_.ess(e.id);
    const isConf = g.confluence === e;
    const n = 1 + e.stones.length;
    const lines = [`All ${n} ${E.name} abilities are lost, and its ${e.stones.length} stone${e.stones.length === 1 ? '' : 's'} destroyed.`];
    if (isConf) lines.push('A confluence can\'t be removed on its own. Remove one of the four essences that formed it.');
    else if (g.confluence) lines.push(`Your confluence, ${G_.ess(g.confluence.id).name}, collapses with it, along with its abilities and stones.`);
    if (G_.fullSet(g) && !isConf) lines.push(g.rank >= 0 ? 'Your set is broken. You keep your rank, but you can\'t break through again until the set is whole.' : '');
    lines.push('A permanent Essence Scar: -1 to every attribute.', `You can never absorb ${E.name} again.`, 'Essence strain for 7 days: -15% max health and mana.', `Costs ${c.marks} marks and ${c.days} days.`);
    return { cost: c, lines: lines.filter(Boolean), blocked: isConf };
  };
  G_.extractEssence = function (g, essIdx) {
    const c = G_.EXTRACT.essence.magisterium;
    const e = G_.allEssences(g)[essIdx];
    if (!e || g.confluence === e) return { error: 'You can\'t remove that.' };
    if (g.marks < c.marks) return { error: `That costs ${c.marks} marks.` };
    if (G_.allEssences(g).length <= 1) return { error: 'Hale refuses to remove your only essence. "You\'d be a corpse with paperwork."' };
    g.marks -= c.marks;
    const drop = (x) => { const E = G_.ess(x.id); for (const id of [E.starter].concat(x.stones.map((s) => E.byStone[s]))) { const A = C().abilities[id]; if (A && A.type === 'familiar') { const t = EB.B.familiarTemplate(A); if (t) delete g.familiars[t]; } delete g.abilities[id]; } };
    drop(e);
    g.essences = g.essences.filter((x) => x !== e);
    let collapsed = null;
    if (g.confluence) { collapsed = g.confluence.id; drop(g.confluence); g.confluence = null; }
    g.burned.push(e.id);
    g.scars++;
    g.day += c.days; g.coresToday = {};
    g.strainUntil = Math.max(g.strainUntil || 0, g.day + 7);
    g.hp = Math.min(g.hp, G_.maxHp(g)); g.mana = Math.min(g.mana, G_.maxMana(g));
    G_.note(g, `Had the ${G_.ess(e.id).name} essence torn out${collapsed ? `; the ${G_.ess(collapsed).name} confluence collapsed` : ''}.`);
    return { removed: e.id, collapsed };
  };

  // ---------- inventory ----------
  G_.give = function (g, id, n) { g.inv[id] = Math.max(0, (g.inv[id] || 0) + (n == null ? 1 : n)); if (!g.inv[id]) delete g.inv[id]; };
  G_.count = (g, id) => g.inv[id] || 0;

  // ---------- conditions / effects (dialogue & events) ----------
  G_.check = function (g, c) {
    if (!c) return true;
    if (c.origin && g.origin !== c.origin) return false;
    if (c.temper && g.temper !== c.temper) return false;
    if (c.flag && !g.flags[c.flag]) return false;
    if (c.not && g.flags[c.not]) return false;
    if (c.social && !G_.socialTags(g).has(c.social)) return false;
    if (c.tag && !G_.essenceTags(g).has(c.tag)) return false;
    if (c.marks != null && g.marks < c.marks) return false;
    if (c.cores != null && G_.count(g, 'core') < c.cores) return false;
    if (c.item && !G_.count(g, c.item)) return false;
    if (c.items) for (const k in c.items) if (G_.count(g, k) < c.items[k]) return false;
    if (c.rank != null && g.rank !== c.rank) return false;
    if (c.taboo != null && G_.hasTaboo(g) !== c.taboo) return false;
    if (c.trialReady && !G_.trialReady(g)) return false;
    if (c.standing) for (const f in c.standing) if ((g.standing[f] || 0) < c.standing[f]) return false;
    if (c.affinity) for (const n in c.affinity) if ((g.npc[n].aff || 0) < c.affinity[n]) return false;
    if (c.quest) for (const q in c.quest) if (!g.quests[q] || g.quests[q].done || g.quests[q].stage !== c.quest[q]) return false;
    return true;
  };

  // Apply effects; returns list of UI notes, and possibly {fight, scene, goto}
  G_.apply = function (g, d) {
    const out = { notes: [] };
    if (!d) return out;
    const note = (m, cls) => out.notes.push({ m, cls: cls || '' });
    for (const k of ['flag', 'flag2']) if (d[k]) g.flags[d[k]] = true;
    if (d.unflag) delete g.flags[d.unflag];
    if (d.standing) for (const f in d.standing) { G_.addStanding(g, f, d.standing[f]); note(`${W.FACTIONS[f].name} ${d.standing[f] > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.standing[f]}`, d.standing[f] > 0 ? 'good' : 'bad'); }
    if (d.affinity) for (const n in d.affinity) { g.npc[n].aff = U.clamp(g.npc[n].aff + d.affinity[n], -100, 100); note(`${W.NPCS[n].short} ${d.affinity[n] > 0 ? 'likes that' : 'didn\'t like that'}`, d.affinity[n] > 0 ? 'good' : 'bad'); }
    if (d.trust) for (const n in d.trust) g.npc[n].trust = U.clamp(g.npc[n].trust + d.trust[n], -100, 100);
    if (d.marks) { g.marks = Math.max(0, g.marks + d.marks); note(`${d.marks > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.marks} marks`, d.marks > 0 ? 'good' : ''); }
    if (d.bcores) { G_.give(g, 'bcore', d.bcores); note(`+${d.bcores} Bronze Core${d.bcores > 1 ? 's' : ''}`, 'good'); }
    if (d.party !== undefined) { g.party = d.party; if (d.party) note(`${W.ALLIES[d.party].name} joins you.`, 'good'); }
    if (d.shop) out.shop = d.shop;
    if (d.cores) { G_.give(g, 'core', d.cores); note(`${d.cores > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.cores} Iron Core${Math.abs(d.cores) > 1 ? 's' : ''}`, d.cores > 0 ? 'good' : ''); }
    if (d.item) { G_.give(g, d.item, 1); note(`Got: ${W.ITEMS[d.item].name}`, 'good'); }
    if (d.take) G_.give(g, d.take, -1);
    if (d.items) for (const k in d.items) { G_.give(g, k, d.items[k]); note(`${d.items[k] > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.items[k]} ${W.ITEMS[k].name}`, d.items[k] > 0 ? 'good' : ''); }
    if (d.stone) { const key = d.stone === 'volatile' ? (g.stones.push({ key: 'volatile', uid: U.uid('s') + ':' + Date.now() % 100000 }), 'volatile') : G_.addStone(g, d.stone); note(`Got: ${key === 'volatile' ? 'Volatile Stone' : G_.stoneDef(key).name}`, 'good'); }
    if (d.essence) { g.essenceItems.push(d.essence); note(`Got: ${G_.ess(d.essence).name} essence`, 'good'); }
    if (d.suspicion) { g.suspicion = U.clamp(g.suspicion + d.suspicion, 0, 100); note(`Suspicion ${d.suspicion > 0 ? '+' : ''}${d.suspicion}`, d.suspicion > 0 ? 'bad' : 'good'); }
    if (d.quest) G_.startQuest(g, d.quest);
    if (d.quest2) G_.startQuest(g, d.quest2);
    if (d.stage) for (const q in d.stage) G_.setStage(g, q, d.stage[q]);
    if (d.done) G_.finishQuest(g, d.done);
    if (d.heal === true) G_.fullHeal(g);
    else if (typeof d.heal === 'number') g.hp = Math.max(g.hp, Math.round(G_.maxHp(g) * d.heal));
    if (d.rumour) { out.rumour = W.RUMOURS[g.rumour % W.RUMOURS.length]; g.rumour++; if (/Varo emptied/.test(out.rumour)) g.flags.heard_ledger = true; }
    if (d.rankUp === 'iron') { g.rank = 0; G_.fullHeal(g); }
    if (d.breakthrough) G_.breakthrough(g);
    if (d.payContract) out.payContract = true;
    if (d.ending) { g.ending = d.ending; g.flags['ending_' + d.ending] = true; }
    if (d.fight) out.fight = { enc: d.fight, win: d.win, lose: d.lose };
    if (d.scene) out.scene = d.scene;
    if (d.goto) out.goto = d.goto;
    return out;
  };

  // Text templating
  G_.fmt = function (g, t, extra) {
    extra = extra || {};
    return String(t).replace(/\{(\w+)\}/g, (m, k) => {
      if (extra[k] != null) return extra[k];
      switch (k) {
        case 'name': return g.name;
        case 'originLine': return {
          dockhand: 'You are walking home from the wharves with a sore back, a hook on your belt, and no plans beyond soup.',
          foundling: 'You are carrying bread from the chapel kitchens to the Lampwick shut-ins, as you have every evening since you could carry bread.',
          heir: 'You are walking home from a creditor\'s office, composing a letter to your mother that begins "Nothing to worry about".',
          smuggler: 'You are walking home from a job that went fine, which is suspicious, carrying a parcel you have not looked into, which is professional.',
          dropout: 'You are walking home from your third interview for a clerk\'s job that requires "no essence theory, thank you".',
          hunter: 'You are walking home from the Verge with a bloom-hare over your shoulder and a strong feeling that something followed you in.',
        }[g.origin];
        case 'tabooLine': return '"One more thing." Maud jerks her head at the door, where a Dawn Choir brother is very obviously not listening. "Your essence is one the Choir watches. Brother Anselm will want a chat. Be nice to him. He\'s the nice one."';
        case 'caspianCouncil': return g.flags.beat_caspian ? 'Caspian Varo, arm still bandaged from your spar, rolls his eyes. "House Varo built half the Verge road. The Hollow is ours. I\'ll prove it at the bottom, and this time there\'ll be no lucky shots."' : 'Caspian Varo lounges against a pillar. "House Varo built half the Verge road. The Hollow is ours by any reasonable measure, and I\'ll prove it at the bottom of it."';
        case 'contractName': return extra.contractName || 'The contract';
        case 'varoLine': return g.flags.ledger_ithric || g.flags.ledger_velvet ? 'You already know whose weatherwitch knew the Lampwick storm was coming.' : 'You may want to ask who in this city keeps a weatherwitch on retainer. One house does. Its colour is red.';
        case 'regentLine': return { lantern: 'The Lantern Society posts guards on Selenne\'s warehouses before anyone can empty them. Maud signs the order herself.', ithric: 'Lady Seraphine, from the Regency chair, declares House Selenne\'s assets forfeit. It is extremely legal and extremely fast.', selenne: 'You gave House Selenne the Hollow. Now the city takes it back. Nobody thanks you for the irony.', varo: 'House Varo, guilty enough itself, keeps very quiet. Caspian does not. He reads the letters aloud a second time, louder.', magisterium: 'The Magisterium seals the Glasswater Hollow \'pending study\', which is the most aggressive thing it has ever done.' }[g.ending] || 'The Council meets the next morning with one fewer chair.';
        case 'regentHand': return { lantern: 'You take the letters to Maud instead of the Council. The Society has never once trusted a Councillor and isn\'t starting now. By morning Petra has fled the city, and House Selenne\'s ships have Lantern guards on every gangplank.', ithric: 'Lady Seraphine reads the letters in the Regency chair you helped put her in. She doesn\'t smile. "Thank you. This will be done properly." It is: a trial, a verdict, Petra\'s seat stripped. Seraphine is now the most powerful person in Calderhal, and she remembers who made her so.', selenne: 'You gave House Selenne the Glasswater rights. Now you hand its head\'s confession to the clerks of the Regency. Petra is gone before the warrant is signed. The Hollow is confiscated. Selenne will never forgive you, which seems fair.', varo: 'Caspian takes the letters to his father and to the Council in the same afternoon. House Varo confesses its part in Lampwick alongside Selenne\'s, and the city doesn\'t know whether to cheer or riot. Caspian stands on the steps through all of it.', magisterium: 'Hale delivers the letters to the Council with a forty-page annotated appendix. Petra flees before anyone finishes reading it.' }[g.ending] || 'The Council takes the letters. Petra is gone by morning.';
        case 'ottoLine': return g.flags.petra_bribed ? 'Ottoline doesn\'t look at you when you come into the Exchange anymore. She serves you, politely, and counts your change twice.' : '"Petra," says Ottoline, the next day, when you come into the Exchange. She takes off her monocle. "I worked for that woman for nineteen years. I appraised the cores from Lampwick for her." She is quiet a while. "Every core you sell me from now on, I pay a fair price. A real one. I should have been doing that for years."';
        case 'companionName': return g.party ? W.ALLIES[g.party].name.split(' ').slice(-1)[0] : 'Jory, who came in out of the rain and refuses to leave';
        case 'act2MaudLine': return g.flags.petra_bribed ? "\"Silver. The Cloister. And Petra Selenne still smiling,\" Maud says. Her eyes stay on yours. \"I do not know what happened on those steps, and tonight I am not asking.\" She lifts her glass, but does not toast." : g.flags.petra_exposed ? "\"Silver. The Cloister. And a councillor in chains,\" Maud says. \"From a nobody who got lucky in a bakery crater.\" She raises her glass. \"Never stop getting lucky.\"" : g.flags.petra_fled ? "\"Silver. The Cloister. And a councillor on the run,\" Maud says. \"From a nobody who got lucky in a bakery crater.\" She raises her glass. \"Never stop getting lucky.\"" : "\"Silver. The Cloister. And the Council forced to answer for Lampwick,\" Maud says. \"From a nobody who got lucky in a bakery crater.\" She raises her glass. \"Never stop getting lucky.\"";
        case 'lastChange': return (g.lastChange && g.lastChange.text) || '';
        default: return m;
      }
    });
  };

  // ---------- contracts ----------
  G_.contractsOffered = function (g) {
    const list = ['c_wolves', 'c_moths', 'c_mill', 'c_cass'];
    if (g.flags.slice_done && g.rank >= 1) list.push('c_wraiths', 'c_gulls', 'c_tide');
    return list.filter((id) => !g.quests[id]);
  };
  G_.contractsToReport = (g) => Object.keys(g.quests).filter((id) => W.QUESTS[id].contract && !g.quests[id].done && g.quests[id].stage === 1);
  G_.payContract = function (g, id) {
    const r = W.QUESTS[id].reward;
    const out = G_.apply(g, { marks: r.marks, cores: r.cores, bcores: r.bcores, standing: r.standing });
    if (r.essence === 'choice' && !g.flags.stipend_taken) { g.flags.stipend_taken = true; out.essenceChoice = G_.stipendChoices(g); }
    g.quests[id].stage = 2; G_.finishQuest(g, id);
    if (G_.questActive(g, 'main_lantern')) { G_.setStage(g, 'main_lantern', 3); G_.finishQuest(g, 'main_lantern'); G_.startQuest(g, 'main_glass'); G_.startQuest(g, 'main_bronze'); }
    return out;
  };

  // ---------- encounters ----------
  G_.encounterFor = function (g, node) {
    const N = W.MAP[node];
    const rng = G_.rng(g, (g.explored[node] || 0) * 13 + g.stats.fights);
    g.explored[node] = (g.explored[node] || 0) + 1;
    // contract & quest elites
    const q = (id) => G_.questActive(g, id) && g.quests[id].stage === 0;
    if (node === 'road' && q('c_cass') && rng() < 0.5) return { enc: 'redcass' };
    if (node === 'mill' && q('c_mill') && (g.flags.mill1_won || rng() < 0.3)) return { enc: 'mill2' };
    if (node === 'ash' && q('c_wolves') && rng() < 0.6) return { enc: rng() < 0.4 ? 'ash3' : 'ash1' };
    if (node === 'ash' && q('c_moths') && rng() < 0.5) return { enc: 'ash2' };
    if (node === 'wrecks' && q('c_tide') && rng() < 0.5) return { enc: 'tidecaller' };
    if (node === 'flats' && q('c_gulls') && rng() < 0.4) return { enc: 'gulls' };
    // events (each once)
    const evs = (N.events || []).filter((e) => !g.flags['done_' + e]);
    if (evs.length && rng() < 0.35) { const e = evs[Math.floor(rng() * evs.length)]; return { event: e }; }
    if (N.pool && rng() < 0.85) return { enc: N.pool[Math.floor(rng() * N.pool.length)] };
    return { find: true };
  };

  G_.playerLevel = (g) => 1 + Math.max(0, g.rank) * 2 + Math.floor(G_.abilityIds(g).length / 4);

  // Build a battle. hooks: {onEvent}
  G_.makeBattle = function (g, encId, opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const E = W.ENCOUNTERS[encId];
    const b = EB.B.create({ seed: (g.seed + g.stats.fights * 31 + g.day) >>> 0, env: { intro: E.intro, noFlee: !!E.noFlee, bonds: Object.values(g.npc).filter((n) => n.aff >= 20).length }, onEvent: opts.onEvent });
    const hero = G_.playerUnit(g, b);
    EB.B.addUnit(b, hero);
    const lvl = G_.playerLevel(g);
    E.foes.forEach((fid, i) => {
      if (fid === '@mirror') { EB.B.addUnit(b, G_.mirrorUnit(g, b)); return; }
      const foe = G_.enemyUnit(g, b, fid, i, lvl);
      EB.B.addUnit(b, foe);
    });
    if (g.party && W.ALLIES[g.party] && !E.duel && !E.trial && !E.tutorial) EB.B.addUnit(b, G_.allyUnit(g, b, g.party));
    b.encId = encId; b.enc = E;
    b.playerId = hero.id;
    EB.B.start(b);
    // stances for familiars
    for (const u of b.units) if (u.kind === 'familiar' && u.owner === hero.id) {
      const f = g.familiars[u.template] || (g.familiars[u.template] = { stance: 'aggressive' });
      u.stance = f.stance;
    }
    G_.enforceCommandCap(g, b, hero);
    if (g.flags.scoutAmbush) {
      g.flags.scoutAmbush = false;
      hero.t = 0; for (const u of b.units) if (u.side === 'enemy') { u.t += 40; K.applyStatus(b, null, u, 'exposed', 1, { dur: 1, force: true }); }
      K.say(b, 'You strike from cover. They\'re still turning around.', 'good');
    }
    if (encId === 'cinder' && g.flags.cinder_ambush) {
      g.flags.cinder_ambush = false;
      const c = b.units.find((u) => u.fid === 'brother_cinder');
      if (c) { c.hp = Math.round(c.hp * 0.75); K.applyStatus(b, null, c, 'stunned', 1, { dur: 1, force: true }); K.say(b, 'You come in through the lamp room window. Brother Cinder never saw you climbing.', 'good'); }
    }
    if (encId === 'prior') {
      const p = b.units.find((u) => u.fid === 'prior');
      if (p && g.flags.prior_weakness) { p.hp = Math.round(p.hp * 0.8); K.say(b, 'You go for the ember at his throat first. It cracks. He staggers.', 'good'); }
      if (p && g.flags.prior_doubt) { p.flags.doubt = true; K.say(b, 'The Prior fights like a man who isn\'t sure anymore.', 'good'); }
    }
    return b;
  };
  G_.enforceCommandCap = function (g, b, hero) {
    let cap = G_.commandCap(g);
    for (const u of b.units.filter((x) => x.kind === 'familiar' && x.owner === hero.id)) {
      const up = Math.max(1, u.upkeep || 1);
      if (cap >= up) cap -= up; else { u.alive = false; K.say(b, `${u.name} stays behind: you can't command any more familiars (Spirit raises your command capacity).`, 'dim'); }
    }
  };

  G_.allyUnit = function (g, b, id) {
    const T = W.ALLIES[id];
    const lvl = G_.playerLevel(g);
    const sc = 1 + Math.max(0, lvl - 6) * 0.05;
    const rank = Math.max(1, g.rank);
    const u = K.makeUnit(b, { name: T.name, side: 'ally', kind: 'hero', row: T.row, rank, attrs: { might: Math.round(T.a[0] * sc), swiftness: Math.round(T.a[1] * sc), spirit: Math.round(T.a[2] * sc), resilience: Math.round(T.a[3] * sc) }, abilities: T.ab.filter((x) => C().abilities[x]).map((x) => ({ id: x, rank })), tags: T.tags, hpScale: T.hp, powerScale: 0.85 });
    u.companion = id;
    return u;
  };
  G_.playerUnit = function (g, b) {
    const a = G_.attrs(g);
    const abilities = G_.abilityIds(g).map((id) => ({ id, rank: G_.abilityState(g, id).rank }));
    const u = K.makeUnit(b, {
      id: 'hero', name: g.name, side: 'ally', kind: 'hero', isPlayer: true, rank: Math.max(0, g.rank), row: g.row || 'vanguard',
      attrs: a, abilities, essences: G_.allEssences(g).map((e) => e.id), tags: ['human'],
      maxHp: G_.maxHp(g), maxMana: G_.maxMana(g), maxSta: G_.maxSta(g), powerScale: G_.TUNE.heroPow,
    });
    for (const slot of u.abilities) {
      const p = G_.seasonPenalty(g, slot.id);
      if (p.u > 0) { slot.def.powerMult *= p.power; slot.def.costMult = (slot.def.costMult || 1) * p.cost; slot.misfire = p.misfire; slot.unseasoned = Math.round(p.u * 100); }
    }
    if (g.hp == null) G_.fullHeal(g);
    u.hp = Math.max(1, Math.min(g.hp, K.maxHp(u))); u.mana = Math.min(g.mana, u.baseMana); u.sta = Math.min(g.sta, u.baseSta);
    // gear
    const wpn = W.ITEMS[g.gear.weapon];
    if (wpn) {
      const m = Object.assign({}, wpn.mods || {});
      if (wpn.wtype) m.weapon_damage_type = wpn.wtype;
      if (wpn.tempo) m.tempo = wpn.tempo;
      u.item = { id: g.gear.weapon, name: wpn.name, mods: m, onHit: wpn.onHit || [] };
    }
    for (const t of g.traits || []) { const T = W.TRAITS[t]; if (T && T.mods) for (const k in T.mods) if (k !== 'max_health') K.addMod(b, u, k, T.mods[k], 'combat', null, { tag: 'gear' }); }
    if (G_.pathCombatMods) { const pm = G_.pathCombatMods(g); for (const k in pm) if (k !== 'max_health') K.addMod(b, u, k, pm[k], 'combat', null, { tag: 'gear' }); }
    for (const slot of ['armor', 'charm']) {
      const it = W.ITEMS[g.gear[slot]];
      if (it && it.mods) for (const k in it.mods) K.addMod(b, u, k, it.mods[k], 'combat', null, { tag: 'gear' });
    }
    if (wpn && wpn.mods) for (const k of ['spell_power', 'crit', 'accuracy']) if (wpn.mods[k]) K.addMod(b, u, k, wpn.mods[k], 'combat', null, { tag: 'gear' });
    return u;
  };

  G_.enemyUnit = function (g, b, fid, i, lvl) {
    const T = W.ENEMIES[fid];
    const scale = 1 + Math.max(0, lvl - T.lv) * 0.06 + (g.rank >= 1 ? 0.12 : 0);
    const [m, s, sp, r] = T.a.map((x) => Math.round(x * scale));
    const rank = T.lv >= 10 ? 2 : T.lv >= 4 || g.rank >= 1 && T.elite ? 1 : 0;
    const u = K.makeUnit(b, {
      name: T.name, side: 'enemy', row: T.row || (i < 2 ? 'vanguard' : 'rear'), kind: 'enemy', rank,
      attrs: { might: m, swiftness: s, spirit: sp, resilience: r }, abilities: T.ab.filter((x) => C().abilities[x]).map((id) => ({ id, rank })),
      tags: T.tags, hpScale: T.hp * (T.boss ? G_.TUNE.bossHp : T.elite ? G_.TUNE.eliteHp : G_.TUNE.enemyHp) * (T.lv >= 6 && !T.boss ? 1.3 : 1), powerScale: (T.boss ? G_.TUNE.bossPow : T.elite ? G_.TUNE.elitePow : G_.TUNE.enemyPow) * (T.lv >= 6 && !T.boss ? 1.25 : 1) * (T.pow || 1), boss: !!T.boss, flying: !!T.flying, essences: T.essences || [], level: T.lv,
    });
    u.fid = fid; u.elite = !!T.elite;
    if (T.phases) G_.installPhases(b, u, T.phases, lvl);
    return u;
  };
  G_.installPhases = function (b, u, phases, lvl) {
    u.phaseIdx = 0;
    K.addHook(b, u, { trigger: 'when_take_damage', caster: u.id, dur: 'combat', fn: (bb, me) => {
      while (me.alive && me.phaseIdx < phases.length && K.hpPct(me) <= phases[me.phaseIdx].at) {
        const p = phases[me.phaseIdx++];
        K.say(bb, p.say, 'sys');
        K.log(bb, { k: 'phase', tgt: me.id, n: me.phaseIdx });
        for (const fid of p.summon || []) { const add = G_.enemyUnit({ rank: 0 }, bb, fid, 2, lvl); add.row = 'rear'; add.flags.summoned = true; EB.B.addUnit(bb, add); add.t = bb.time + 20; EB.AI.plan(bb, add); }
        for (const id of p.add || []) if (C().abilities[id]) K.learn(me, id, me.rank);
        for (const bf of p.buff || []) K.addMod(bb, me, bf.stat, bf.amount * (me.flags.doubt ? 0.3 : 1), 'combat', me);
        X_cleanseAll(bb, me);
      }
    } });
  };
  function X_cleanseAll(b, u) { for (const id of K.afflictions(u)) if (EB.FX[id] && EB.FX[id].control) K.removeStatus(b, u, id); }

  // The Mirror Trial: you, as your essence understands you.
  G_.mirrorUnit = function (g, b) {
    const a = G_.attrs(g);
    const u = K.makeUnit(b, {
      name: 'Mirror ' + g.name, side: 'enemy', row: 'vanguard', kind: 'enemy', rank: Math.max(0, g.rank),
      attrs: { might: a.might, swiftness: a.swiftness, spirit: a.spirit, resilience: a.resilience },
      abilities: G_.abilityIds(g).map((id) => ({ id, rank: Math.max(0, g.rank) })), essences: G_.allEssences(g).map((e) => e.id), tags: ['construct'], hpScale: 0.9, powerScale: 0.8,
    });
    u.flags.noAutoFamiliar = false; u.fid = 'mirror';
    return u;
  };

  // After a battle: write back state, award loot and training. Returns summary.
  G_.resolveBattle = function (g, b) {
    EB.B.finish(b);
    const hero = b.units.find((u) => u.id === b.playerId) || b.units.find((u) => u.isPlayer);
    const E = b.enc || {};
    const sum = { win: b.winner === 'ally', fled: b.winner === 'fled', loot: [], training: [], notes: [] };
    g.stats.fights++;
    // training from use
    const uses = {};
    for (const e of b.log) if (e.k === 'use' && e.src === hero.id && e.ab && C().abilities[e.ab]) uses[e.ab] = (uses[e.ab] || 0) + 1;
    const foes = b.units.filter((u) => u.side === 'enemy' && u.fid);
    const worth = foes.length ? Math.max(...foes.map((f) => W.ENEMIES[f.fid] ? W.ENEMIES[f.fid].lv : G_.playerLevel(g))) : 1;
    const lvl = G_.playerLevel(g);
    const factor = worth >= lvl - 1 ? 1 : worth >= lvl - 2 ? 0.5 : 0.2;
    if (sum.win || sum.fled === false) {
      for (const id in uses) {
        const n = uses[id];
        const amt = Math.round((Math.min(n, 2) * 9 + Math.max(0, n - 2) * 4) * factor * (sum.win ? 1 : 0.5));
        const got = G_.train(g, id, amt);
        const sea = G_.season(g, id, Math.round((Math.min(n, 3) * 8) * Math.max(factor, 0.5)));
        if (got > 0 || sea > 0) sum.training.push({ id, name: C().abilities[id].name, got, now: G_.abilityState(g, id).train, seasoned: sea, left: G_.unseasoned(g, id) });
      }
      // passives and familiars train a little just by being there
      for (const id of G_.abilityIds(g)) {
        const A = C().abilities[id];
        if ((A.passive || A.type === 'familiar' || A.toggle && !uses[id]) && !uses[id]) { const got = G_.train(g, id, Math.round(6 * factor)); const sea = G_.season(g, id, 6); if (got > 0 || sea > 0) sum.training.push({ id, name: A.name, got, now: G_.abilityState(g, id).train, seasoned: sea, left: G_.unseasoned(g, id) }); }
      }
    }
    if (sum.win) {
      g.stats.wins++;
      const rng = G_.rng(g, g.stats.fights * 17);
      let cores = 0, bcores = 0, marks = 0;
      for (const f of foes) {
        const T = W.ENEMIES[f.fid]; if (!T || T.noLoot) continue;
        cores += T.core || 0; bcores += T.bcore || 0; if (T.bcoreChance && rng() < T.bcoreChance) bcores++; marks += T.marks ? Math.round(T.marks * (0.7 + rng() * 0.6)) : 0;
        if (T.elite && rng() < 0.5) { const k = G_.addStone(g, 'random'); sum.loot.push(G_.stoneDef(k).name); }
        else if (!T.elite && !T.boss && rng() < 0.08 + (b.lootBonus || 0)) { const k = G_.addStone(g, 'random'); sum.loot.push(G_.stoneDef(k).name); }
      }
      cores = Math.round(cores * (1 + (b.lootBonus || 0)));
      if (cores) { G_.give(g, 'core', cores); sum.loot.push(`${cores} Iron Core${cores > 1 ? 's' : ''}`); }
      if (bcores) { G_.give(g, 'bcore', bcores); sum.loot.push(`${bcores} Bronze Core${bcores > 1 ? 's' : ''}`); }
      if (marks) { g.marks += marks; sum.loot.push(`${marks} marks`); }
      if (E.boss) {
        const pool = Object.values(C().essences).filter((e) => (e.rarity === 'uncommon' || e.rarity === 'rare') && !e.divine && !e.astral && !G_.allEssences(g).some((x) => x.id === e.id) && !g.essenceItems.includes(e.id));
        const pick = pool.find((e) => e.id === 'ess_glass' || e.id === 'ess_crystal') || pool[Math.floor(rng() * pool.length)];
        if (pick && !G_.allEssences(g).some((x) => x.id === 'ess_glass') && C().essences.ess_glass && !g.essenceItems.includes('ess_glass')) { g.essenceItems.push('ess_glass'); sum.loot.push('Glass essence'); }
        else if (pick) { g.essenceItems.push(pick.id); sum.loot.push(pick.name + ' essence'); }
        G_.give(g, 'charm_glass', 1); sum.loot.push('Glass Heart');
        const k = G_.addStone(g, 'random'); sum.loot.push(G_.stoneDef(k).name);
      }
      for (const f of foes) {
        const T = W.ENEMIES[f.fid]; if (!T || !T.dropEssence || f.alive) continue;
        if (g.flags['ess_drop_' + f.fid]) continue;
        const pool = T.dropEssence.filter((id) => C().essences[id] && !G_.allEssences(g).some((x) => x.id === id) && !g.essenceItems.includes(id) && !(g.burned || []).includes(id));
        if (pool.length) { const id = pool[Math.floor(rng() * pool.length)]; g.essenceItems.push(id); g.flags['ess_drop_' + f.fid] = true; sum.loot.push(G_.ess(id).name + ' essence'); }
      }
      if (E.elite && encGivesWeapon(b.encId)) { G_.give(g, 'wpn_glass', 1); sum.loot.push('Glasswater Blade'); }
      G_.questProgressOnWin(g, b.encId, sum);
    }
    // write back resources
    const alive = hero && hero.alive;
    if (!sum.win && !sum.fled) {
      sum.defeat = true;
      if (!E.duel && !E.trial && !E.tutorial && G_.wardSave && G_.wardSave(g, sum)) { sum.defeat = false; sum.fled = true; g.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round(G_.maxHp(g) * 0.1)); g.mana = G_.maxMana(g); g.sta = G_.maxSta(g); }
      else if (!E.duel && !E.trial && !E.tutorial && g.hardcore) { sum.permadeath = true; g.dead = { day: g.day, enc: E.name || b.encId, where: (W.MAP[g.node] || {}).name || 'Calderhal' }; }
      else if (!E.duel && !E.trial && !E.tutorial) {
        const lostMarks = Math.round(g.marks * 0.2), lostCores = Math.floor(G_.count(g, 'core') * 0.25);
        g.marks -= lostMarks; G_.give(g, 'core', -lostCores);
        sum.notes.push(`Lost ${lostMarks} marks and ${lostCores} cores.`);
        g.hp = Math.round(G_.maxHp(g) * 0.5); g.mana = G_.maxMana(g); g.sta = G_.maxSta(g);
        sum.wakeAtLodge = true;
      } else { g.hp = Math.max(1, Math.round(G_.maxHp(g) * 0.4)); }
    } else {
      g.hp = alive ? Math.max(1, Math.min(G_.maxHp(g), hero.hp)) : Math.round(G_.maxHp(g) * 0.3);
      g.mana = alive ? hero.mana : 0; g.sta = alive ? hero.sta : 0;
      if (E.tutorial || E.trial || E.duel) G_.fullHeal(g);
    }
    // suspicion: taboo abilities used near the city
    if (['caspian', 'trial'].includes(b.encId)) {
      const taboo = Object.keys(uses).some((id) => (G_.ess(C().abilities[id].essence) || {}).taboo > 0);
      if (taboo) { g.suspicion = Math.min(100, g.suspicion + 8); sum.notes.push('People saw you use a taboo essence. Suspicion +8.'); }
    }
    for (const id of Object.keys(b.socialFlags || {})) g.flags['social_' + id] = true;
    if (G_.afterBattle) G_.afterBattle(g, b, sum);
    return sum;
  };
  function encGivesWeapon(id) { return id === 'knight'; }

  G_.questProgressOnWin = function (g, enc, sum) {
    const done = (q) => { if (G_.questActive(g, q) && g.quests[q].stage === 0) { G_.setStage(g, q, 1); sum.notes.push(`${W.QUESTS[q].name}: return to Maud.`); } };
    if (enc === 'ash1' || enc === 'ash3') done('c_wolves');
    if (enc === 'ash2') done('c_moths');
    if (enc === 'mill1') g.flags.mill1_won = true;
    if (enc === 'mill2') done('c_mill');
    if (enc === 'redcass') done('c_cass');
    if (enc === 'matriarch') g.flags.matriarch_won = true;
    if (enc === 'salt1' || enc === 'salt2') done('c_wraiths');
    if (enc === 'gulls') done('c_gulls');
    if (enc === 'tidecaller') done('c_tide');
  };

  // ---------- shop ----------
  G_.shopStock = function (g, shop) {
    const rng = G_.rng(g, shop === 'black' ? 501 : 500);
    const keys = G_.stoneKeys();
    const pick = (pool, n) => { const out = []; const p = pool.slice(); while (out.length < n && p.length) out.push(p.splice(Math.floor(rng() * p.length), 1)[0]); return out; };
    if (shop === 'landing') return { items: ['draught', 'elixir', 'salts', 'tonic', 'wpn_tide', 'arm_saltplate'], stones: [], essences: [], priceMult: 0.9 };
    if (shop === 'black') {
      return {
        items: ['smoke', 'flask', 'salts', 'wpn_knife', 'charm_tooth'],
        stones: pick(keys.filter((k) => ['uncommon', 'rare'].includes(G_.stoneDef(k).rarity)), 5),
        essences: pick(Object.values(C().essences).filter((e) => e.taboo > 0 && e.rarity !== 'legendary').map((e) => e.id), 1),
        priceMult: 1.1,
      };
    }
    return {
      items: ['draught', 'tonic', 'salts', 'flask', 'wpn_sword', 'wpn_bow', 'arm_leathers', 'arm_coat', 'arm_mail', 'charm_ward'].concat(g.rank >= 1 ? ['elixir', 'bcore', 'wpn_cinder', 'arm_wardcoat'] : []),
      stones: pick(keys.filter((k) => G_.stoneDef(k).rarity !== 'legendary'), 8),
      essences: pick(Object.values(C().essences).filter((e) => (e.rarity === 'common' || e.rarity === 'uncommon') && !e.taboo).map((e) => e.id), 2),
      priceMult: g.flags.cheap_stones ? 0.85 : 1,
    };
  };
  G_.priceOf = function (g, kind, id, shop) {
    const st = G_.shopStock(g, shop);
    let p = kind === 'item' ? W.ITEMS[id].price : kind === 'stone' ? STONE_PRICE[G_.stoneDef(id).rarity] : ESS_PRICE[G_.ess(id).rarity];
    if (kind === 'stone' && shop !== 'black') p *= st.priceMult;
    if (kind !== 'item' && g.flags.petra_exposed && shop === 'exchange') p *= 0.9;
    if (shop === 'black') p *= st.priceMult;
    const sel = g.standing.selenne || 0;
    if (shop !== 'black') p *= 1 - Math.min(0.15, Math.max(0, sel) / 300);
    if (g.suspicion >= 60 && shop !== 'black') p *= 1.2;
    return Math.round(p);
  };
  G_.sellPrice = (g, id) => (g.flags.petra_exposed || g.flags.petra_fled || g.flags.petra_regent) && (id === 'core' || id === 'bcore') ? (id === 'bcore' ? 45 : 8) : id === 'bcore' ? 35 : Math.max(1, Math.round((W.ITEMS[id].price || 0) * (id === 'core' ? 1 : 0.4)));
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
// Faith, veneration and the unsworn road; the Lantern Society's ranks and board work.
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const W = EB.W, G_ = EB.G;
  const C = () => EB.content;

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- data
  W.FACTIONS.ferry = { name: 'The Ferry House', blurb: 'Morrow\'s priests. They bury the dead, collect the fare, and make sure nobody comes back.', color: '#8fa3b0' };
  W.FACTIONS.wheel = { name: 'The Gilded Wheel', blurb: 'Kesh\'s temple, which is also a gaming hall, which is also a bank.', color: '#e0b64a' };
  W.FACTIONS.horn = { name: 'Hornhall', blurb: 'Vask\'s lodge of hunters. Older than the Lantern Society and says so often.', color: '#7a9a5a' };
  W.FACTIONS.orrery = { name: 'The Unlit Orrery', blurb: 'People who venerate things that are not gods, and are not quite people.', color: '#9b8cd8' };

  // Points needed for each rank; ranks 2-4 also need an essence rank and a trial.
  W.PATH_PTS = [0, 20, 70, 160, 300];
  W.PATH_REQ_RANK = [null, null, 0, 1, 2];

  W.PATHS = {
    unsworn: {
      kind: 'unsworn', name: 'The Unsworn Road', short: 'Unsworn', color: '#cfc6b8', pointName: 'Resolve',
      ranks: ['Wayfarer', 'Self-Reliant', 'Unbowed', 'Free Blade', 'Sovereign of Self'],
      blurb: 'You answer to no god and no star. Your strength is your own, earned on the road and on the Society\'s board.',
      tenet: 'Earn it yourself. Resolve comes from winning fights, finishing Society board work and passing your own trials.',
      taboo: 'None. Nobody is watching you, which is the point.',
      gift: 'At Self-Reliant, the Society\'s quartermaster lets you choose one of three rare essences. Nobody can tell you what it will wake in you.',

      boon: { name: 'Second Wind', text: 'Once a day: fully recover, and deal +8% damage in your next 3 fights.' },
      trial: ['', '', 'Maud sets you against her old sparring partners in the yard, all at once.', 'The Lodge brings out the things it keeps in cages for exactly this.', 'You fight your own reflection in the Magisterium\'s mirror, with nobody watching but you.'],

      kind: 'god', name: 'Solenne, the Morning', short: 'Solenne', color: '#f2d27a', faction: 'choir', temple: 'chapel', templeName: 'Dawn Choir Chapel', essence: 'ess_dawn', pointName: 'Devotion',

      boon: { name: 'Morning Prayer', text: 'Once a day, wherever you are: fully heal and recover.' },
      trial: ['', '', 'Anselm leads you into the crypt under the chapel, where the Choir keeps things that refused to stay dead.', 'The Choir sends you into the dark to bring back a light. Something is guarding it.', 'Solenne\'s regard falls on you, and with it, the oldest shadow in the Choir\'s keeping.'],
    },
    morrow: {

      ranks: ['Mourner', 'Initiate of the Ferry', 'Lantern-Keeper', 'Boatman', 'Hand of the Ferryman'],

      boon: { name: 'Ferryman\'s Grace', text: 'Once a day: the next time you would die today, you are turned back at the door and wake at the edge of the fight.' },
      trial: ['', '', 'The Ferry House sends you to escort a body across the marsh. It does not want to go.', 'Something has been coming back across. Morrow wants it returned, permanently.', 'You take the oar yourself for one night. The far bank sends someone to argue.'],
      tabooEss: ['ess_death', 'ess_bone', 'ess_ghost'],
    },
    kesh: {
      kind: 'god', name: 'Kesh, the Wager', short: 'Kesh', color: '#e0b64a', faction: 'wheel', temple: 'wheel', templeName: 'The Gilded Wheel', essence: 'ess_wager', pointName: 'Favour',
      ranks: ['Punter', 'Initiate of the Wheel', 'Dealer', 'High Roller', 'Kesh\'s Own'],
      blurb: 'Goddess of luck, trade and nerve. Her temple is a gaming hall, and the priests are very good at cards.',
      tenet: 'Take the risk. Favour comes from critical hits and from winning fights you very nearly lost.',
      taboo: 'Losing. Kesh has no use for a loser: every defeat costs you favour.',
      gift: 'Her Initiates receive the Wager essence.',
      mods: [{}, { crit: 0.03, crit_damage: 0.05 }, { crit: 0.05, crit_damage: 0.1 }, { crit: 0.07, crit_damage: 0.15 }, { crit: 0.1, crit_damage: 0.2 }],
      boon: { name: 'Lucky Coin', text: 'Once a day: your next 3 fights pay 50% more marks and are likelier to drop stones.' },
      trial: ['', '', 'The Wheel stakes you against three of its best in a game where the cards cut.', 'A debt the Wheel is owed has gone bad. Collect it, against whoever is holding it.', 'Kesh herself deals one hand. You play it in blood.'],
    },
    vask: {
      kind: 'god', name: 'Vask, the Long Hunt', short: 'Vask', color: '#7a9a5a', faction: 'horn', temple: 'hornhall', templeName: 'Hornhall', essence: 'ess_quarry', pointName: 'Devotion',
      ranks: ['Beater', 'Initiate of the Hunt', 'Tracker', 'Huntsman', 'Master of the Long Hunt'],

      gift: 'His Initiates receive the Quarry essence.',
      mods: [{}, { accuracy: 0.04, damage: 0.03 }, { accuracy: 0.06, damage: 0.05 }, { accuracy: 0.08, damage: 0.07 }, { accuracy: 0.1, damage: 0.1 }],
      boon: { name: 'Scent of Prey', text: 'Once a day: for your next 3 fights you scout like a hunter and strike first.' },
      trial: ['', '', 'Hornhall releases a beast into the yard and gives you a head start. It does not need one.', 'You run the night roads with the Hunt. Something runs with you that isn\'t a hunter.', 'The Long Hunt rides, and tonight you are its quarry. Survive until dawn.'],
    },
    loom: {
      kind: 'astral', name: 'The Loom', short: 'The Loom', color: '#c0b0f0', faction: 'orrery', temple: 'orrery', templeName: 'The Unlit Orrery', essence: 'ess_thread', pointName: 'Attunement',
      ranks: ['Noticed', 'Threaded', 'Woven', 'Patterned', 'Shuttle'],
      blurb: 'A great astral being of fate and connection. It does not answer prayers. It notices people, sometimes, and they are never quite the same.',
