      boon: { name: 'Pull a Thread', text: 'Once a day: your next 3 fights train your abilities 50% faster.' },
      trial: ['', '', 'The Orrery\'s keepers turn the brass rings, and the Loom shows you a thread that should have been cut.', 'A knot in the pattern has a shape, and the shape has teeth.', 'The Loom shows you the thread of your own life. Something else is pulling it.'],
    },
    eye: {
      kind: 'astral', name: 'The Unclosing Eye', short: 'The Eye', color: '#9fd0e8', faction: 'orrery', temple: 'orrery', templeName: 'The Unlit Orrery', essence: 'ess_witness', pointName: 'Attunement',
      ranks: ['Observed', 'Seeing', 'Watcher', 'Unblinking', 'Witness'],
      blurb: 'A great astral being that observes everything and forgets nothing. It does not answer prayers. It watches, and the watched learn to watch back.',
      tenet: 'See everything. Attunement comes from exploring new ground, scouting fights before they start, and winning fights.',
      taboo: 'The Eye does not like to lose sight of you: hiding in battle costs attunement. Astral regard: the gods\' churches distrust you, and no god will take you while the Eye has you.',
      gift: 'Those it attunes receive the Witness essence.',
      mods: [{}, { crit: 0.03, accuracy: 0.05 }, { crit: 0.05, accuracy: 0.07 }, { crit: 0.07, accuracy: 0.1 }, { crit: 0.09, accuracy: 0.12 }],
      boon: { name: 'Foresight', text: 'Once a day: for your next 3 fights you see what\'s coming: +10% accuracy and +10% crit chance.' },
      trial: ['', '', 'The Orrery dims every lamp. In the dark, something you can\'t see is watching you back.', 'The Eye shows you a fight that hasn\'t happened yet. Then it happens.', 'You look into the Eye. It looks into you. Then it shows you everything at once.'],
    },
  };
  W.DIVINE_ESS = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(W.PATHS).filter(([, P]) => P.essence).map(([id, P]) => [P.essence, id]));

  // Trials: one encounter per path and tier. Trials are not lethal, even in hardcore.
  const TRIAL_FOES = { 2: ['alpha', 'wolf', 'lampmoth'], 3: ['knight', 'stalker', 'archer'], 4: ['@mirror', 'cinderknight'] };
  for (const [pid, P] of Object.entries(W.PATHS)) for (const t of [2, 3, 4]) {
    W.ENCOUNTERS[`ptrial_${pid}_${t}`] = { name: `Trial: ${P.ranks[t]}`, foes: TRIAL_FOES[t], intro: P.trial[t], noFlee: true, trial: true, elite: true };
  }

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Lantern Society ranks
  W.SOCIETY = [
    { name: 'Recruit', pay: 1, discount: 0, contracts: 0, rank: null, text: 'Your name is on the roll. Maud remembers it about half the time.' },
    { name: 'Iron Adventurer', pay: 1.1, discount: 0.05, contracts: 3, rank: 0, text: 'An iron badge. Board work pays 10% more, and the Exchange gives Society members 5% off goods.' },
    { name: 'Bronze Adventurer', pay: 1.2, discount: 0.1, contracts: 8, rank: 1, assess: 'assess_bronze', text: 'A bronze badge, a seat near the fire, and harder work on the board. Pay +20%, Exchange goods 10% off.' },
    { name: 'Silver Adventurer', pay: 1.3, discount: 0.15, contracts: 15, rank: 2, assess: 'assess_silver', text: 'Silver. People stand up when you come in. Pay +30%, Exchange goods 15% off, and the Society\'s hardest bounties.' },
  ];
  W.ENCOUNTERS.assess_bronze = { name: 'Bronze Assessment', foes: ['knight', 'archer', 'bandit'], intro: 'Maud folds her arms at the edge of the yard. "Assessment. Three of ours, no holding back. Impress me."', noFlee: true, trial: true, elite: true };
  W.ENCOUNTERS.assess_silver = { name: 'Silver Assessment', foes: ['tidecaller', 'wrecker', 'stormgull'], intro: 'The examiners came down from the capital for this. They don\'t smile. Neither do the things they brought.', noFlee: true, trial: true, elite: true };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- state
  G_.path = function (g) {
    if (!g.path) g.path = { id: 'unsworn', pts: 0, rank: 0, trials: {}, renounced: {} };
    return g.path;
  };
  G_.pathDef = (g) => W.PATHS[G_.path(g).id];
  G_.pathRankName = (g) => G_.pathDef(g).ranks[G_.path(g).rank];
  G_.pathBlocked = function (g) {
    const p = G_.path(g), P = W.PATHS[p.id];
    if (p.id === 'solenne' && G_.hasTaboo(g)) return 'You carry a taboo essence. Solenne will not hear you.';
    if (P.tabooEss && G_.allEssences(g).some((e) => P.tabooEss.includes(e.id))) return 'You carry an essence that cheats the ferry. Morrow will not hear you.';
    return null;
  };
  G_.pathMod = function (g, k) { const p = G_.path(g); const m = W.PATHS[p.id].mods[p.rank] || {}; return m[k] || 0; };
  G_.pathCombatMods = function (g) {
    const p = G_.path(g); const out = Object.assign({}, W.PATHS[p.id].mods[p.rank] || {});
    const bo = p.boon;
    if (bo && bo.n > 0) {
      if (bo.k === 'sight') { out.accuracy = (out.accuracy || 0) + 0.1; out.crit = (out.crit || 0) + 0.1; }
      if (bo.k === 'grit') out.damage = (out.damage || 0) + 0.08;
    }
    return out;
  };
  G_.addPoints = function (g, n, why) {
    const p = G_.path(g);
    if (n > 0 && G_.pathBlocked(g)) return 0;
    const before = p.pts; p.pts = Math.max(0, p.pts + n);
    return p.pts - before;
  };
  // What the next rank needs.
  G_.pathNext = function (g) {
    const p = G_.path(g); const r = p.rank + 1;
    if (r > 4) return null;
    const need = W.PATH_PTS[r], reqRank = W.PATH_REQ_RANK[r];
    const out = { rank: r, name: W.PATHS[p.id].ranks[r], need, pts: p.pts, reqRank, trial: r >= 2, trialDone: !!p.trials[r] };
    out.ptsOk = p.pts >= need; out.rankOk = reqRank == null || g.rank >= reqRank;
    out.blocked = G_.pathBlocked(g);
    out.trialReady = out.trial && !out.trialDone && out.ptsOk && out.rankOk && !out.blocked;
    out.ready = out.ptsOk && out.rankOk && (!out.trial || out.trialDone) && !out.blocked;
    return out;
  };
  G_.pathRankUp = function (g) {
    const nx = G_.pathNext(g); if (!nx || !nx.ready) return null;
    const p = G_.path(g), P = W.PATHS[p.id];
    p.rank = nx.rank;
    const res = { rank: p.rank, name: P.ranks[p.rank], mods: P.mods[p.rank] };
    if (p.rank === 1) {
      if (P.essence) {
        const has = G_.allEssences(g).some((e) => e.id === P.essence) || g.essenceItems.includes(P.essence);
        if (!has) { g.essenceItems.push(P.essence); res.essence = P.essence; }
      } else if (!g.flags.unsworn_gift) { g.flags.unsworn_gift = true; res.choice = G_.unswornChoices(g); }
    }
    G_.note(g, `${P.short}: raised to ${res.name}.`);
    return res;
  };
  G_.unswornChoices = function (g) {
    const rng = G_.rng(g, 717);
    const pool = Object.values(C().essences).filter((e) => (e.rarity === 'rare' || e.rarity === 'uncommon') && !e.taboo && !e.divine && !e.astral && !G_.allEssences(g).some((x) => x.id === e.id) && !g.essenceItems.includes(e.id) && !(g.burned || []).includes(e.id));
    const out = [];
    while (out.length < 3 && pool.length) out.push(pool.splice(Math.floor(rng() * pool.length), 1)[0].id);
    return out;
  };
  G_.canJoin = function (g, id) {
    const p = G_.path(g), P = W.PATHS[id];
    if (p.id === id) return { error: 'You already follow this path.' };
    if (p.renounced[id]) return { error: 'You turned away once. This door stays shut.' };
    if (p.id !== 'unsworn') return { error: `You already follow ${W.PATHS[p.id].short}. You would have to renounce first.` };
    if (P.kind === 'god' && g.flags.astral_marked) return { error: 'An astral being has noticed you. No god will take you now.' };
    return { ok: true };
  };
  G_.joinPath = function (g, id) {
    const c = G_.canJoin(g, id); if (c.error) return c;
    const p = G_.path(g), P = W.PATHS[id];
    const carried = Math.min(W.PATH_PTS[1] - 1, Math.floor(p.pts / 2));
    p.unswornSaved = { pts: p.pts, rank: p.rank };
    p.id = id; p.pts = carried; p.rank = 0; p.trials = {}; p.boon = null; p.boonDay = null;
    if (P.kind === 'astral') { g.flags.astral_marked = true; G_.addStanding(g, 'choir', -10); G_.addStanding(g, 'orrery', 15); }
    else G_.addStanding(g, P.faction, 15);
    G_.note(g, `You ${P.kind === 'astral' ? 'began to venerate' : 'swore yourself to'} ${P.name}.`);
    return { ok: true, carried };
  };
  G_.renounce = function (g) {
    const p = G_.path(g); if (p.id === 'unsworn') return { error: 'There is nothing to renounce.' };
    const P = W.PATHS[p.id];
    p.renounced[p.id] = true; const hadGift = p.rank >= 1;
    if (P.faction) G_.addStanding(g, P.faction, -25);
    const back = p.unswornSaved || { pts: 0, rank: 0 };
    const pts = back.pts + Math.floor(p.pts / 2);
    p.id = 'unsworn'; p.pts = pts; p.rank = Math.min(back.rank, 4); p.trials = {}; p.boon = null; p.boonDay = null;
    // A god's gift already took an essence slot: don't also hand out the unsworn gift.
    if (hadGift) { g.flags.unsworn_gift = true; while (p.rank < 1 && p.pts >= W.PATH_PTS[1]) p.rank++; }
    G_.note(g, `You renounced ${P.name}.`);
    return { ok: true, name: P.name };
  };
  // Offerings (gods & astral) or sparring (unsworn): 3 per day.
  G_.offerLeft = (g) => { const p = G_.path(g); if (p.offerDay !== g.day) { p.offerDay = g.day; p.offers = 0; } return 3 - p.offers; };
  G_.offer = function (g, kind) {
    if (G_.offerLeft(g) <= 0) return { error: 'Enough for one day.' };
    const p = G_.path(g);
    if (kind === 'core') { if (!G_.count(g, 'core')) return { error: 'You have no Iron Cores.' }; G_.give(g, 'core', -1); }
    else { if (g.marks < 25) return { error: 'You need 25 marks.' }; g.marks -= 25; }
    p.offers++;
    const got = G_.addPoints(g, kind === 'core' ? 5 : 2);
    return { got };
  };
  // Daily boon.
  G_.boonReady = (g) => { const p = G_.path(g); return p.rank >= 1 && p.boonDay !== g.day; };
  G_.useBoon = function (g) {
    if (!G_.boonReady(g)) return { error: 'Not again today.' };
    const p = G_.path(g); p.boonDay = g.day;
    const P = W.PATHS[p.id];
    switch (p.id) {
      case 'solenne': G_.fullHeal(g); break;
      case 'morrow': p.ward = g.day; break;
      case 'kesh': p.boon = { k: 'luck', n: 3 }; break;
      case 'vask': p.boon = { k: 'scent', n: 3 }; break;
      case 'loom': p.boon = { k: 'thread', n: 3 }; break;
      case 'eye': p.boon = { k: 'sight', n: 3 }; break;
      default: G_.fullHeal(g); p.boon = { k: 'grit', n: 3 }; break;
    }
    G_.note(g, `${P.boon.name}.`);
    return { ok: true, name: P.boon.name, text: P.boon.text };
  };
  G_.wardSave = function (g, sum) {
    const p = G_.path(g);
    if (p.id === 'morrow' && p.ward === g.day) { p.ward = null; sum.notes.push('The door opened, and Morrow turned you back. You wake at the edge of the fight, barely alive.'); sum.warded = true; return true; }
    return false;
  };

  // Wrap utilities for Vask's scent, battles for Kesh's luck and scent ambush.
  const baseUtil = G_.utilities;
  G_.utilities = function (g) {
    const out = baseUtil(g);
    const bo = g.path && g.path.boon;
    if (bo && bo.k === 'scent' && bo.n > 0) (out.scout = out.scout || []).push('Scent of Prey');
    return out;
  };
  const baseMake = G_.makeBattle;
  G_.makeBattle = function (g, encId, opts) {
    const bo = g.path && g.path.boon; const E = W.ENCOUNTERS[encId] || {};
    if (bo && bo.n > 0 && bo.k === 'scent' && !E.trial && !E.duel && !E.tutorial && !E.boss) g.flags.scoutAmbush = true;
    const amb = !!g.flags.scoutAmbush;
    const b = baseMake(g, encId, opts);
    b.scouted = amb;
    if (bo && bo.n > 0 && bo.k === 'luck') b.lootBonus = (b.lootBonus || 0) + 0.5;
    return b;
  };
  const basePrice = G_.priceOf;
  G_.priceOf = function (g, kind, id, shop) {
    let p = basePrice(g, kind, id, shop);
    if (kind === 'item' && shop !== 'black') p = Math.max(1, Math.round(p * (1 - G_.societyDef(g).discount)));
    return p;
  };
  const basePay = G_.payContract;
  G_.payContract = function (g, id) {
    const r = W.QUESTS[id].reward; const extra = Math.round((r.marks || 0) * (G_.societyDef(g).pay - 1));
    const out = basePay(g, id);
    if (extra > 0) { g.marks += extra; out.notes.push({ m: `Society rank bonus: +${extra} marks`, cls: 'good' }); }
    const pid = G_.path(g).id; const bonus = pid === 'loom' ? 6 : pid === 'unsworn' ? 4 : 0;
    const got = bonus ? G_.addPoints(g, bonus) : 0;
    if (got) out.notes.push({ m: `${W.PATHS[pid].pointName} +${got}`, cls: 'good' });
    return out;
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- after every battle
  G_.afterBattle = function (g, b, sum) {
    const p = G_.path(g); const pid = p.id;
    const hero = b.units.find((u) => u.isPlayer);
    const E = b.enc || {};
    const foes = b.units.filter((u) => u.side === 'enemy' && u.fid);
    const myKills = b.log.filter((e) => e.k === 'death' && e.src === (hero && hero.id) && foes.some((f) => f.id === e.tgt));
    const killed = (tag) => myKills.filter((e) => { const f = foes.find((x) => x.id === e.tgt); return f && f.tags && f.tags.has(tag); }).length;
    let pts = 0; const why = [];
    const isTrial = E.trial || E.duel || E.tutorial;
    if (sum.win && !isTrial) {
      pts += foes.some((f) => f.boss) ? 8 : foes.some((f) => f.elite) ? 4 : 2;
      switch (pid) {
        case 'solenne': {
          let healed = 0; for (const e of b.log) if (e.k === 'heal' && e.src === hero.id) healed += e.amt;
          const hp = K().maxHp(hero) || 100; const t = Math.min(4, Math.floor(healed / (hp * 0.15)));
          if (t) { pts += t; why.push('healing'); }
          if (foes.some((f) => f.tags.has('corrupted') || f.tags.has('undead'))) { pts += 2; why.push('the tainted'); }
          break;
        }
        case 'morrow': { const k = Math.min(4, myKills.length) + 2 * killed('undead'); if (k) { pts += k; why.push('the dead ferried'); } break; }
        case 'kesh': {
          const crits = b.log.filter((e) => e.k === 'dmg' && e.src === hero.id && e.crit).length; const c = Math.min(3, Math.floor(crits / 2));
          if (c) { pts += c; why.push('crits'); }
          if (hero && hero.alive && hero.hp < K().maxHp(hero) * 0.3) { pts += 3; why.push('a close call'); }
          break;
        }
        case 'vask': { const k = Math.min(3, killed('beast')) + (foes.some((f) => f.elite) ? 3 : 0); if (k) { pts += k; why.push('the hunt'); } break; }
        case 'loom': if (g.party) { pts += 2; why.push('fighting beside a friend'); } break;
        case 'eye': if (b.scouted) { pts += 2; why.push('seeing it first'); } break;
        default: pts += 1; break;
      }
    }
    if (pid === 'kesh' && sum.defeat && !isTrial) { G_.addPoints(g, -6); sum.notes.push('Kesh has no use for a loser. Favour -6.'); }
    if (pid === 'vask' && sum.fled && !sum.warded) { G_.addPoints(g, -5); sum.notes.push('A hunter that runs is no hunter. Devotion -5.'); }
    if (pid === 'eye' && b.log.some((e) => e.k === 'status' && e.tgt === (hero && hero.id) && e.id === 'stealthed')) { G_.addPoints(g, -2); sum.notes.push('You hid from the Eye. Attunement -2.'); }
    if (pts) {
      const got = G_.addPoints(g, pts);
      if (got) sum.notes.push(`${W.PATHS[pid].pointName} +${got}${why.length ? ' (' + why.join(', ') + ')' : ''}.`);
      else if (G_.pathBlocked(g)) sum.notes.push(G_.pathBlocked(g));
    }
    // trials and assessments
    if (sum.win && /^ptrial_/.test(b.encId || '')) { const t = +b.encId.split('_').pop(); p.trials[t] = true; sum.notes.push('Trial passed. Return to be raised.'); }
    if (sum.win && /^assess_/.test(b.encId || '')) { const s = G_.society(g); s.assessed = s.assessed || {}; s.assessed[b.encId] = true; sum.notes.push('Assessment passed. Maud has a badge for you.'); }
    // daily boon charges
    const bo = p.boon;
    if (bo && bo.n > 0 && !isTrial) {
      if (bo.k === 'luck' && sum.win) { const m = Math.round(sum.loot.reduce((a, l) => { const x = /^(\d+) marks$/.exec(l); return a + (x ? +x[1] : 0); }, 0) * 0.5); if (m) { g.marks += m; sum.loot.push(`${m} marks (Lucky Coin)`); } }
      if (bo.k === 'thread') for (const t of sum.training) { const add = Math.round((t.got || 0) * 0.5); if (add > 0) { const got = G_.train(g, t.id, add); if (got) { t.got += got; t.now = G_.abilityState(g, t.id).train; } } }
      bo.n--; if (bo.n <= 0) p.boon = null;
    }
    // Society board work
    if (sum.win && !isTrial) for (const t of G_.society(g).active) if (t.node === g.node && t.have < t.need) { t.have++; sum.notes.push(t.have >= t.need ? `Board work done: ${t.title}. Report to the Lodge.` : `${t.title}: ${t.have}/${t.need}.`); }
    if (sum.win && !isTrial && p.duty && p.duty.day === g.day && p.duty.node === g.node && p.duty.have < p.duty.need) { p.duty.have++; if (p.duty.have >= p.duty.need) sum.notes.push(`Temple duty done: ${p.duty.title}. Return to ${W.PATHS[pid].templeName}.`); }
  };
  const K = () => EB.K;

  // Eye: new ground explored is noticed.
  const baseEnc = G_.encounterFor;
  G_.encounterFor = function (g, node) {
    const first = !(g.explored && g.explored[node]);
    const r = baseEnc(g, node);
    if (first && G_.path(g).id === 'eye') { const got = G_.addPoints(g, 3); if (got) r.attune = got; }
    return r;
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- temple duties (one a day)
  const DUTY_NODES = { solenne: ['ash', 'scar', 'marsh', 'mill'], morrow: ['mill', 'flats', 'wrecks'], kesh: ['road', 'wrecks'], vask: ['ash', 'flats', 'marsh'], loom: ['road', 'mill', 'flats'], eye: ['scar', 'marsh', 'wrecks', 'ash'] };
  const DUTY_TEXT = { solenne: 'Cleanse the taint at', morrow: 'Lay the restless to rest at', kesh: 'Collect a debt on behalf of the Wheel at', vask: 'Run down the beasts at', loom: 'Tie off a loose thread at', eye: 'Witness what happens at' };
  G_.unlockedPoolNodes = (g) => Object.entries(W.MAP).filter(([id, n]) => n.pool && ((n.region || 'verge') === 'verge' || g.flags.saltgrave_open)).map(([id]) => id);
  G_.templeDuty = function (g) {
    const p = G_.path(g); if (p.id === 'unsworn') return null;
    if (!p.duty || p.duty.day !== g.day) {
      const ok = G_.unlockedPoolNodes(g); const pref = DUTY_NODES[p.id].filter((n) => ok.includes(n));
      const pool = pref.length ? pref : ok; const node = pool[(g.day * 7 + p.pts) % pool.length];
      p.duty = { day: g.day, node, need: 2, have: 0, done: false, title: `${DUTY_TEXT[p.id]} ${W.MAP[node].name}` };
    }
    return p.duty;
  };
  G_.finishDuty = function (g) {
    const d = G_.templeDuty(g); if (!d || d.done || d.have < d.need) return null;
    d.done = true; const got = G_.addPoints(g, 8); const marks = 20 + 10 * Math.max(0, g.rank);
    g.marks += marks; const P = G_.pathDef(g); if (P.faction) G_.addStanding(g, P.faction, 3);
    return { got, marks };
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Society
  G_.society = function (g) {
    if (!g.society) g.society = { rank: 0, done: 0, active: [], board: null, assessed: {} };
    return g.society;
  };
  G_.societyDef = (g) => W.SOCIETY[G_.society(g).rank] || W.SOCIETY[0];
  G_.contractsDone = (g) => Object.keys(g.quests).filter((id) => W.QUESTS[id] && W.QUESTS[id].contract && g.quests[id].done).length + G_.society(g).done;
  G_.societyNext = function (g) {
    const s = G_.society(g); const r = s.rank + 1; const D = W.SOCIETY[r]; if (!D) return null;
    const out = { rank: r, name: D.name, contracts: D.contracts, have: G_.contractsDone(g), reqRank: D.rank, assess: D.assess };
    out.contractsOk = out.have >= D.contracts; out.rankOk = g.rank >= D.rank; out.assessDone = !D.assess || !!(s.assessed || {})[D.assess];
    out.assessReady = D.assess && !out.assessDone && out.contractsOk && out.rankOk;
    out.ready = out.contractsOk && out.rankOk && out.assessDone;
    return out;
  };
  G_.societyRankUp = function (g) { const nx = G_.societyNext(g); if (!nx || !nx.ready) return null; G_.society(g).rank = nx.rank; G_.addStanding(g, 'lantern', 5); G_.note(g, `Lantern Society: ${nx.name}.`); return nx; };
  const BOARD_TITLES = ['Thin out', 'Clear', 'Bounty:', 'Escort duty, then cull', 'Patrol and purge'];
  G_.boardOffers = function (g) {
    const s = G_.society(g);
    if (!s.board || s.board.day !== g.day) {
      const rng = G_.rng(g, 900 + g.day * 3); const nodes = G_.unlockedPoolNodes(g); const offers = [];
      for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) {
        const node = nodes[Math.floor(rng() * nodes.length)]; const N = W.MAP[node]; const need = 2 + Math.floor(rng() * 2);
        const base = 18 + 12 * (N.danger || 1) + 6 * need;
        offers.push({ id: `bw${g.day}_${i}`, node, need, have: 0, title: `${BOARD_TITLES[Math.floor(rng() * BOARD_TITLES.length)]} ${N.name}`, marks: base, cores: (N.danger || 1) >= 3 ? 2 : 1 });
      }
      s.board = { day: g.day, offers };
    }
    return s.board.offers.filter((o) => !s.active.some((a) => a.id === o.id) && !o.taken);
  };
  G_.takeBoard = function (g, id) {
    const s = G_.society(g); if (s.active.length >= 2) return { error: 'Two jobs at once is plenty. Finish one first.' };
    const o = (s.board && s.board.offers || []).find((x) => x.id === id); if (!o) return { error: 'Gone already.' };
    o.taken = true; s.active.push(Object.assign({}, o)); return { ok: true };
  };
  G_.reportBoard = function (g, id) {
    const s = G_.society(g); const i = s.active.findIndex((a) => a.id === id); if (i < 0) return null;
    const t = s.active[i]; if (t.have < t.need) return null;
    s.active.splice(i, 1); s.done++;
    const marks = Math.round(t.marks * G_.societyDef(g).pay);
    g.marks += marks; G_.give(g, 'core', t.cores); G_.addStanding(g, 'lantern', 2);
    const res = { marks, cores: t.cores };
    if (G_.path(g).id === 'unsworn') { const got = G_.addPoints(g, 4); if (got) res.resolve = got; }
    return res;
  };
  G_.dropBoard = function (g, id) { const s = G_.society(g); s.active = s.active.filter((a) => a.id !== id); };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = (EB.UI = { g: null, screen: null, params: {}, stack: [] });

  // ---------- DOM helper ----------
  // h('div.card#id', {onclick, style, ...}, children...)
  UI.h = function (sel, attrs, ...kids) {
    const m = /^([a-z0-9]+)?((?:[.#][\w-]+)*)$/i.exec(String(sel || 'div').trim().replace(/\s+/g, '.')) || [null, 'div', ''];
    const el = document.createElement(m[1] || 'div');
    (m[2] || '').replace(/([.#])([\w-]+)/g, (_, t, n) => { if (t === '.') el.classList.add(n); else el.id = n; });
    if (attrs && (typeof attrs !== 'object' || attrs instanceof Node || Array.isArray(attrs))) { kids.unshift(attrs); attrs = null; }
    for (const k in attrs || {}) {
      const v = attrs[k];
      if (v == null || v === false) continue;
      if (k.startsWith('on')) el.addEventListener(k.slice(2), v);
      else if (k === 'style' && typeof v === 'object') Object.assign(el.style, v);
      else if (k === 'html') el.innerHTML = v;
      else if (k === 'class') el.className += ' ' + v;
      else el.setAttribute(k, v === true ? '' : v);
    }
    const add = (k) => { if (k == null || k === false) return; if (Array.isArray(k)) k.forEach(add); else if (k instanceof Node) el.append(k); else el.appendChild(document.createTextNode(String(k))); };
    kids.forEach(add);
    return el;
  };
  const h = UI.h;
  UI.esc = (s) => String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c]));

    gem: 'M6 3h12l4 6-10 12L2 9zM2 9h20M12 21L8 9l4-6 4 6z',

  UI.icon = function (name, size, cls) {
    const ns = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
    const s = document.createElementNS(ns, 'svg');
    s.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 24 24'); s.setAttribute('width', size || 18); s.setAttribute('height', size || 18);
    s.setAttribute('class', 'ico ' + (cls || ''));
    const p = document.createElementNS(ns, 'path');
    p.setAttribute('d', P[name] || P.star); p.setAttribute('fill', 'none'); p.setAttribute('stroke', 'currentColor');
    p.setAttribute('stroke-width', '2'); p.setAttribute('stroke-linecap', 'round'); p.setAttribute('stroke-linejoin', 'round');
    s.appendChild(p);
    return s;
  };

  // ---------- colours ----------
  UI.TAGC = { Fire: '#ff7a3d', Water: '#4fb3e8', Earth: '#b08a5a', Air: '#9fd8ec', Life: '#6fd38a', Death: '#9c88bd', Holy: '#f2d27a', Shadow: '#8a77c4', Mind: '#c07bd8', Beast: '#d39556', Metal: '#a9b4bf', Arcane: '#7b8cff', Blood: '#d4544a', Poison: '#9ccc45', Order: '#d9c08c', Trickery: '#e07fb5', Protection: '#7fb0d9', War: '#e06a4f', Fate: '#e0c35a', Light: '#ffe9a3', Hunt: '#a9b56f', Craft: '#c79a62', Sound: '#8fd6c6', Dream: '#a49bf0', Space: '#6f7df0', Sin: '#c24a7c', Wealth: '#e3b341', Void: '#8b8bb8', Force: '#9aa7ff', Storm: '#6fa6d9', Time: '#cdbd8a', Cold: '#a8e0ff' };
  UI.essColor = function (id) { const e = EB.content.essences[id]; if (!e) return '#aaa'; const t = Object.keys(e.tags).sort((a, b) => e.tags[b] - e.tags[a])[0]; return UI.TAGC[t] || '#cfc7b8'; };
  UI.abColor = (ab) => UI.essColor(ab.essence);

    root.className = 'scr-' + name;
    const s = UI.screens[name];
    if (!s) { root.appendChild(h('div.pad', 'Unknown screen ' + name)); return; }
    s(root, UI.params);
    root.scrollTop = 0; window.scrollTo(0, 0);
  };

    wrap.appendChild(t);
    setTimeout(() => t.classList.add('out'), 2300);
    setTimeout(() => t.remove(), 2800);
  };

  UI.sheet = function (title, body, actions) {

        h('div.sheet-body', body), actions && actions.length ? h('div.sheet-actions', actions) : null));

  };

  // Closing any sheet opened over a finished fight's results brings the results back, so Continue is never lost.

  UI.confirm = function (title, text, yes, onYes) { UI.sheet(title, UI.para(text), [UI.btn('Cancel', UI.closeSheet, 'ghost'), UI.btn(yes || 'Confirm', () => { UI.closeSheet(); onYes(); }, 'primary')]); };

  UI.bar = function (v, max, cls, label) {
    const pct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (100 * v) / Math.max(1, max)));

  // Training bar: earned progress solid, core-fed progress striped.

    return h('div.bar.xp' + (thin ? '.thin' : ''), h('div.fill', { style: { width: earned + '%' } }), h('div.fill.cored', { style: { left: earned + '%', width: cored + '%' } }), thin ? null : h('span.lbl', `${Math.round(s.train)}%`));
  };
  UI.seasonNote = function (g, id) {
    const p = EB.G.seasonPenalty(g, id);
    if (!p.u) return null;

  };
  // Wrap status names in text so they can be tapped for an explanation.
  UI._stRe = null;
  UI.richText = function (text) {
    text = String(text || '');
    if (!UI._stRe) {
      const map = {};
      for (const id in EB.content.statuses) { const n = EB.content.statuses[id].name; if (n && n.length > 2) map[n] = id; }
      UI._stMap = map;
      const names = Object.keys(map).sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length).map((n) => n.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&'));
      UI._stRe = new RegExp('\\b(' + names.join('|') + ')\\b', 'g');
    }
    const out = []; let last = 0; let m;
    UI._stRe.lastIndex = 0;
    while ((m = UI._stRe.exec(text))) {
      if (m.index > last) out.push(text.slice(last, m.index));
      const id = UI._stMap[m[1]];
      out.push(h('span.kw' + (EB.K.isBoon(id) ? '.boon' : '.affl'), { onclick: (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); UI.statusSheet(id); } }, m[1]));
      last = m.index + m[0].length;
    }
    if (last < text.length) out.push(text.slice(last));
    return out;
  };
  UI.statusText = function (id) { const d = EB.K.statusDef(id); return d.text || ''; };

  UI.save = function () { if (UI.g) EB.G.save(UI.g); };

  // Top status header for exploration screens

    );

  // Bottom navigation

    const g = UI.g;
    const where = g.loc === 'verge' ? 'map' : g.loc === 'hollow' ? 'hollow' : 'town';
    const item = (key, label, icon, go) => h('button.nav-btn' + (active === key ? '.on' : ''), { onclick: () => UI.go(go || key) }, UI.icon(icon, 20), h('span', label));
    const badge = EB.G.trialReady(g) ? h('i.dot') : null;
    const char = item('char', 'Essence', 'gem'); if (badge) char.appendChild(badge);
    return h('nav.bottom', item('explore', g.loc === 'town' ? 'City' : 'Verge', g.loc === 'town' ? 'home' : 'map', where), char, item('bag', 'Bag', 'bag'), item('journal', 'Journal', 'book'), item('settings', 'Menu', 'gear'));
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, G = EB.G, W = EB.W, S = UI.screens;
  const C = () => EB.content;

  // ---------- ability detail ----------
  UI.abilitySheet = function (id, extra) {
    const a = C().abilities[id];
    if (!a) return;
    const g = UI.g;
    const st = g && g.abilities[id];
    const costs = Object.entries(a.cost || {}).map(([k, v]) => `${v}${k === 'health' ? '% health' : ' ' + k}`).join(', ') || 'Free';
    const rid = a.riders && a.riders.bronze; const r = rid && C().riders[rid];
    const body = [
      h('div.ab-tags', h('span.pill', { style: { color: UI.abColor(a) } }, C().essences[a.essence].name), h('span.pill', a.type.replace('_', ' ')), a.passive ? h('span.pill', 'passive') : null, a.toggle ? h('span.pill', 'toggle') : null, h('span.pill', (a.tempo || 'standard') + ' tempo')),
      h('p.ab-sum', UI.richText(a.summary)),
      h('p.flavor', '"' + a.flavor + '"'),
      h('div.kv', h('span', 'Cost'), h('b', costs)),
      h('div.kv', h('span', 'Cooldown'), h('b', a.cooldown ? a.cooldown + ' turns' : 'none')),
      h('div.kv', h('span', 'Target'), h('b', a.target.replace(/_/g, ' '))),
      r ? h('div.kv', h('span', 'At Bronze'), h('b', r.text)) : null,
      a.riders && a.riders.silver && C().riders[a.riders.silver] ? h('div.kv', h('span', 'At Silver'), h('b', C().riders[a.riders.silver].text)) : null,
      a.evolution ? h('p.dim', a.evolution) : null,
      a.social ? h('div.kv', h('span', 'Social: ' + a.social.tag), h('b', a.social.text)) : null,
      st ? UI.btn(g.favorites && g.favorites[id] ? '★ Pinned to the top of your combat tray' : '☆ Pin to the top of your combat tray', () => { g.favorites = g.favorites || {}; if (g.favorites[id]) delete g.favorites[id]; else g.favorites[id] = true; UI.save(); UI.abilitySheet(id, extra); }, 'small ghost wide') : null,
      st ? h('div.train', h('div.kv', h('span', EB.K.RANK_NAMES[st.rank] + '-rank ability'), h('b', Math.round(st.train) + '% trained')), UI.trainBar(g, id), UI.seasonNote(g, id)) : null,
      extra || null,
    ];
    UI.sheet(a.name, body);
  };
  UI.statusSheet = function (id, st) {
    const d = EB.K.statusDef(id);
    UI.sheet(d.name, [h('p', UI.richText(d.text || '')), h('div.kv', h('span', d.kind === 'boon' ? 'Boon' : 'Affliction'), h('b', d.category)), st ? h('div.kv', h('span', 'Stacks'), h('b', String(Math.round(st.stacks * 10) / 10))) : null, st && st.dur != null ? h('div.kv', h('span', 'Duration'), h('b', st.dur === 'combat' ? 'until combat ends' : st.dur + ' turn' + (st.dur > 1 ? 's' : ''))) : null]);
  };

  // ---------- title ----------
  EB.TITLE_ART = `<svg class="eb-title-artwork" viewBox="0 0 520 690" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" role="presentation" focusable="false" aria-hidden="true">
