  S.title = function (root) {
    const has = G.hasSave();
    let save = null;
    if (has) { try { const g = G.load(); if (g && g.name) save = G.saveInfo(g); } catch (_) {} }
    const card = h('div.eb-title-content');
    card.append(
      h('div.eb-title-topline', 'A Calderhal saga'),
      h('h1.eb-title-wordmark', 'Essence', h('span', 'bound')),
      h('div.eb-title-rule'),
      h('p.eb-title-tagline', 'The world remembers what you become.'),
      h('p.eb-title-description', 'Forge your awakening. Walk the Verge. Choose who stands beside you when the lanterns go dark.')
    );
    if (save) card.append(h('div.eb-title-saved',
      h('div.eb-title-saved-head', 'Your current journey'),
      h('div.eb-title-saved-name', save.name),
      h('div.eb-title-saved-meta', `${save.rank} · Day ${save.day} · ${save.where}`)
    ));
    const actions = h('div.eb-title-actions');
    const action = (title, desc, cssClass, click, icon) => {
      const b = h('button.eb-title-action.'+cssClass, {type:'button', onclick:click},
        h('span.eb-title-action-text', h('span', title), desc ? h('span.ebt-action-note',desc) : null),
        UI.icon(icon || 'chev',20));
      return b;
    };
    const newGame = () => has
      ? UI.confirm('Begin another journey?', 'Your current autosave will be replaced when you first save the new character. You can store this character in a manual save slot first.', 'New game', () => UI.go('create'))
      : UI.go('create');
    if (has && save) actions.append(action('Continue Journey', 'Return to your last adventure', 'primary', () => { UI.g=G.load(); if (UI.g) UI.resume(); else UI.toast('This save could not be loaded.','bad'); }, 'chev'));
    actions.append(action(has ? 'Begin New Journey' : 'Begin Your Journey', has ? 'Create a new Awakened' : 'Create your character and enter Calderhal', has && save ? 'secondary' : 'primary',newGame,'gem'));
    card.append(actions);
    const links = h('div.eb-title-subactions');
    const link = (label,click) => h('button.eb-title-link',{type:'button',onclick:click},label);
    links.append(link('Saved games',()=>UI.loadSheet()),link('Import save',()=>UI.importSheet()),link('How to play',()=>UI.sheet('Before the first lantern',[
      h('p','Choose an origin and temperament, then discover four Essences. Their combination shapes your abilities and confluence.'),
      h('p','Awakening Stones create unique powers for each Essence. Train the abilities you favor and rise from Iron toward Gold.'),
      h('p','Travel between Calderhal, the Verge and Hollows. Combat is turn-based; positioning, statuses and timing matter.'),
      h('p','Your choices shape companions, societies, and the fate of the city.'),
      h('p.dim.small','Progress saves locally in this browser. Use Saved games or export a save code to keep a backup.')
    ],[UI.btn('Begin',UI.closeSheet,'primary')])));
    if (G.fallen().length) links.append(link('Hall of the Fallen',()=>UI.hallSheet()));
    card.append(links,
      h('div.eb-title-sigil','Legacy & Horizons'),
      h('div.eb-title-footer',h('b','An offline, single-player adventure.'),' Your progress stays in this browser unless you export it.'));
    const art = h('div.eb-title-illustration',{ 'aria-hidden':'true' });
    art.innerHTML = EB.TITLE_ART;
    root.append(h('main.eb-title-page'+(save?'.has-save':'.fresh'), h('div.eb-title-shell',art,card)));
  };
  UI.importSheet = function () {
    const ta = h('textarea.code', { placeholder: 'Paste a save code' });
    UI.sheet('Import save', [ta], [UI.btn('Import', () => { const g = G.importCode(ta.value); if (!g) return UI.toast('That code didn\'t work.', 'bad'); UI.g = g; UI.save(); UI.closeSheet(); UI.resume(); }, 'primary')]);
  };
  UI.resume = function () {
    const g = UI.g;
    if (g.scene) return UI.playScene(g.scene.id, g.scene.node);
    if (g.loc === 'prologue') return UI.playScene('prologue');
    UI.go(g.loc === 'verge' ? 'map' : g.loc === 'hollow' ? 'hollow' : 'town');
  };

  // ---------- creation ----------
  S.create = function (root, p) {
    const st = (UI.createState = UI.createState || { name: '', origin: null, temper: null });
    const wrap = h('div.pad.create');
    wrap.append(h('h2', 'Who were you, before?'));
    const name = h('input.name', { value: st.name, placeholder: 'Your name', maxlength: 20, oninput: (e) => { st.name = e.target.value; } });
    wrap.append(h('label.lbl', 'Name'), name);
    wrap.append(h('h3', 'Origin'));
    const og = h('div.cards');
    for (const [id, o] of Object.entries(W.ORIGINS)) {
      og.append(h('button.card.pick' + (st.origin === id ? '.on' : ''), { onclick: () => { st.origin = id; UI.refresh(); } },
        h('div.card-title', o.name), h('p', o.blurb), h('p.perk', o.perk),
        h('div.chips', Object.entries(o.attrs).map(([k, v]) => h('span.chip', `+${v} ${k}`)))));
    }
    wrap.append(og);
    wrap.append(h('h3', 'Temperament'), h('p.dim', 'How you sound when you open your mouth. Changes your dialogue, not your stats.'));
    const tg = h('div.cards.two');
    for (const [id, t] of Object.entries(W.TEMPERS)) tg.append(h('button.card.pick.small' + (st.temper === id ? '.on' : ''), { onclick: () => { st.temper = id; UI.refresh(); } }, h('div.card-title', t.name), h('p', t.blurb)));
    wrap.append(tg);
    wrap.append(h('h3', 'Difficulty'));
    wrap.append(h('div.cards.two',
      h('button.card.pick.small' + (!st.hardcore ? '.on' : ''), { onclick: () => { st.hardcore = false; UI.refresh(); } }, h('div.card-title', 'Standard'), h('p', 'Defeat sends you back to the Lodge, a little poorer. Save slots and autosave history.')),
      h('button.card.pick.small.hc' + (st.hardcore ? '.on' : ''), { onclick: () => { st.hardcore = true; UI.refresh(); } }, h('div.card-title', UI.icon('skull', 14), ' Hardcore'), h('p', 'One life. Die in a real fight and this character is gone for good. One save, no slots, no rewinds. Trials and duels are still safe.'))));
    const ok = st.origin && st.temper;
    wrap.append(h('div.sticky-foot', UI.btn('Begin', () => {
      const nm = (st.name || '').trim() || { dockhand: 'Brann', foundling: 'Wren', heir: 'Aurelian', smuggler: 'Kit', dropout: 'Ines', hunter: 'Rook' }[st.origin];
      UI.g = G.newGame(nm, st.origin, st.temper);
      UI.g.hardcore = !!st.hardcore;
      UI.createState = null;
      UI.save();
      UI.playScene('prologue');
    }, 'primary big', ok ? {} : { disabled: true })));
    root.append(wrap);
  };

  // ---------- scenes (dialogue) ----------
  UI.playScene = function (id, nodeId, onDone, params) {
    const g = UI.g;
    const sc = EB.SCENES[id];
    if (!sc) { console.warn('no scene', id); return onDone ? onDone() : UI.go('town'); }
    UI.sceneDone = onDone || null;
    UI.lastNodeApplied = null;
    g.scene = { id, node: nodeId || sc.start };
    UI.save();
    UI.go('scene', Object.assign({ id }, params || {}));
  };
  UI.endScene = function (gotoLoc) {
    const g = UI.g;
    g.scene = null;
    UI.save();
    const cb = UI.sceneDone; UI.sceneDone = null;
    if (gotoLoc === 'town') { g.loc = 'town'; return UI.go('town'); }
    if (gotoLoc === 'verge') { g.loc = 'verge'; return UI.go('map'); }
    if (cb) return cb();
    UI.resume();
  };
  S.scene = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g;
    const sc = EB.SCENES[g.scene.id];
    let node = sc.nodes[g.scene.node];
    // skip nodes whose condition fails
    while (node && node.if && !G.check(g, node.if)) { g.scene.node = node.n; node = sc.nodes[node.n]; }
    if (!node) return UI.endScene();
    // node effects (once per visit)
    const key = g.scene.id + '.' + g.scene.node;
    let out = { notes: [] };
    // A node that sets a story flag pays out once: replaying a conversation never repeats its gifts.
    const onceDone = node.d && node.d.flag && [].concat(node.d.flag).every((f) => g.flags[f]);
    if (node.d && UI.lastNodeApplied !== key && !onceDone) { UI.lastNodeApplied = key; out = G.apply(g, node.d); UI.notes(out); UI.save(); }
    if (out.shop) { const back = UI.sceneDone; g.scene = null; UI.sceneDone = null; return UI.go('shop', { shop: out.shop, back }); }
    if (out.payContract && UI.params.contract && !g.quests[UI.params.contract].done) { const o2 = G.payContract(g, UI.params.contract); UI.notes(o2); UI.save(); if (o2.essenceChoice) setTimeout(() => UI.stipendSheet(o2.essenceChoice), 300); }
    const spk = W.NPCS[node.s] || W.NPCS.nar;
    const card = h('div.dlg' + (spk.system ? '.sys' : spk.narrator ? '.nar' : ''));
    if (!spk.narrator) card.append(h('div.dlg-who', { style: { color: spk.color } }, spk.system ? h('span', UI.icon('gem', 14), ' Essence') : spk.name));
    let text = G.fmt(g, node.t, { lastEssence: UI.lastEssenceName, lastAbility: UI.lastAbilityName, contractName: UI.params.contract && W.QUESTS[UI.params.contract].name });
    if (out.rumour) text += '\n\n"' + out.rumour + '"';
    card.append(h('div.dlg-text', UI.para(text)));
    const opts = h('div.dlg-opts');
    const next = (go, onFightDone) => {
      UI.lastNodeApplied = null;
      if (out.fight) return;
      if (!go || go === 'end') return UI.endScene(out.goto);
      g.scene.node = go; UI.save(); UI.refresh();
    };
    // fights started by node effects
    if (out.fight) {
      const f = out.fight;
      const sid = g.scene.id, keep = UI.params;
      opts.append(UI.btn('Fight!', () => UI.startCombat(f.enc, (sum) => { UI.lastNodeApplied = null; g.scene = { id: sid, node: sum.win ? f.win : f.lose }; if (!g.scene.node) return UI.endScene(); UI.save(); UI.go('scene', keep); }), 'primary big'));
    } else if (node.pickTrait) {
      for (const tid of G.bronzeChoices(g)) {
        const T = W.TRAITS[tid];
        opts.append(h('button.card.ess-pick', { onclick: () => UI.confirm(`Become ${T.name}?`, T.text + '\n\nThis is permanent.', 'Choose', () => { G.takeTrait(g, tid); g.pendingTrait = false; UI.save(); next(node.n); }) },
          h('div.card-title', { style: { color: 'var(--amber2)' } }, T.name), h('p', T.text)));
      }
    } else if (node.pickEssence) {
      const choices = G.prologueChoices(g);
      for (const eid of choices) {
        const E = C().essences[eid]; const ab = C().abilities[E.starter];
        opts.append(h('button.card.ess-pick', { style: { borderColor: UI.essColor(eid) }, onclick: () => UI.confirm(`Absorb ${E.name}?`, `${E.identity}\n\nWhat it wakes in you, you'll find out when it does.`, 'Absorb', () => {
          G.absorbEssence(g, eid, { free: true });
          UI.lastEssenceName = E.name; UI.lastAbilityName = ab.name;
          next(node.n);
        }) },
        h('div.card-title', { style: { color: UI.essColor(eid) } }, E.name, h('span.rar', { style: { color: UI.RAR[E.rarity] } }, ' ' + E.rarity)),
        h('p', E.identity), E.taboo ? h('p.warn', E.taboo > 1 ? 'Hunted by the Dawn Choir.' : 'Frowned on by the Dawn Choir.') : null));
      }
    } else if (node.o) {
      for (const o of node.o) {
        if (o.if && !G.check(g, o.if)) continue;
        const label = (o.v && o.v[g.temper]) || o.t;
        const gated = o.if && (o.if.social || o.if.tag || o.if.origin);
        opts.append(h('button.opt' + (gated ? '.gated' : ''), { onclick: () => {
          const r = G.apply(g, o.d); UI.notes(r);
          if (r.rumour) UI.sheet('Nell leans in', [h('p', '"' + r.rumour + '"')]);
          if (r.fight) { const sid = g.scene.id, keep = UI.params; return UI.startCombat(r.fight.enc, (sum) => { g.scene = { id: sid, node: sum.win ? r.fight.win : r.fight.lose }; UI.lastNodeApplied = null; if (!g.scene.node) return UI.endScene(); UI.save(); UI.go('scene', keep); }); }
          if (r.goto) return UI.endScene(r.goto);
          if (r.shop) { const back = UI.sceneDone; g.scene = null; UI.sceneDone = null; return UI.go('shop', { shop: r.shop, back }); }
          next(o.g || node.n || 'end');
        } }, G.fmt(g, label)));
      }
      if (!opts.children.length) opts.append(UI.btn('Continue', () => next(node.n || 'end'), 'primary'));
    } else {
      opts.append(UI.btn(node.n ? 'Continue' : 'Done', () => { if (out.scene) { UI.lastNodeApplied = null; return UI.playScene(out.scene, null, UI.sceneDone); } if (out.goto) return UI.endScene(out.goto); next(node.n || 'end'); }, 'primary'));
    }
    const sceneBox = h('div.scene-wrap', h('div.scene-loc', W.NPCS[node.s] && !W.NPCS[node.s].narrator && !W.NPCS[node.s].system ? '' : ''), card, opts);
    if (['maud','caspian','gannet','anselm','seraphine','ottoline'].includes(node.s)) {
      const portrait = h('img.art-portrait', { src: 'assets/npcs/' + node.s + '.webp', alt: spk.name, loading: 'lazy', decoding: 'async', onerror: e => e.currentTarget.remove() });
      sceneBox.insertBefore(portrait, card);
    }
    root.append(sceneBox);
  };

  // ---------- town ----------
  UI.LOCS = {
    lodge: { name: 'Lantern Society Lodge', icon: 'lantern', desc: 'Contracts, the training yard, and Maud.' },
    exchange: { name: 'Marrow Exchange', icon: 'coin', desc: 'Stones, essences, cores, and Ottoline\'s monocle.' },
    lamp: { name: 'The Drowned Lamp', icon: 'home', desc: 'Rest, train, absorb, gossip. Your room is upstairs.' },
    steps: { name: 'Council Steps', icon: 'star', desc: 'Seven chairs, one empty, all hungry.' },
    magisterium: { name: 'Magisterium Annex', icon: 'book', desc: 'Scholars, biscuits, and the Mirror Trial.' },
    chapel: { name: 'Dawn Choir Chapel', icon: 'up', desc: 'Healing, penance, and a very long list.' },
    temples: { name: 'Temple Row', icon: 'flame', desc: 'The Ferry House, the Gilded Wheel and Hornhall, side by side and not speaking.' },
    orrery: { name: 'The Unlit Orrery', icon: 'eye', desc: 'A dark dome where people venerate things that are not gods.' },
    undercroft: { name: 'The Undercroft', icon: 'eye', desc: 'Under the Lamp\'s cellar. Sixpence sells what others won\'t.', need: 'undercroft_open' },
    gate: { name: 'Verge Gate', icon: 'map', desc: 'Out to the wilds.' },
  };
  S.town = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g; g.loc = 'town';
    if (g.origin === 'smuggler') g.flags.undercroft_open = true;
    if (g.flags.slice_done && !g.flags.act2_open) { g.flags.act2_open = true; UI.save(); return UI.playScene('act2_open', null, () => UI.go('town')); }
    root.append(UI.header('Calderhal', `Day ${g.day} · ${G.rankName(g)}${g.hardcore ? ' · Hardcore' : ''}`));
    const list = h('div.pad.locs');
    const q = UI.questHint();
    if (q) list.append(h('div.hint', UI.icon('star', 14), ' ', q));
    for (const [id, L] of Object.entries(UI.LOCS)) {
      if (L.need && !g.flags[L.need]) continue;
      const badge = UI.locBadge(id);
      list.append(h('button.loc', { onclick: () => id === 'gate' ? (G.hasUtil(g, 'waypoint') ? UI.waypointSheet() : UI.toVerge()) : UI.go('loc', { id }) }, h('div.loc-ico', UI.icon(L.icon, 22)), h('div.loc-txt', h('div.loc-name', L.name, badge ? h('span.badge', badge) : null), h('div.loc-desc', L.desc)), UI.icon('chev', 16, 'chev')));
    }
    if (g.suspicion >= 30) list.append(h('div.warnbox', UI.icon('eye', 14), ` Suspicion ${g.suspicion}: the Dawn Choir is watching you${g.suspicion >= 60 ? ', and the Exchange charges you extra' : ''}.`));
    root.append(list);
    root.append(UI.nav('explore'));
  };
  UI.toVerge = function () { const g = UI.g; g.loc = 'verge'; g.node = 'gate'; UI.save(); UI.go('map'); };
  UI.locBadge = function (id) {
    const g = UI.g;
    if (id === 'lodge' && G.contractsToReport(g).length) return 'report';
    if (id === 'exchange' && !g.flags.met_ottoline) return 'new';
    if (id === 'steps' && g.flags.matriarch_dead && !g.flags.council_done) return '!';
    if (id === 'steps' && !g.flags.knows_hollow && G.questActive(g, 'main_glass')) return 'new';
    if (id === 'magisterium' && G.trialReady(g)) return 'trial';
    if (id === 'magisterium' && G.questActive(g, 'act2_embers') && g.quests.act2_embers.stage === 0) return '!';
    if (id === 'chapel' && G.questActive(g, 'act2_embers') && g.quests.act2_embers.stage === 1) return '!';
    if (id === 'lodge' && (G.questActive(g, 'act2_embers') && g.quests.act2_embers.stage === 4 || g.flags.prior_dead && G.questActive(g, 'act2_cloister') || UI.act2EndReady())) return '!';
    if (id === 'steps' && G.count(g, 'letters') && G.questActive(g, 'act2_traitor')) return '!';
    if (id === 'chapel' && G.questActive(g, 's_confess')) return '!';
    if (id === 'lodge' && G.society(g).active.some((t) => t.have >= t.need)) return 'report';
    if (id === 'chapel' && G.path(g).id === 'solenne' && UI.templeHot('solenne')) return '!';
    if ((id === 'temples' || id === 'orrery') && G.path(g).id !== 'unsworn' && UI.templeLoc(G.path(g).id) === id && UI.templeHot(G.path(g).id)) return '!';
    return null;
  };
  UI.act2EndReady = function () { const g = UI.g; return !!(g.flags.prior_dead && g.quests.act2_cloister && g.quests.act2_cloister.done && g.quests.act2_traitor && g.quests.act2_traitor.done && g.rank >= 2 && !g.flags.act2_done); };
  UI.questHint = function () {
    const g = UI.g;
    const main = ['main_iron', 'main_lantern', 'main_glass', 'act2_embers', 'act2_cloister', 'act2_traitor', 'main_bronze', 'main_silver'].find((q) => G.questActive(g, q));
    if (UI.act2EndReady()) return 'Everything is done. Maud is waiting at the Lodge.';
    if (!main) return g.flags.act2_done ? 'Act II is complete. Keep exploring, training and fighting as long as you like.' : null;
    return W.QUESTS[main].stages[g.quests[main].stage];
  };

  // ---------- locations ----------
  S.loc = function (root, p) {
    const g = UI.g; const L = UI.LOCS[p.id];
    // first-visit scenes
    if (p.id === 'exchange' && !g.flags.met_ottoline) return UI.playScene('ottoline_first', null, () => UI.go('loc', p));
    if (p.id === 'steps' && !g.flags.knows_hollow && (G.questActive(g, 'main_glass') || g.quests.main_glass)) return UI.playScene('council_first', null, () => UI.go('loc', p));
    root.append(UI.header(L.name));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    body.append(h('p.loc-intro', L.desc));
    const acts = h('div.acts');
    const act = (label, icon, fn, sub, cls) => acts.append(h('button.act' + (cls ? '.' + cls : ''), { onclick: fn }, UI.icon(icon, 20), h('div', h('div.act-name', label), sub ? h('div.act-sub', sub) : null)));
    const talk = (scene, npc, label) => act(label || `Talk to ${W.NPCS[npc].short}`, 'chat', () => UI.playScene(scene, null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), W.NPCS[npc].blurb);
    switch (p.id) {
      case 'lodge': {
        const rep = G.contractsToReport(g);
        for (const c of rep) act(`Report: ${W.QUESTS[c].name.replace('Contract: ', '').replace('Bounty: ', '')}`, 'star', () => UI.playSceneWith('maud_report', { contract: c }, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'Collect your reward.', 'hot');
        if (G.questActive(g, 'act2_embers') && g.quests.act2_embers.stage === 4) act('Tell Maud about the Cloister', 'lantern', () => UI.playScene('maud_act2', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'She\'ll want to hear this.', 'hot');
        if (g.flags.prior_dead && G.questActive(g, 'act2_cloister')) act('Report to Maud', 'lantern', () => UI.playScene('maud_cloister_done', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'The storm came. It was only rain.', 'hot');
        if (UI.act2EndReady()) act('Join Maud for a drink', 'lantern', () => UI.playScene('act2_end', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'Act II finale.', 'hot');
        if (g.flags.act2_open && !g.flags.s_varo_seen && !(g.quests.s_varo && g.quests.s_varo.done) && (g.npc.caspian.aff >= 5 || g.flags.beat_caspian || g.flags.caspian_helped)) act('Caspian, on the steps', 'chat', () => { g.flags.s_varo_seen = true; UI.playScene('caspian_debt', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)); }, 'He\'s sitting in the rain.');
        act('Contracts board', 'log', () => UI.contractsSheet(), `${G.contractsOffered(g).length} posted`);
        { const s = G.society(g); const ready = s.active.filter((t) => t.have >= t.need).length; act('Board work', 'log', () => UI.boardSheet(), ready ? `${ready} ready to collect` : `${s.active.length}/2 taken, ${G.boardOffers(g).length} posted`, ready ? 'hot' : ''); }
        { const nx = G.societyNext(g); act(`Society rank: ${G.societyDef(g).name}`, 'lantern', () => UI.societySheet(), nx ? (nx.ready ? 'Ready for promotion.' : nx.assessReady ? 'Ready for assessment.' : `Next: ${nx.name}`) : 'Highest rank.', nx && (nx.ready || nx.assessReady) ? 'hot' : ''); }
        if (G.path(g).id === 'unsworn') { const nx = G.pathNext(g); act(`Your own road: ${G.pathRankName(g)}`, 'user', () => UI.roadSheet(), nx && (nx.ready || nx.trialReady) ? 'Ready to advance.' : `Resolve ${G.path(g).pts}. No god required.`, nx && (nx.ready || nx.trialReady) ? 'hot' : ''); }
        talk('maud_chat', 'maud');
        if (G.questActive(g, 'main_glass') || g.quests.c_wolves) { if (!g.quests.s_rival || (g.quests.s_rival && !g.quests.s_rival.done)) act('Caspian Varo, in the yard', 'sword', () => UI.playScene('caspian_challenge', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), g.quests.s_rival ? 'He\'s still waiting. Loudly.' : 'Someone in red is showing off.'); }
        act('Infirmary cot', 'cross', () => { if (g.hp >= G.maxHp(g)) return UI.toast('You\'re already fine.'); g.hp = Math.max(g.hp, Math.round(G.maxHp(g) * 0.75)); UI.save(); UI.toast('Patched up to 75%.', 'good'); UI.refresh(); }, 'Free. Gets you to 75% health.');
        break;
      }
      case 'exchange':
        act('Buy', 'bag', () => UI.go('shop', { shop: 'exchange' }), 'Stones, essences, gear, draughts.');
        act('Sell', 'coin', () => UI.go('shop', { shop: 'exchange', sell: true }), 'Cores and spare gear.');
        if (G.count(g, 'shard') && g.flags.ottoline_hollow && !g.flags.shard_shown) act('Show Ottoline the Heart-Shard', 'gem', () => { g.flags.shard_shown = true; UI.notes(G.apply(g, { marks: 80, standing: { selenne: 6 }, affinity: { ottoline: 8 } })); UI.sheet('Ottoline', [h('p', '"Oh. Oh my." She doesn\'t touch it, which is the most respectful thing you\'ve seen her do. "Keep it for the Council. But here, a finder\'s fee, so you remember who saw it first."')]); UI.save(); }, 'She asked to see first.');
        talk('ottoline_stones', 'ottoline', 'Ask Ottoline about stones');
        break;
      case 'lamp':
        act('Rest the night', 'zz', () => { if (g.marks < 10) return UI.toast('Nell wants 10 marks for the room.', 'bad'); g.marks -= 10; G.rest(g); UI.save(); UI.toast(`Day ${g.day}. Fully rested.`, 'good'); UI.refresh(); }, '10 marks. Full heal, new day, core training resets.');
        act('Train with cores', 'up', () => UI.go('train'), `${G.count(g, 'core')} Iron Cores`);
        act('Absorb stones & essences', 'gem', () => UI.go('char'), `${g.stones.length} stone${g.stones.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}, ${g.essenceItems.length} essence${g.essenceItems.length === 1 ? '' : 's'}`);
        talk('nell', 'nell');
        act('Save game', 'book', () => { UI.save(); UI.toast('Saved.', 'good'); }, 'Also saves automatically.');
        break;
      case 'steps':
        if (G.count(g, 'letters') && G.questActive(g, 'act2_traitor')) act('Confront Petra Selenne', 'coin', () => UI.playScene('petra_confront', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'You know whose seal is on the letters.', 'hot');
        talk('council_chat', 'seraphine', 'Approach the Council'); break;
      case 'magisterium':
        if (G.questActive(g, 'act2_embers') && g.quests.act2_embers.stage === 0) act('Show Hale the Heart-Shard', 'gem', () => UI.playScene('hale_shard', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'The sigil is still glowing.', 'hot');
        if (G.trialReady(g) && g.rank === 1) act('The Trial of the Unmaking', 'star', () => UI.playScene('hale_silver', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'Bronze to Silver.', 'hot');
        talk('hale', 'hale'); act('Essence surgery', 'x', () => UI.surgerySheet('magisterium'), 'Remove a stone or an essence. Permanent consequences.', 'danger'); break;
      case 'chapel':
        if (G.questActive(g, 'act2_embers') && g.quests.act2_embers.stage === 1) act('Ask Anselm about the sigil', 'eye', () => UI.playScene('anselm_sigil', null, () => UI.go('loc', p)), 'A ring of ash around an open eye.', 'hot');
        act('Solenne\'s altar', 'flame', () => UI.go('temple', { id: 'solenne' }), G.path(g).id === 'solenne' ? `${G.pathRankName(g)}. ${G.path(g).pts} Devotion.` : 'Worship the Morning.', G.path(g).id === 'solenne' && UI.templeHot('solenne') ? 'hot' : '');
        talk('anselm', 'anselm'); break;
      case 'temples': case 'orrery':
        for (const pid of p.id === 'temples' ? ['morrow', 'kesh', 'vask'] : ['loom', 'eye']) { const P = W.PATHS[pid]; act(p.id === 'orrery' ? P.name : P.templeName, p.id === 'orrery' ? 'eye' : 'flame', () => UI.go('temple', { id: pid }), G.path(g).id === pid ? `${G.pathRankName(g)}. ${G.path(g).pts} ${P.pointName}.` : P.blurb, G.path(g).id === pid && UI.templeHot(pid) ? 'hot' : ''); }
        if (p.id === 'orrery') body.append(h('p.dim.small', 'Great astral beings are older than the gods and much less interested in people. Venerating one is not worship: it does not listen. But sometimes it notices.'));
        break;
      case 'undercroft':
        act('Black market', 'bag', () => UI.go('shop', { shop: 'black' }), 'Taboo essences. Rare stones. No questions.');
        act('Back-alley extraction', 'x', () => UI.surgerySheet('undercroft'), 'Stones only. Cheap. Risky.', 'danger');
        talk('sixpence', 'sixpence');
        break;
    }
    body.append(acts);
    root.append(body);
    root.append(h('div.pad', UI.btn('Back to the streets', () => UI.go('town'), 'ghost wide')));
    root.append(UI.nav('explore'));
  };
  UI.playSceneWith = function (id, params, done) { UI.playScene(id, null, done, params); };

  UI.contractsSheet = function () {
    const g = UI.g;
    const offered = G.contractsOffered(g);
    const active = Object.keys(g.quests).filter((id) => W.QUESTS[id].contract && !g.quests[id].done);
    const body = [];
    if (active.length) body.push(h('h4', 'Active'), ...active.map((id) => h('div.qline', h('b', W.QUESTS[id].name), h('div.dim', W.QUESTS[id].stages[g.quests[id].stage]))));
    body.push(h('h4', 'Posted'));
    if (!offered.length) body.push(h('p.dim', 'Nothing new. Maud says the bloom-beasts are "having a quiet week", ominously.'));
    for (const id of offered) {
      const Q = W.QUESTS[id];
      body.push(h('div.contract', h('div.card-title', Q.name), h('p', Q.stages[0]), h('p.dim', `Reward: ${Q.reward.marks} marks${Q.reward.cores ? `, ${Q.reward.cores} cores` : ''}`), UI.btn('Take it', () => {
        G.startQuest(g, id); G.setStage(g, 'main_lantern', 2); UI.save(); UI.toast('Contract taken.', 'good'); UI.contractsSheet();
      }, 'primary small')));
    }
    UI.sheet('Contracts Board', body);
  };

  // ---------- shop ----------
  S.shop = function (root, p) {
    const g = UI.g;
    const black = p.shop === 'black';
    const landing = p.shop === 'landing';
    root.append(UI.header(black ? 'Black Market' : landing ? 'Gannet\'s Landing' : 'Marrow Exchange', p.sell ? 'Selling' : 'Buying'));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    const tabs = h('div.tabs', UI.btn('Buy', () => UI.go('shop', { shop: p.shop, back: p.back }), p.sell ? 'tab' : 'tab on'), UI.btn('Sell', () => UI.go('shop', { shop: p.shop, sell: true, back: p.back }), p.sell ? 'tab on' : 'tab'));
    body.append(tabs);
    if (p.sell) {
      const sellable = Object.keys(g.inv).filter((id) => W.ITEMS[id] && W.ITEMS[id].kind !== 'quest' && W.ITEMS[id].price > 0);
      if (!sellable.length) body.append(h('p.dim', 'Nothing to sell.'));
      for (const id of sellable) {
        const it = W.ITEMS[id]; const pr = G.sellPrice(g, id);
        body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', it.name), h('span.dim', ` ×${g.inv[id]}`), h('div.dim.small', it.desc)), h('div.row-r',
          UI.btn(`Sell ${pr}`, () => { G.give(g, id, -1); g.marks += pr; UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small'),
          g.inv[id] > 1 ? UI.btn('All', () => { g.marks += pr * g.inv[id]; delete g.inv[id]; UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small ghost') : null)));
      }
    } else {
      const st = G.shopStock(g, p.shop);
      if (st.stones.length) body.append(h('h4', 'Awakening stones'), h('p.dim.small', 'Stock changes each day.'));
      for (const k of st.stones) {
        const sd = G.stoneDef(k); const pr = G.priceOf(g, 'stone', k, p.shop);
        body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', { style: { color: UI.RAR[sd.rarity] } }, sd.name), h('div.dim.small', sd.theme)), h('div.row-r', UI.btn(`${pr}`, () => buy(pr, () => { g.stones.push({ key: k, uid: EB.U.uid('s') + ':' + Date.now() % 100000 }); }), 'small'))));
      }
      if (st.essences.length) body.append(h('h4', 'Essences'));
      for (const eid of st.essences) {
        const E = G.ess(eid); const pr = G.priceOf(g, 'essence', eid, p.shop);
        const owned = G.allEssences(g).some((e) => e.id === eid) || g.essenceItems.includes(eid);
        body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', { style: { color: UI.essColor(eid) } }, E.name), h('span.rar', { style: { color: UI.RAR[E.rarity] } }, ' ' + E.rarity), h('div.dim.small', E.identity), E.taboo ? h('div.warn.small', 'Taboo') : null), h('div.row-r', UI.btn('Info', () => UI.essenceSheet(eid), 'small ghost'), owned ? h('span.dim.small', 'Owned') : UI.btn(`${pr}`, () => buy(pr, () => g.essenceItems.push(eid)), 'small'))));
      }
      body.append(h('h4', 'Goods'));
      for (const id of st.items) {
        const it = W.ITEMS[id]; const pr = G.priceOf(g, 'item', id, p.shop);
        body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', it.name), h('div.dim.small', it.desc)), h('div.row-r', UI.btn(`${pr}`, () => buy(pr, () => G.give(g, id, 1)), 'small'))));
      }
    }
    function buy(pr, fn) { if (g.marks < pr) return UI.toast('Not enough marks.', 'bad'); g.marks -= pr; fn(); UI.save(); UI.toast('Bought.', 'good'); UI.refresh(); }
    root.append(body);
    root.append(h('div.pad', UI.btn('Back', () => p.back ? p.back() : UI.go('loc', { id: black ? 'undercroft' : 'exchange' }), 'ghost wide')));
    root.append(UI.nav('explore'));
  };
  UI.essenceSheet = function (eid) {
    const E = G.ess(eid);
    const ab = C().abilities[E.starter];
    UI.sheet(E.name + ' essence', [h('p', E.identity), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Rarity'), h('b', { style: { color: UI.RAR[E.rarity] } }, E.rarity)), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Affinity'), h('b', Object.entries(E.affinity || {}).map(([k, v]) => `+${v} ${k}`).join(', '))), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Tags'), h('b', Object.keys(E.tags).join(', '))), E.taboo ? h('p.warn', E.taboo > 1 ? 'Hunted: the Dawn Choir actively pursues people with this essence.' : 'Frowned on: the Dawn Choir watches people with this essence.') : null, h('p.dim.small', 'What an essence wakes in you can\'t be known until you absorb it.')]);
  };

  // ---------- training ----------
  S.train = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g;
    root.append(UI.header('Training', `${G.count(g, 'core')} Iron · ${G.count(g, 'bcore')} Bronze cores`));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    body.append(h('p.dim', 'Spend monster cores to train an ability. Each core teaches less than the last, for that ability, until you rest. Iron cores barely teach Bronze abilities; Bronze cores teach anything.'));
    body.append(h('div.warnbox', 'Mastery focus: choose seven abilities across your five essences and train each to 80%, with no more than 20% unseasoned growth. Trial still required. Core-fed growth is unseasoned (striped on the bar). Until you use the ability in real fights, it hits up to 25% softer, costs up to 20% more, and can misfire. Break through with unseasoned abilities and some of that hollowness follows you to the next rank. Progress earned in fights has no downside.'));
    for (const id of G.abilityIds(g)) {
      const a = C().abilities[id]; const s = G.abilityState(g, id);
      const un = G.unseasoned(g, id);
      body.append(h('div.row.trainrow', h('div.grow', h('b', { style: { color: UI.abColor(a) } }, a.name), un ? h('span.unseasoned-tag', ` unseasoned ${Math.round(un)}%`) : null, UI.trainBar(g, id)),
        s.train >= 100 ? h('span.ok', 'Ready') : h('div.row-r',
          UI.btn(`Iron +${G.coreValue(g, id, 'core')}%`, () => { const r = G.spendCore(g, id, 'core'); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad'); UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small ghost', G.count(g, 'core') ? {} : { disabled: true }),
          G.count(g, 'bcore') || g.rank >= 1 ? UI.btn(`Bronze +${G.coreValue(g, id, 'bcore')}%`, () => { const r = G.spendCore(g, id, 'bcore'); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad'); UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small', G.count(g, 'bcore') ? {} : { disabled: true }) : null)));
    }
    if (G.trialReady(g)) body.append(h('div.hint', UI.icon('star', 14), ` Everything is ready. Take the ${g.rank >= 1 ? 'Trial of the Unmaking' : 'Mirror Trial'} at the Magisterium Annex.`));
    root.append(body);
    root.append(h('div.pad', UI.btn('Back', () => UI.go('loc', { id: 'lamp' }), 'ghost wide')));
    root.append(UI.nav('explore'));
  };

  // ---------- character / essences ----------
  S.char = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g;
    root.append(UI.header(g.name, `${W.ORIGINS[g.origin].name} · ${G.rankName(g)}`));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    const a = G.attrs(g);
    body.append(h('div.attrs', ['might', 'swiftness', 'spirit', 'resilience'].map((k) => h('button.attr', { onclick: () => UI.statSheet(k) }, h('div.attr-v', String(a[k])), h('div.attr-k', k)))));
    body.append(h('button.linkbtn', { onclick: () => UI.statsGuide() }, UI.icon('book', 13), ' What do these do? Tap any stat.'));
    const ids = G.abilityIds(g);
    const avg = ids.length ? ids.reduce((s, id) => s + G.abilityState(g, id).train, 0) / ids.length : 0;
    if (g.rank < 0 || !G.fullSet(g)) body.append(h('div.rankbox', h('div.kv', h('span', g.rank < 0 ? 'Progress to Iron' : 'Your set is broken'), h('b', `${g.essences.length}/4 essences`)), UI.bar(g.essences.length, 4, 'xp'), h('p.dim.small', g.rank < 0 ? 'Absorb four essences. The fourth sparks a confluence, and the full set makes you Iron-rank.' : 'Absorb a fourth essence to make your set whole again. You can\'t break through until you do.')));
    if (G.strained(g)) body.append(h('div.warnbox', `Essence strain until day ${g.strainUntil}: -15% max health and mana.`));
    if ((g.rank === 0 || g.rank === 1) && G.fullSet(g)) body.append(h('div.rankbox', h('div.kv', h('span', `Progress to ${W.RANKS[g.rank + 1]}`), h('b', Math.round((G.masteryInfo(g).mature.length / G.masteryTarget) * 100) + '%')), UI.bar(G.masteryInfo(g).mature.length, G.masteryTarget, 'xp'), h('p.dim.small', G.trialReady(g) ? 'Ready! Visit the Magisterium Annex for your trial.' : 'Master seven focused abilities to 80%, covering all five essences. Select your focus in Training; then complete the Mirror Trial.')));
    for (const [i, e] of G.allEssences(g).entries()) {
      const E = G.ess(e.id);
      const col = UI.essColor(e.id);
      const card = h('div.ess', { style: { borderColor: col } });
      card.append(h('div.ess-head', h('button.ess-name', { style: { color: col }, onclick: () => UI.essenceSheet(e.id) }, E.name, i === 4 || (g.confluence && g.confluence === e) ? h('span.pill', 'confluence') : null), h('span.dim.small', `${e.stones.length}/4 stones`)));
      const slots = [E.starter].concat(e.stones.map((s) => E.byStone[s]));
      for (const id of slots) {
        const ab = C().abilities[id]; const s = G.abilityState(g, id);
        const un = G.unseasoned(g, id);
        card.append(h('button.abl', { onclick: () => UI.abilitySheet(id) }, h('div.abl-top', h('b', ab.name), h('span.dim.small', `${EB.K.RANK_NAMES[s.rank]} · ${Math.round(s.train)}%`)), h('div.dim.small', ab.summary), un ? h('div.unseasoned-tag', `Unseasoned ${Math.round(un)}%`) : null, UI.trainBar(g, id, true)));
      }
      for (let k = e.stones.length; k < 4; k++) card.append(h('div.slot-empty', 'Empty stone slot'));
      body.append(card);
    }
    if ((g.changes || []).length || (g.traits || []).length || g.scars) {
      body.append(h('h3', 'What you\'ve become'));
      for (const t of g.traits || []) { const T = W.TRAITS[t]; body.append(h('div.trait', h('b', T.name), h('p.small', T.text))); }
      if (g.scars) body.append(h('div.trait.neg', h('b', `Essence Scar${g.scars > 1 ? ' ×' + g.scars : ''}`), h('p.small', `-${g.scars} to every attribute. Permanent.`)));
      for (const c of g.changes || []) body.append(h('div.change', h('p', c.text), h('div.dim', `${W.RANKS[c.rank] || ''} breakthrough · day ${c.day}`)));
    }
    const ut = G.utilities(g);
    body.append(h('h3', 'Utilities'));
    for (const [k, U2] of Object.entries(W.UTILITIES)) {
      const src = ut[k];
      body.append(h('div.fam' + (src ? '' : '.util-off'), h('b', U2.name, src ? '' : ' (not yet)'), h('p.small', U2.text), h('p.dim.small', src ? 'From: ' + src.slice(0, 4).join(', ') + (src.length > 4 ? '…' : '') : 'Gained from Sage, Pathfinder, Wanderer or Wayfarer stones, and from Step, Wing, Compass or Hook.')));
    }
    body.append(h('p.dim.small', 'Essences and stones are semi-permanent. They can be removed through essence surgery at the Magisterium, at real cost.'));
    // stones
    body.append(h('h3', 'Awakening stones'));
    if (!g.stones.length) body.append(h('p.dim', 'None. Defeat elites, explore, or buy them at the Marrow Exchange.'));
    for (const s of g.stones) {
      const sd = s.key === 'volatile' ? { name: 'Volatile Stone', theme: 'Becomes a random stone when absorbed.', rarity: 'rare' } : G.stoneDef(s.key);
      body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', { style: { color: UI.RAR[sd.rarity] } }, sd.name), h('div.dim.small', sd.theme)), h('div.row-r', UI.btn('Absorb', () => UI.absorbStoneSheet(s.key, s.uid), 'small'))));
    }
    // essences in the bag
    if (g.essenceItems.length) {
      body.append(h('h3', 'Unabsorbed essences'));
      for (const eid of g.essenceItems) {
        const E = G.ess(eid);
        body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', { style: { color: UI.essColor(eid) } }, E.name), h('div.dim.small', E.identity)), h('div.row-r', UI.btn('Absorb', () => UI.absorbEssenceFlow(eid), 'small'))));
      }
    }
    if (g.party && W.ALLIES[g.party]) body.append(h('h3', 'Companion'), h('div.fam', h('div', h('b', W.ALLIES[g.party].name), h('div.dim.small', W.ALLIES[g.party].blurb + ' Fights beside you, except in duels and trials.')), h('div.stances', UI.btn('Part ways', () => UI.confirm('Part ways?', `${W.ALLIES[g.party].name} will go home. You can't call them back in this act.`, 'Part ways', () => { g.flags['left_' + g.party] = true; g.party = null; UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }), 'small ghost'))));
    // familiars
    const fams = Object.keys(g.familiars);
    if (fams.length) {
      body.append(h('h3', 'Familiars'), h('p.dim.small', `Command capacity: ${G.commandCap(g)}. Stance decides how they act in a fight.`));
      for (const t of fams) {
        const T = EB.K.tpl(t); if (!T) continue;
        const f = g.familiars[t];
        body.append(h('div.fam', h('div', h('b', T.name), h('div.dim.small', T.notes || '')), h('div.stances', ['aggressive', 'guard', 'support', 'hold'].map((st) => UI.btn(st, () => { f.stance = st; UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small' + (f.stance === st ? ' on' : ' ghost'))))));
      }
    }
    root.append(body);
    root.append(UI.nav('char'));
  };
  // Absorbing a stone: you choose the essence, and find out what wakes when it wakes.
  UI.readingSheet = function (key, absorb, uid) { return UI.absorbStoneSheet(key, uid); };
  UI.absorbStoneSheet = function (key, uid) {
    const g = UI.g;
    const sd = key === 'volatile' ? { name: 'Volatile Stone', theme: 'It could become anything. Even the stone doesn\'t know yet.' } : G.stoneDef(key);
    const rows = G.allEssences(g).map((e, i) => {
      const E = G.ess(e.id);
      const why = e.stones.length >= 4 ? 'Full (4 stones).' : key !== 'volatile' && e.stones.includes(key) ? 'Already holds this stone.' : null;
      return h('div.reading', h('div.kv', h('b', { style: { color: UI.essColor(e.id) } }, E.name + (g.confluence === e ? ' (confluence)' : '')), h('span.dim', `${e.stones.length}/4 stones`)),
        why ? h('p.dim.small', why) : h('div.row-r', UI.btn('Absorb here', () => UI.confirm(`Absorb into ${E.name}?`, `${sd.name} will wake something in your ${E.name} essence. What, exactly, is between you and the stone: you'll know when it happens.\n\nThis is semi-permanent. Removing it later means essence surgery, which destroys the stone and costs you training, marks and days.`, 'Absorb', () => doAbsorb(i)), 'small primary')));
    });
    const doAbsorb = (i) => {
      const r = G.absorbStone(g, uid, i);
      if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad');
      UI.closeSheet(); UI.save();
      UI.sheet('Awakened', [h('p.sysline', `[${G.stoneDef(r.key).name} absorbed.]`), h('p.sysline', `[New ability: ${r.ability.name}.]`), h('p', UI.richText(r.ability.summary)), h('p.flavor', '"' + r.ability.flavor + '"'), h('p.dim', EB.SYSLINES.train[Math.floor(Math.random() * 3)])]);
      UI.refresh();
    };
    UI.sheet(sd.name, [h('p.dim', sd.theme), h('p.small', 'Which essence takes it?'), ...rows]);
  };
  UI.absorbEssenceFlow = function (eid) {
    const g = UI.g; const E = G.ess(eid);
    if (g.loc !== 'town') return UI.toast('Absorbing an essence takes a day. Do it somewhere safe, in the city.', 'bad');
    if (g.essences.length >= 4) return UI.toast('You already hold four essences and a confluence.', 'bad');
    let text = `Absorbing ${E.name} takes a full day. It is semi-permanent: tearing it out later scars you for life.\n\n${E.identity}`;
    if (g.essences.length === 3) text += `\n\nThis is your fourth essence. Your essences will react and form a confluence. Nobody can tell you what it will be. The full set will make you ${g.rank < 0 ? 'Iron-rank' : 'whole again'}, and the confluence is permanent.`;
    if (E.taboo) text += '\n\nThe Dawn Choir will take an interest.';
    UI.confirm(`Absorb ${E.name}?`, text, 'Absorb', () => {
      const r = G.absorbEssence(g, eid);
      if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad');
      g.essenceItems.splice(g.essenceItems.indexOf(eid), 1);
      UI.save();
      const lines = [h('p.sysline', `[A day passes. Essence absorbed: ${E.name}.]`), h('p.sysline', `[New ability: ${r.ability.name}.]`)];
      if (r.confluence) lines.push(h('p.sysline', `[Confluence! Your four essences react and produce: ${G.ess(r.confluence).name}.]`), h('p.sysline', `[New ability: ${C().abilities[G.ess(r.confluence).starter].name}.]`));
      if (r.iron) {
        lines.push(h('h4', 'Iron-rank'), h('p.sysline', '[Full set. Your essences lock together like a key turning.]'), h('p.sysline', `[You are Iron-rank. +${r.iron.gain} to every attribute.]`), h('p', r.iron.change.text), h('div.trait', h('b', r.iron.trait.name), h('p.small', r.iron.trait.text)));
      }
      UI.sheet(r.iron ? 'Breakthrough: Iron' : 'Essence absorbed', lines);
      UI.refresh();
    });
  };

  // ---------- bag ----------
  S.bag = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g;
    root.append(UI.header('Bag'));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    body.append(h('h3', 'Equipped'));
    for (const slot of ['weapon', 'armor', 'charm']) {
      const it = W.ITEMS[g.gear[slot]];
      body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('span.dim.small', slot), h('div', h('b', it ? it.name : 'Nothing')), it ? h('div.dim.small', it.desc) : null), it ? h('div.row-r', UI.btn('Remove', () => { G.give(g, g.gear[slot], 1); g.gear[slot] = null; UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small ghost')) : null));
    }
    body.append(h('h3', 'Items'));
    const ids = Object.keys(g.inv).filter((id) => W.ITEMS[id]);
    if (!ids.length) body.append(h('p.dim', 'Empty. Suspiciously so.'));
    for (const id of ids) {
      const it = W.ITEMS[id];
      const eq = ['weapon', 'armor', 'charm'].includes(it.kind);
      body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', it.name), h('span.dim', ` ×${g.inv[id]}`), h('div.dim.small', it.desc)), h('div.row-r',
        eq ? UI.btn('Equip', () => { if (g.gear[it.kind]) G.give(g, g.gear[it.kind], 1); g.gear[it.kind] = id; G.give(g, id, -1); UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small') : null,
        id === 'draught' ? UI.btn('Drink', () => { if (g.hp >= G.maxHp(g)) return UI.toast('You\'re already at full health.'); G.give(g, id, -1); g.hp = Math.min(G.maxHp(g), g.hp + Math.round(G.maxHp(g) * 0.35)); UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small ghost') : null,
        id === 'tonic' ? UI.btn('Drink', () => { G.give(g, id, -1); g.mana = Math.min(G.maxMana(g), g.mana + Math.round(G.maxMana(g) * 0.4)); UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, 'small ghost') : null)));
    }
    root.append(body);
    root.append(UI.nav('bag'));
  };

  // ---------- journal ----------
  S.journal = function (root, p) {
    const g = UI.g;
    const tab = p.tab || 'quests';
    root.append(UI.header('Journal'));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    body.append(h('div.tabs', ['quests', 'path', 'factions', 'people', 'notes'].map((t) => UI.btn(t, () => UI.go('journal', { tab: t }), 'tab' + (t === tab ? ' on' : '')))));
    if (tab === 'quests') {
      const ids = Object.keys(g.quests).sort((a, b) => (g.quests[a].done - g.quests[b].done) || (W.QUESTS[b].main ? 1 : 0) - (W.QUESTS[a].main ? 1 : 0));
      for (const id of ids) {
        const Q = W.QUESTS[id]; const q = g.quests[id];
        body.append(h('div.qline' + (q.done ? '.done' : ''), h('b', Q.name, Q.main ? h('span.pill', 'main') : null), h('div.dim', q.done ? 'Done.' : Q.stages[q.stage])));
      }
    } else if (tab === 'path') {
      body.append(...UI.pathPanel());
      if (G.boonReady(g)) body.append(UI.btn(G.pathDef(g).boon.name, () => UI.useBoon(), 'wide'));
      const sd = G.societyDef(g); const nx = G.societyNext(g);
      body.append(h('h3', 'Lantern Society'), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Rank'), h('b', sd.name)), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Contracts done'), h('b', String(G.contractsDone(g)))), nx ? h('p.dim.small', `Next: ${nx.name} (${nx.contracts} contracts, ${W.RANKS[nx.reqRank]}-rank${nx.assess ? ', assessment' : ''}).`) : null);
      const act = G.society(g).active; if (act.length) body.append(h('h4', 'Board work'), ...act.map((t) => h('div.qline', h('b', t.title), h('div.dim', `${t.have}/${t.need} fights at ${W.MAP[t.node].name}`))));
    } else if (tab === 'factions') {
      for (const [f, F] of Object.entries(W.FACTIONS)) {
        if (F.hidden && !g.flags.slice_done) continue;
        const v = g.standing[f] || 0;
        body.append(h('div.fac', h('div.kv', h('b', { style: { color: F.color } }, F.name), h('span', G.tier(v) + ` (${v})`)), h('div.facbar', h('div.facfill', { style: { left: v < 0 ? (50 + v / 2) + '%' : '50%', width: Math.abs(v) / 2 + '%', background: v < 0 ? '#c4473a' : F.color } })), h('p.dim.small', F.blurb)));
      }
      body.append(h('div.fac', h('div.kv', h('b', 'Suspicion'), h('span', String(g.suspicion))), UI.bar(g.suspicion, 100, 'sus'), h('p.dim.small', 'How closely the Dawn Choir and the watch are looking at you.')));
    } else if (tab === 'people') {
      for (const [n, N] of Object.entries(W.NPCS)) {
        if (N.system || N.narrator || n === 'you') continue;
        const s = g.npc[n];
        if (!s || (s.aff === 0 && s.trust === 0 && !['maud', 'jory'].includes(n))) continue;
        body.append(h('div.person', h('b', { style: { color: N.color } }, N.name), h('p.dim.small', N.blurb), h('div.kv.small', h('span', 'Affinity'), h('span', String(s.aff))), h('div.kv.small', h('span', 'Trust'), h('span', String(s.trust)))));
      }
    } else {
      for (const j of g.journal) body.append(h('div.note', h('span.dim.small', `Day ${j.day} `), j.msg));
    }
    root.append(body);
    root.append(UI.nav('journal'));
  };

  // ---------- settings ----------
  S.settings = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g;
    root.append(UI.header('Menu'));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    if (g.hardcore) body.append(h('h3', 'Hardcore'), h('p.dim', 'One life, one save. It saves itself; there are no slots, rewinds or save codes.'));
    else body.append(h('h3', 'Save slots'));
    for (const n of g.hardcore ? [] : [1, 2, 3]) {
      const info = G.slotInfo(n);
      body.append(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', `Slot ${n}`), h('div.dim.small', info ? UI.saveLine(info) : 'Empty')), h('div.row-r',
        UI.btn('Save', () => { const go = () => { G.saveSlot(g, n); UI.toast(`Saved to slot ${n}.`, 'good'); UI.refresh(); }; info ? UI.confirm(`Overwrite slot ${n}?`, UI.saveLine(info), 'Overwrite', go) : go(); }, 'small'),
        info ? UI.btn('Load', () => UI.confirm(`Load slot ${n}?`, 'Unsaved progress since your last autosave will be lost.', 'Load', () => { UI.g = G.loadSlot(n); UI.save(); UI.resume(); }), 'small ghost') : null)));
    }
    if (!g.hardcore) body.append(UI.btn('Autosave history', () => UI.loadSheet(true), 'wide ghost'), h('h3', 'Backup'));
    if (!g.hardcore) body.append(UI.btn('Copy save code', () => { const code = G.exportCode(g); UI.sheet('Save code', [h('p.dim', 'Keep this somewhere safe. Paste it into "Import save code" on any device.'), h('textarea.code', { readonly: true }, code)]); try { navigator.clipboard.writeText(code); UI.toast('Copied to clipboard.', 'good'); } catch (e) {} }, 'wide'));
    body.append(UI.btn('Import save code', () => UI.importSheet(), 'wide ghost'));
    body.append(UI.btn('Title screen', () => { UI.save(); UI.go('title'); }, 'wide ghost'));
    body.append(h('div.stats', h('p.dim.small', `Day ${g.day}. Fights: ${g.stats.fights}, won ${g.stats.wins}.`), h('p.dim.small', `Essencebound. ${Object.keys(EB.content.essences).length} essences, ${Object.keys(EB.content.stones).length} stones, ${Object.keys(EB.content.abilities).length.toLocaleString()} abilities.`)));
    root.append(body);
    root.append(UI.nav('settings'));
  };

  // ---------- the Verge ----------
  S.map = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g; g.loc = 'verge';
    const cur = W.MAP[g.node];
    root.append(UI.header(cur.region === 'salt' ? 'The Saltgrave' : 'The Verge', cur.name));
    const ns = 'http://www.w3.org/2000/svg';
    const svg = document.createElementNS(ns, 'svg'); svg.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 100 100'); svg.setAttribute('class', 'map');
    const mk = (tag, at) => { const e = document.createElementNS(ns, tag); for (const k in at) e.setAttribute(k, at[k]); return e; };
    const drawn = {};
    const region = cur.region || 'verge';
    g.visited = g.visited || {}; g.visited[g.node] = true;
    const nodes = Object.entries(W.MAP).filter(([, n]) => (n.region || 'verge') === region);
    const trav = G.hasUtil(g, 'traverse');
    const shortcuts = trav ? W.SHORTCUTS.filter((s) => (W.MAP[s.a].region || 'verge') === region) : [];
    // quest markers; objectives in the other region point at the crossing
    const markers = G.questMarkers(g).map((m) => { const r = W.MAP[m.node].region || 'verge'; return Object.assign(m, { at: r === region ? m.node : region === 'verge' ? (g.flags.saltgrave_open ? 'gate' : null) : 'causeway' }); }).filter((m) => m.at);
    const shortOf = (id) => shortcuts.find((s) => (s.a === g.node && s.b === id) || (s.b === g.node && s.a === id));
    for (const s of shortcuts) { const m1 = W.MAP[s.a], m2 = W.MAP[s.b]; svg.append(mk('line', { x1: m1.x, y1: m1.y, x2: m2.x, y2: m2.y, class: 'map-path short' })); }
    for (const [id, n] of nodes) for (const l of n.links) { const k = [id, l].sort().join(); if (drawn[k]) continue; drawn[k] = 1; const m = W.MAP[l]; svg.append(mk('line', { x1: n.x, y1: n.y, x2: m.x, y2: m.y, class: 'map-path' })); }
    for (const [id, n] of nodes) {
      const linked = cur.links.includes(id) || !!shortOf(id);
      const gEl = mk('g', { class: 'map-node' + (id === g.node ? ' here' : linked ? ' near' : '') + (n.dungeon ? ' hollow' : '') });
      gEl.append(mk('circle', { cx: n.x, cy: n.y, r: id === g.node ? 4.2 : 3.2 }));
      const t = n.dungeon ? mk('text', { x: n.x + 5, y: n.y + 1.2, 'text-anchor': 'start' }) : mk('text', { x: n.x, y: n.y + (n.y < 15 ? 9 : -6), 'text-anchor': 'middle' }); t.textContent = n.name; gEl.append(t);
      const pins = markers.filter((m) => m.at === id);
      if (pins.length) { gEl.append(mk('circle', { cx: n.x + 3.6, cy: n.y - 3.6, r: 2, class: 'qpin' })); const tq = mk('text', { x: n.x + 3.6, y: n.y - 2.6, 'text-anchor': 'middle', class: 'qpin-t' }); tq.textContent = '!'; gEl.append(tq); }
      if (linked) gEl.addEventListener('click', () => { const sc = shortOf(id); g.node = id; UI.save(); if (sc && !cur.links.includes(id)) UI.toast('Shortcut: ' + sc.text, 'good'); UI.refresh(); });
      svg.append(gEl);
    }
    const body = h('div.pad');
    body.append(h('div.mapwrap', svg));
    if (markers.length) body.append(h('div.objectives', h('div.obj-title', UI.icon('star', 13), ' Objectives'), markers.map((m) => h('div.obj' + (m.at === g.node ? '.here' : ''), h('b', m.name), h('span.dim', ` — ${W.MAP[m.node].name}${m.at !== m.node ? ' (via ' + W.MAP[m.at].name + ')' : ''}${m.at === g.node ? ', here' : ''}`)))));
    body.append(h('div.node-card', h('div.card-title', cur.name, cur.danger ? h('span.danger', '◆'.repeat(cur.danger)) : null), h('p', cur.desc)));
    const acts = h('div.acts');
    const act = (label, icon, fn, sub, cls) => acts.append(h('button.act' + (cls ? '.' + cls : ''), { onclick: fn }, UI.icon(icon, 20), h('div', h('div.act-name', label), sub ? h('div.act-sub', sub) : null)));
    if (cur.pool || cur.events) act('Explore', 'eye', () => UI.explore(), cur.pool ? 'Look for trouble. Trouble usually obliges.' : 'Look around.');
    if (G.boonReady(g)) act(G.pathDef(g).boon.name, 'star', () => UI.useBoon(), G.pathDef(g).boon.text);
    if (cur.pool) act('Auto-explore', 'hourglass', () => UI.autoExploreSheet(), 'Let your essence fight on until something needs you.');
    if (cur.safe) act('Make camp', 'zz', () => { G.camp(g); UI.save(); UI.toast(`Day ${g.day}. You sleep under the open sky and wake rested.`, 'good'); UI.refresh(); }, 'Free. Full rest, a new day, core training resets.');
    if (g.node === 'shrine') act('Visit the shrine', 'star', () => UI.playScene('ev_shrine', null, () => UI.go('map')));
    if (g.node !== 'gate' && g.node !== 'causeway' && G.hasUtil(g, 'waypoint')) act('Waypoint: return to Calderhal', 'home', () => { g.loc = 'town'; UI.save(); UI.go('town'); }, 'Instant travel.');
    if (g.node === 'gate' || g.node === 'causeway') act('Return to Calderhal', 'home', () => { g.loc = 'town'; UI.save(); UI.go('town'); }, 'Safe streets. Relatively.');
    if (cur.dungeon) act('Descend into ' + W.DUNGEONS[cur.dungeon].name.replace(/^The /, 'the '), 'stairs', () => {
      if (cur.dungeon === 'cinder' && !G.questActive(g, 'act2_cloister') && !g.flags.prior_dead) return UI.toast('The water over the Cloister is boiling. Not yet. Talk to Maud first.', 'bad');
      g.loc = 'hollow'; g.dungeon = cur.dungeon; if (cur.dungeon === 'glass') G.setStage(g, 'main_glass', 2); else G.setStage(g, 'act2_cloister', 1); UI.save(); UI.go('hollow');
    }, W.DUNGEONS[cur.dungeon].name, 'hot');
    if (cur.cross && (!cur.cross.need || g.flags[cur.cross.need])) act(cur.cross.label, 'map', () => { g.node = cur.cross.to; if (cur.cross.to === 'causeway' && !g.flags.saw_salt) { g.flags.saw_salt = true; UI.save(); return UI.sheet('The Saltgrave', [h('p', 'The coast road runs out of road an hour past the Verge. After that it\'s marsh, then mud, then a grey sea that smells of iron and old fires. Somewhere out there is a lighthouse that shouldn\'t be lit.')], [UI.btn('Onward', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.refresh(); }, 'primary wide')]); } UI.save(); UI.refresh(); }, cur.cross.to === 'causeway' ? 'Bronze-rank danger.' : null);
    if (g.node === 'landing') act('Talk to Reeve Tarn', 'chat', () => UI.playScene(g.flags.met_tarn ? 'landing' : 'landing_first', null, () => UI.go('map')), W.NPCS.tarn.blurb);
    if (g.node === 'light' && G.questActive(g, 'act2_embers') && g.quests.act2_embers.stage === 3) act('Climb Saint Ivo\'s Light', 'stairs', () => UI.playScene('lighthouse', null, () => UI.go('map')), 'Someone is up there.', 'hot');
    if (g.node === 'scar' && G.questActive(g, 'main_glass')) G.setStage(g, 'main_glass', Math.max(1, g.quests.main_glass.stage));
    act('Drink a potion', 'cross', () => { if (UI.drinkBest()) UI.refresh(); }, `${G.count(g, 'draught')} draughts, ${G.count(g, 'elixir')} elixirs`);
    body.append(acts);
    body.append(h('p.dim.small.center', 'Tap a nearby place on the map to travel there.' + (trav ? ' Blue paths are shortcuts only you can take.' : '')));
    root.append(body);
    root.append(UI.nav('explore'));
  };
  UI.explore = function () {
    const g = UI.g;
    const r = G.encounterFor(g, g.node);
    UI.save();
    if (r.event) { g.flags['done_' + r.event] = true; return UI.playScene(r.event, null, () => UI.go('map')); }
    if (r.enc && G.hasUtil(g, 'scout')) return UI.scoutSheet(r.enc);
    if (r.enc) return UI.startCombat(r.enc, () => UI.go('map'));
    const rng = G.rng(g, g.stats.fights + 77);
    const found = rng() < 0.5 ? { cores: 1 } : { marks: 5 + Math.floor(rng() * 10) };
    UI.notes(G.apply(g, found));
    UI.sheet('Nothing stirs', [h('p', ['You poke around. The Verge keeps its secrets, mostly, but you find a little something.', 'A quiet stretch. Birds, ash, a boot with no owner. You pocket what\'s worth pocketing.', 'Nothing attacks you, which in the Verge counts as an event.'][Math.floor(rng() * 3)])]);
    UI.refresh();
  };

  // ---------- dungeons (Glasswater Hollow, Cinder Cloister) ----------
  UI.dstate = function (g, d) {
    g.dstate = g.dstate || {};
    if (!g.dstate.glass) g.dstate.glass = { room: g.hollowRoom || 0, cleared: g.hollowCleared || {} };
    return g.dstate[d] || (g.dstate[d] = { room: 0, cleared: {} });
  };
  S.hollow = function (root) {
    const g = UI.g; g.loc = 'hollow';
    const did = g.dungeon || 'glass'; const D = W.DUNGEONS[did]; const ds = UI.dstate(g, did);
    root.append(UI.header(D.name, `Depth ${ds.room + 1}/${D.rooms.length}`));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    if (g.party) body.append(h('p.dim.small', `${W.ALLIES[g.party].name} is with you.`));
    D.rooms.forEach((room, i) => {
      const cleared = ds.cleared[room.id];
      const here = i === ds.room;
      body.append(h('div.room' + (cleared ? '.cleared' : '') + (here ? '.here' : '') + (i > ds.room ? '.locked' : ''), h('div.room-n', { style: { color: D.color } }, String(i + 1)), h('div', h('b', i > ds.room + 1 ? '???' : room.name), here || cleared ? h('p.small.dim', room.text) : null)));
    });
    const room = D.rooms[ds.room];
    const acts = h('div.acts');
    if (g.flags[D.doneFlag]) acts.append(h('p.dim', 'It\'s quiet down here now. Nothing left but echoes.'));
    else if (room) acts.append(h('button.act.hot', { onclick: () => UI.enterRoom() }, UI.icon(room.enc ? 'sword' : 'eye', 20), h('div', h('div.act-name', ds.cleared[room.id] ? 'Continue deeper' : room.enc ? 'Press on' : 'Investigate'), h('div.act-sub', room.name))));
    acts.append(h('button.act', { onclick: () => { if (!UI.drinkBest()) return; UI.refresh(); } }, UI.icon('cross', 20), h('div', h('div.act-name', 'Drink a potion'), h('div.act-sub', `${G.count(g, 'draught')} draughts, ${G.count(g, 'elixir')} elixirs`))));
    acts.append(h('button.act', { onclick: () => { g.loc = 'verge'; g.node = D.exit; UI.save(); UI.go('map'); } }, UI.icon('flee', 20), h('div', h('div.act-name', 'Climb back out'), h('div.act-sub', 'Progress is kept.'))));
    body.append(acts);
    root.append(body);
    root.append(UI.nav('explore'));
  };
  UI.drinkBest = function () {
    const g = UI.g;
    if (g.hp >= G.maxHp(g)) { UI.toast('Already at full health.'); return false; }
    const id = G.count(g, 'elixir') && g.hp < G.maxHp(g) * 0.5 ? 'elixir' : G.count(g, 'draught') ? 'draught' : G.count(g, 'elixir') ? 'elixir' : null;
    if (!id) { UI.toast('No potions.', 'bad'); return false; }
    G.give(g, id, -1); g.hp = Math.min(G.maxHp(g), g.hp + Math.round(G.maxHp(g) * (id === 'elixir' ? 0.6 : 0.35))); UI.save(); return true;
  };
  UI.enterRoom = function () {
    const g = UI.g;
    const did = g.dungeon || 'glass'; const D = W.DUNGEONS[did]; const ds = UI.dstate(g, did);
    const room = D.rooms[ds.room];
    const advance = () => { ds.cleared[room.id] = true; if (ds.room < D.rooms.length - 1) ds.room++; UI.save(); UI.go('hollow'); };
    if (ds.cleared[room.id]) return advance();
    if (room.event) return UI.playScene(room.event, null, advance);
    UI.startCombat(room.enc, (sum) => {
      if (!sum.win) return UI.go(sum.wakeAtLodge ? 'town' : 'hollow');
      if (room.enc === D.boss) { ds.cleared[room.id] = true; UI.save(); return UI.playScene(D.after, null, () => UI.go('map')); }
      advance();
    });
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, G = EB.G, W = EB.W;
  const C = () => EB.content;
  UI.stipendSheet = function (choices) {

    UI.sheet('The Society stipend', [h('p', 'Maud slides a lead box across the desk. Three raw essences turn inside it. "First contract bonus. Pick one. Don\'t make that face, it\'s a loan against your future usefulness."'),
      ...choices.map((eid) => { const E = G.ess(eid); return h('button.card.ess-pick', { style: { borderColor: UI.essColor(eid), width: '100%', marginTop: '8px' }, onclick: () => { g.essenceItems.push(eid); UI.save(); UI.closeSheet(); UI.toast(`Got: ${E.name} essence. Absorb it at the Drowned Lamp.`, 'good'); } },
        h('div.card-title', { style: { color: UI.essColor(eid) } }, E.name), h('p.small', E.identity)); })]);
  };
  UI.surgerySheet = function (where) {
    const g = UI.g;
    const body = [h('p.dim', where === 'magisterium' ? 'Hale rolls up his sleeves. "Tell me what you want gone, and I\'ll tell you what it will cost you. Not just in marks."' : 'Sixpence produces a velvet roll of very thin knives. "Stones only. I\'m not a butcher. Well. Not that kind."')];
    G.allEssences(g).forEach((e, i) => {
      const E = G.ess(e.id);
      body.push(h('h4', { style: { color: UI.essColor(e.id) } }, E.name + (g.confluence === e ? ' (confluence)' : '')));
      for (const key of e.stones) {
        const ab = C().abilities[E.byStone[key]];
        body.push(h('div.row', h('div', h('b', ab.name), h('div.dim.small', G.stoneDef(key).name)), h('div.row-r', UI.btn('Remove', () => {
          const p = G.stonePreview(g, i, key, where);
          UI.confirm(`Remove ${ab.name}?`, p.lines.join('\n\n'), 'Cut it out', () => {
            const r = G.extractStone(g, i, key, where); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad');
            UI.save(); UI.sheet('Extraction', [h('p.sysline', `[${r.lost.name} is gone.]`), h('p', 'It hurts in a way that has nothing to do with the knife. Your essence feels like a room with a picture taken off the wall.'), r.scarred ? h('div.trait.neg', h('b', 'Essence Scar'), h('p.small', 'The cut went wrong. -1 to every attribute, permanently.')) : null]); UI.refresh();
          });
        }, 'small danger-btn'))));
      }
      if (where === 'magisterium' && g.confluence !== e) {
        body.push(UI.btn(`Tear out the ${E.name} essence`, () => {
          const p = G.essencePreview(g, i);
          UI.confirm(`Remove ${E.name}?`, p.lines.join('\n\n'), 'Tear it out', () => {
            const r = G.extractEssence(g, i); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad');
            UI.save(); UI.sheet('Extraction', [h('p.sysline', `[${G.ess(r.removed).name} essence removed.]`), r.collapsed ? h('p.sysline', `[Confluence collapsed: ${G.ess(r.collapsed).name}.]`) : null, h('p', 'You wake three days later in a Magisterium cot with a hole in you that nothing will ever quite fill.'), h('div.trait.neg', h('b', 'Essence Scar'), h('p.small', '-1 to every attribute, permanently.'))]); UI.refresh();
          });
        }, 'small ghost danger-btn wide'));
      }
    });
    if (G.allEssences(g).every((e) => !e.stones.length) && where !== 'magisterium') body.push(h('p.dim', 'Nothing to cut.'));
    UI.sheet(where === 'magisterium' ? 'Essence surgery' : 'Back-alley extraction', body);
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, G = EB.G, W = EB.W;
  UI.scoutSheet = function (encId) {
    const g = UI.g; const E = W.ENCOUNTERS[encId];
    const counts = {}; for (const f of E.foes) { const n = (W.ENEMIES[f] || {}).name || f; counts[n] = (counts[n] || 0) + 1; }
    const foes = Object.entries(counts).map(([n, c]) => c > 1 ? `${c}× ${n}` : n).join(', ');
    const s = UI.sheet('You see them first', [h('p', E.intro), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Ahead'), h('b', foes)), E.elite ? h('p.warn.small', 'Elite fight.') : null, h('p.dim.small', 'Scout: they haven\'t noticed you.')],
      [UI.btn('Slip past', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.toast('You let them walk on by.', 'good'); UI.refresh(); }, 'ghost'),
       UI.btn('Ambush them', () => { UI.closeSheet(); g.flags.scoutAmbush = true; UI.startCombat(encId, () => UI.go('map')); }, 'primary')]);
    s.querySelector('.sheet-bg').onclick = null;
  };
  UI.waypointSheet = function () {
    const g = UI.g;
    const places = Object.keys(g.visited || {}).filter((id) => W.MAP[id] && (id !== 'causeway' || g.flags.saltgrave_open));
    UI.sheet('Waypoints', [h('p.dim', 'Where to?'),
      UI.btn('Verge Gate', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.toVerge(); }, 'wide'),
      ...places.filter((id) => id !== 'gate').map((id) => UI.btn(W.MAP[id].name + (W.MAP[id].region === 'salt' ? ' (Saltgrave)' : ''), () => { UI.closeSheet(); g.loc = 'verge'; g.node = id; UI.save(); UI.go('map'); }, 'wide'))]);
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, G = EB.G, W = EB.W;
  const C = () => EB.content;
  UI.saveLine = (i) => `${i.name}, ${i.rank}, day ${i.day}, ${i.where}${i.t ? ' · ' + new Date(i.t).toLocaleString([], { month: 'short', day: 'numeric', hour: 'numeric', minute: '2-digit' }) : ''}`;
  UI.loadSheet = function (autoOnly) {
    const rows = [];
    if (!autoOnly) for (const n of [1, 2, 3]) { const info = G.slotInfo(n); if (info) rows.push(UI.btn(`Slot ${n}: ${UI.saveLine(info)}`, () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.g = G.loadSlot(n); UI.save(); UI.resume(); }, 'wide')); }
    const auto = G.autoList();
    if (auto.length) rows.push(h('h4', 'Autosaves'));
    auto.forEach((a, i) => rows.push(UI.btn(`${UI.saveLine(Object.assign({}, a.info, { t: a.t }))}`, () => UI.confirm('Load this autosave?', 'Anything since then will be lost unless it\'s in a slot.', 'Load', () => { UI.g = JSON.parse(a.data); G._autoSig = null; UI.save(); UI.resume(); }), 'wide ghost')));
    if (!rows.length) rows.push(h('p.dim', 'No saves yet.'));
    UI.sheet(autoOnly ? 'Autosave history' : 'Load game', rows);
  };

  // ---------- auto-explore ----------
  UI.autoOpts = UI.autoOpts || { hp: 0.5, fights: 5, elites: true, potions: true };
  UI.autoExploreSheet = function () {
    const o = UI.autoOpts; const g0 = UI.g;
    const pick = (label, key, vals, fmt) => h('div.optrow', h('span.small', label), h('div.stances', vals.map((v) => UI.btn(fmt(v), () => { o[key] = v; UI.autoExploreSheet(); }, 'tiny' + (o[key] === v ? ' on' : ' ghost')))));
    UI.sheet('Auto-explore', [
      h('p.dim.small', 'Your essence fights on its own until one of these happens. Events always stop it so you can choose.'),
      g0.hardcore ? h('p.warn.small', 'Hardcore: a defeat during auto-explore is still permanent.') : null,
      pick('Stop below health', 'hp', [0.3, 0.5, 0.7], (v) => Math.round(v * 100) + '%'),
      pick('Stop after fights', 'fights', [3, 5, 10], (v) => String(v)),
      pick('Stop at elites', 'elites', [true, false], (v) => v ? 'Yes' : 'No'),
      pick('Drink potions first', 'potions', [true, false], (v) => v ? 'Yes' : 'No'),
    ], [UI.btn('Cancel', UI.closeSheet, 'ghost'), UI.btn('Go', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.runAutoExplore(); }, 'primary')]);
  };
  UI.runAutoExplore = function () {
    const g = UI.g; const o = UI.autoOpts;
    const res = { fights: 0, wins: 0, slipped: 0, loot: {}, training: {}, stop: null, event: null, elite: null };
    const scout = G.hasUtil(g, 'scout');
    for (let step = 0; step < 30 && res.fights < o.fights; step++) {
      if (g.hp < G.maxHp(g) * o.hp) {
        if (o.potions && (G.count(g, 'elixir') || G.count(g, 'draught'))) { UI.drinkBest(); if (g.hp >= G.maxHp(g) * o.hp) continue; }
        res.stop = `Health below ${Math.round(o.hp * 100)}%.`; break;
      }
      const r = G.encounterFor(g, g.node);
      if (r.event) { g.flags['done_' + r.event] = true; res.event = r.event; res.stop = 'Something here needs your attention.'; break; }
      if (r.find) { const fnd = Math.random() < 0.5 ? { cores: 1 } : { marks: 5 + Math.floor(Math.random() * 10) }; G.apply(g, fnd); for (const k in fnd) res.loot[k === 'cores' ? 'Iron Core' : 'marks'] = (res.loot[k === 'cores' ? 'Iron Core' : 'marks'] || 0) + fnd[k]; continue; }
      const E = W.ENCOUNTERS[r.enc];
      if (E.elite && o.elites) { if (scout) { res.slipped++; continue; } res.elite = r.enc; res.stop = `${E.name} found you.`; break; }
      const sum = G.autoFight(g, r.enc);
      res.fights++;
      for (const l of sum.loot) { const m = /^(\d+) (.+)$/.exec(l); const k = m ? m[2].replace(/Cores$/, 'Core') : l; res.loot[k] = (res.loot[k] || 0) + (m ? +m[1] : 1); }
      for (const t of sum.training) { const x = res.training[t.name] || (res.training[t.name] = { got: 0, sea: 0, now: 0 }); x.got += t.got || 0; x.sea += t.seasoned || 0; x.now = t.now; }
      if (sum.permadeath) { return UI.sheet('Fallen', [h('p.sysline', '[You were defeated.]'), h('p', 'There is no waking up at the Lodge this time.')], [UI.btn('Continue', () => UI.permadeath(), 'primary wide')]); }
      if (!sum.win) { res.stop = sum.fled ? 'The fight stalled and you pulled back.' : 'You were defeated.'; res.defeat = sum.wakeAtLodge; break; }
      res.wins++;
    }
    if (!res.stop) res.stop = `${res.fights} fights done.`;
    UI.save();
    const body = [h('p', res.stop), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Fights won'), h('b', `${res.wins}/${res.fights}`)), res.slipped ? h('div.kv', h('span', 'Elites avoided (Scout)'), h('b', String(res.slipped))) : null];
    const loot = Object.entries(res.loot);
    if (loot.length) body.push(h('h4', 'Loot'), h('div.chips', loot.map(([k, v]) => h('span.chip.good', /Core$/.test(k) ? `${v} ${k}${v > 1 ? 's' : ''}` : k === 'marks' ? `${v} marks` : v > 1 ? `${k} ×${v}` : k))));
    const tr = Object.entries(res.training);
    if (tr.length) body.push(h('h4', 'Training'), ...tr.map(([k, x]) => h('div.kv.small', h('span', k), h('b', `${x.got ? '+' + x.got + '% → ' + Math.round(x.now) + '%' : Math.round(x.now) + '%'}${x.sea ? ' · seasoned ' + x.sea : ''}`))));
    const acts = [];
    if (res.event) acts.push(UI.btn('See what it is', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.playScene(res.event, null, () => UI.go('map')); }, 'primary'));
    else if (res.elite) acts.push(UI.btn('Fight', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.startCombat(res.elite, () => UI.go('map')); }, 'primary'));
    else if (res.defeat) acts.push(UI.btn('Wake up', () => { UI.closeSheet(); g.loc = 'town'; UI.save(); UI.go('town'); }, 'primary'));
    else acts.push(UI.btn('Done', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.refresh(); }, 'primary'));
    const s = UI.sheet('Auto-explore', body.filter(Boolean), acts);
    if (res.event || res.elite || res.defeat) s.querySelector('.sheet-bg').onclick = null;
    if (!res.defeat) UI.refresh();
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, G = EB.G, W = EB.W;
  // ---------- stats explained, with the player's real numbers ----------
  UI.ATTR_INFO = {
    might: { what: 'Physical power.', does: (a) => [
      `Powers physical abilities and weapon attacks. Your base physical hit is about ${UI.hitOf(a.might)} before the ability's own multiplier.`,
      'Abilities that list Might in their scaling (most blades, fists, hammers and bows) get stronger with every point.'] },
    swiftness: { what: 'Speed and finesse.', does: (a) => [
      `Sets how often you act. With ${a.swiftness} Swiftness you take turns about ${UI.speedVs(a.swiftness)}% faster than someone with 10.`,
      'Breaks ties when two people would act at the same moment.',
      'Improves your chance to flee: +3% for every point you have over the fastest enemy.',
      'Powers agile abilities that scale with it (quick strikes, thrown weapons, many movement and skirmish abilities).'] },
    spirit: { what: 'Magical power and reserves.', does: (a) => [
      `Max mana: 30 + 4 per point = ${30 + 4 * a.spirit}.`,
      `Mana regenerates ${(3 + 0.45 * a.spirit).toFixed(1)} per turn in combat.`,
      `Powers spells, healing and most non-physical damage (fire, cold, holy, psychic and so on). Base magical hit about ${UI.hitOf(a.spirit)}.`] },
    resilience: { what: 'Toughness and stamina.', does: (a) => [
      `Max health: 40 + 5 per point = ${40 + 5 * a.resilience} (before gear and traits).`,
      `Max stamina: 30 + 4 per point = ${30 + 4 * a.resilience}. Stamina regenerates ${(3 + 0.5 * a.resilience).toFixed(1)} per turn.`,
      'Powers most shields and defensive abilities.'] },
  };
  UI.hitOf = (v) => Math.round((3 + 1.2 * v) * ((G.TUNE && G.TUNE.heroPow) || 1));
  UI.speedVs = (sw) => Math.round(((100 + 3 * sw) / 130 - 1) * 100);
  UI.attrBreakdown = function (g, k) {
    const out = [['Origin', g.attrs[k] || 0]];
    for (const e of G.allEssences(g)) { const v = (G.ess(e.id).affinity || {})[k]; if (v) out.push([`${G.ess(e.id).name} essence`, v]); }
    const r = W.RANK_ATTR[g.rank] || 0; if (r) out.push([`${G.rankName(g)} rank`, r]);
    if (g.scars) out.push(['Essence scars', -g.scars]);
    return out;
  };
  UI.statSheet = function (k) {
    const g = UI.g; const a = G.attrs(g); const I = UI.ATTR_INFO[k];
    UI.sheet(k[0].toUpperCase() + k.slice(1), [
      h('p', h('b', `${a[k]}. `), I.what),
      h('ul.statlist', I.does(a).map((t) => h('li', t))),
      h('h4', 'Where yours comes from'),
      ...UI.attrBreakdown(g, k).map(([n, v]) => h('div.kv.small', h('span', n), h('b', (v > 0 && n !== 'Origin' ? '+' : '') + v))),
      h('p.dim.small', 'Some traits, forms and buffs also raise an attribute by a percentage during a fight.'),
    ], [UI.btn('All stats explained', () => UI.statsGuide(), 'ghost')]);
  };
  UI.statsGuide = function () {
    const g = UI.g; const a = G.attrs(g);
    const sec = (title, lines) => [h('h4', title), h('ul.statlist', lines.map((t) => h('li', t)))];
    UI.sheet('How stats work', [
      h('p.small', 'You have four attributes. Each ability says which ones it scales with: a Might ability hits harder the more Might you have, and so on. Mixed abilities use a blend.'),
      ...['might', 'swiftness', 'spirit', 'resilience'].flatMap((k) => sec(`${k[0].toUpperCase() + k.slice(1)} (${a[k]})`, UI.ATTR_INFO[k].does(a))),
      ...sec('Where attributes come from', [
        'Your origin sets your starting attributes.',
        'Every essence you absorb adds its own attribute bonuses.',
        `Rank adds to every attribute: +${W.RANK_ATTR[0]} at Iron and +${W.RANK_ATTR[1]} at Bronze${W.RANK_ATTR[2] ? `, +${W.RANK_ATTR[2]} at Silver` : ''}.`,
        'Essence scars from removing an essence take 1 from every attribute, permanently.']),
      ...sec('Combat stats', [
        `Health (${G.maxHp(g)}): reach 0 and you're down. Gear and traits can raise it by a percentage.`,
        `Mana (${G.maxMana(g)}) pays for spells; stamina (${G.maxSta(g)}) pays for physical techniques. Both regenerate every turn.`,
        'Crit chance starts at 5%. A crit hits much harder. Gear, traits, blessings and some afflictions on the target (like Witnessed) add to it.',
        'Accuracy starts at 95%, minus the target\'s evasion. Jinxed lowers it further.',
        'Damage reduction cuts all damage you take (up to 80%). Armor and physical resistance cut physical damage. Elemental resistances cut their element.',
        'Affliction resistance gives a chance to shrug off afflictions. Your affliction potency cuts into enemies\' resistance. Bosses resist stuns, fear and sleep extra.',
        'Speed (from buffs like Hasted, or Slowed) stacks with Swiftness to decide turn order.']),
      ...sec('Out of combat', [
        'Social options come from your abilities\' social tags (like Track, Insight or Persuade), not your attributes.',
        'Seasoned abilities (trained in fights) work at full strength. Abilities trained only with cores are unseasoned: weaker, costlier and they can misfire.']),
    ]);
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, G = EB.G, K = EB.K, B = EB.B, W = EB.W, S = UI.screens;
  const C = () => EB.content;
  const SPEED = { normal: 520, fast: 220 };

  UI.startCombat = function (encId, onDone) {
    const g = UI.g;
    if (!g.flags.tut_combat) {
      g.flags.tut_combat = true;
      setTimeout(() => UI.sheet('How fights work', [
        h('p', 'Turn order runs along the top: everyone acts when their chip reaches the front. Quick actions bring your next turn sooner; slow ones push it back.'),
        h('p', 'Tap an ability (or Attack), then tap a glowing target. Enemies show what they intend to do next, under their names.'),
        h('p', 'Melee can\'t reach the enemy Rear row while their Vanguard stands. Tap any card or status for details. AUTO lets the essence fight for you.'),
      ], [UI.btn('Got it', UI.closeSheet, 'primary wide')]), 600);
    }
    const st = { queue: [], onDone, mode: 'idle', pending: null, busy: false, tab: 'abilities', auto: false, log: [], speed: UI.prefSpeed || 'normal' };
    st.b = G.makeBattle(g, encId, { onEvent: (e) => { st.queue.push(e); st.log.push(e); } });
    UI.battle = st;
    g.pendingFight = encId; UI.save();
    UI.go('combat');
    setTimeout(() => UI.combatLoop(), 350);
  };

  const N = (b, id) => (K.byId(b, id) || {}).name || '?';
  UI.eventText = function (b, e) {
    switch (e.k) {
      case 'use': return `${N(b, e.src)} uses ${e.name}${e.tgt && e.tgt !== e.src ? ' on ' + N(b, e.tgt) : ''}.`;
      case 'dmg': return e.pay ? `${N(b, e.tgt)} pays ${e.amt} health.` : `${N(b, e.tgt)} takes ${e.amt}${e.abs ? ` (+${e.abs} absorbed)` : ''} ${e.type}${e.crit ? ' (critical!)' : ''}${e.dot ? ' over time' : ''}.`;
      case 'heal': return e.amt > 0 ? `${N(b, e.tgt)} heals ${e.amt}.` : null;
      case 'shield': return `${N(b, e.tgt)} gains a ${e.amt} shield.`;
      case 'status': return e.add > 0 ? `${N(b, e.tgt)}: ${K.statusDef(e.id).name} ${e.stacks > 1 ? '×' + Math.round(e.stacks * 10) / 10 : ''}` : null;
      case 'miss': return `${N(b, e.src)} misses ${N(b, e.tgt)} (${e.why}).`;
      case 'resist': return `${N(b, e.tgt)} ${e.why === 'immune' ? 'is immune to' : e.why === 'warded' ? 'wards off' : 'resists'} ${K.statusDef(e.id).name}.`;
      case 'text': return e.msg;
      case 'skip': return null;
      case 'summon': return null;
      case 'death': return null;
      default: return null;
    }
  };

  // ---------- main loop ----------
  UI.combatLoop = async function () {
    const st = UI.battle; if (!st) return;
    if (st.busy) return; st.busy = true;
    const b = st.b;
    try {
      while (!b.over) {
        if (UI.screen !== 'combat') return;
        const r = B.stepOne(b);
        if (!r) break;
        if (r.awaitPlayer) {
          if (st.auto) { EB.AI.act(b, r.unit); await UI.flushEvents(st, r.unit); continue; }
          st.mode = 'choose'; st.actor = r.unit;
          UI.renderCombat(); await UI.flushEvents(st, null, true);
          st.busy = false; return;
        }
        await UI.flushEvents(st, r.unit);
      }
      UI.renderCombat(); await UI.flushEvents(st, null, true);
      await UI.sleep(450);
      UI.endCombat();
    } finally { st.busy = false; }
  };
  UI.sleep = (ms) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms));

  // Render, then animate queued events
