  UI.flushEvents = async function (st, actor, quick) {
    const evs = st.queue.splice(0);
    if (!evs.length) return;
    UI.renderCombat();
    const useEv = evs.find((e) => e.k === 'use');
    if (useEv) UI.banner(st.b, useEv);
    let i = 0;
    for (const e of evs) {
      const el = document.querySelector(`[data-uid="${e.tgt}"]`);
      const fl = UI.floatFor(e);
      if (el && fl) { UI.float(el, fl.t, fl.c, i * 110); i++; }
      if (e.k === 'text' && e.msg && e.cls !== 'dim') UI.ticker(e.msg, e.cls);
    }
    const sp = SPEED[st.speed] || 520;
    if (!quick) await UI.sleep(actor && !actor.isPlayer ? sp + Math.min(600, i * 70) : 260 + Math.min(500, i * 60));
  };
  UI.floatFor = function (e) {
    switch (e.k) {
      case 'dmg': return e.amt || e.abs ? { t: (e.crit ? '!' : '') + '-' + e.amt + (e.abs ? ` [${e.abs}]` : ''), c: e.crit ? 'crit' : e.dot ? 'dot' : e.pay ? 'pay' : 'dmg' } : null;
      case 'heal': return e.amt > 0 ? { t: '+' + e.amt, c: 'heal' } : null;
      case 'shield': return { t: '+' + e.amt + ' shield', c: 'shield' };
      case 'status': return e.add > 0 ? { t: K.statusDef(e.id).name + (e.add > 1 ? ' ' + Math.round(e.add) : ''), c: e.boon ? 'boon' : 'affl' } : null;
      case 'miss': return { t: e.why === 'missed' ? 'Miss' : e.why[0].toUpperCase() + e.why.slice(1), c: 'miss' };
      case 'resist': return { t: e.why === 'immune' ? 'Immune' : e.why === 'warded' ? 'Warded' : 'Resist', c: 'miss' };
      case 'delay': return { t: 'Delayed', c: 'affl' };
      case 'summon': return null;
      default: return null;
    }
  };
  UI.float = function (el, text, cls, delay) {
    setTimeout(() => {
      const f = h('div.float.' + cls, text);
      const r = el.getBoundingClientRect();
      f.style.left = (r.left + r.width / 2 + (Math.random() * 30 - 15)) + 'px';
      f.style.top = (r.top + window.scrollY + r.height * 0.35) + 'px';
      document.body.append(f);
      el.classList.add(cls === 'heal' || cls === 'shield' || cls === 'boon' ? 'pulse-good' : cls === 'miss' ? 'pulse-miss' : 'pulse-bad');
      setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('pulse-good', 'pulse-bad', 'pulse-miss'), 450);
      setTimeout(() => f.remove(), 1200);
    }, delay || 0);
  };
  UI.banner = function (b, e) {
    const el = document.getElementById('cbanner'); if (!el) return;
    const u = K.byId(b, e.src);
    el.textContent = `${u ? u.name : ''}: ${e.name}`;
    el.className = 'show ' + (u && u.side === 'ally' ? 'ally' : 'enemy');
    clearTimeout(UI._bt); UI._bt = setTimeout(() => { el.className = ''; }, 1300);
  };
  UI.ticker = function (msg, cls) {
    const el = document.getElementById('cticker'); if (!el) return;
    el.textContent = msg; el.className = cls || '';
  };

  // ---------- render ----------
  S.combat = function (root) { UI.renderCombat(); };
  UI.renderCombat = function () {
    const st = UI.battle; if (!st || UI.screen !== 'combat') return;
    const b = st.b; const root = UI.root();
    root.innerHTML = '';
    const hero = b.units.find((u) => u.isPlayer);
    // header
    root.append(h('div.c-head',
      h('div.c-title', b.enc.name),
      h('div.c-tools',
        h('button.icon-btn' + (st.auto ? '.on' : ''), { onclick: () => { st.auto = !st.auto; if (st.auto && st.mode === 'choose') { st.mode = 'idle'; EB.AI.act(b, hero); UI.combatLoop(); } else UI.renderCombat(); }, title: 'Auto' }, h('span.small', 'AUTO')),
        h('button.icon-btn', { onclick: () => { st.speed = st.speed === 'fast' ? 'normal' : 'fast'; UI.prefSpeed = st.speed; UI.renderCombat(); } }, h('span.small', st.speed === 'fast' ? '2×' : '1×')),
        h('button.icon-btn', { onclick: () => UI.logSheet() }, UI.icon('log')))));
    // timeline
    const fc = B.forecast(b, 10);
    root.append(h('div.timeline', fc.map((u, i) => h('div.tl-chip.' + u.side + (i === 0 && st.mode === 'choose' ? '.now' : ''), { title: u.name }, UI.initials(u)))));
    // field
    const field = h('div.field');
    const row = (side, rowName) => {
      const units = b.units.filter((u) => u.side === side && u.row === rowName && (u.alive || st.recentDead && st.recentDead[u.id]) && u.kind !== 'decoy' || u.side === side && u.row === rowName && u.alive && u.kind === 'decoy');
      return h('div.frow.' + side + '.' + rowName, h('div.frow-lbl', rowName === 'vanguard' ? 'Vanguard' : 'Rear'), h('div.frow-units', units.filter((u) => u.alive).map((u) => UI.unitCard(b, u))));
    };
    field.append(row('enemy', 'rear'));
    field.append(row('enemy', 'vanguard'));
    field.append(h('div.midline', h('span#cbanner'), b.zones.length ? h('div.zones', b.zones.map((z) => h('span.zone', z.name))) : null));
    field.append(row('ally', 'vanguard'));
    field.append(row('ally', 'rear'));
    root.append(field);
    root.append(h('div#cticker', ''));
    if (hero) {
      const sh = K.shieldTotal(hero);
      root.append(h('div.cres',
        h('div.cres-item.hp', h('span.cres-k', 'Health'), h('b', `${hero.hp}${sh ? ' +' + sh : ''} / ${K.maxHp(hero)}`), UI.bar(hero.hp, K.maxHp(hero), 'hp')),
        h('div.cres-item.mp', h('span.cres-k', 'Mana'), h('b', `${Math.round(hero.mana)} / ${hero.baseMana}`), UI.bar(hero.mana, hero.baseMana, 'mp')),
        h('div.cres-item.sp', h('span.cres-k', 'Stamina'), h('b', `${Math.round(hero.sta)} / ${hero.baseSta}`), UI.bar(hero.sta, hero.baseSta, 'sp'))));
    }
    // tray
    root.append(UI.tray(st, hero));
  };
  UI.initials = (u) => u.name.split(/\s+/).filter((w) => /^[A-Z]/.test(w)).map((w) => w[0]).join('').slice(0, 2) || u.name.slice(0, 2);

  const INTENT_ICON = { attack: 'sword', aoe: 'burst', heal: 'cross', buff: 'up', debuff: 'down', summon: 'star', defend: 'shield', move: 'move' };
  UI.unitCard = function (b, u) {
    const st = UI.battle;
    const valid = st.mode === 'target' && st.validIds && st.validIds.has(u.id);
    const cur = b.current === u && !b.over;
    const maxHp = K.maxHp(u); const sh = K.shieldTotal(u);
    const card = h('div.unit.' + u.side + (cur ? '.cur' : '') + (valid ? '.valid' : '') + (u.kind === 'decoy' ? '.decoy' : '') + (u.boss ? '.boss' : '') + (u.kind === 'familiar' || u.kind === 'conjuration' ? '.summon' : ''),
      { 'data-uid': u.id, onclick: () => UI.tapUnit(u) });
        if (u.side === 'enemy' && u.fid && ['wolf','shardling','ghoul','cinderknight','spawn','acolyte','matriarch','prior'].includes(u.fid)) {
      const group = (u.fid === 'matriarch' || u.fid === 'prior') ? 'bosses' : 'enemies';
      card.append(h('img.enemy-art', { src: 'assets/' + group + '/' + u.fid + '.webp', alt: '', loading: 'lazy', decoding: 'async', onerror: e => e.currentTarget.remove() }));
    }
    const nameRow = h('div.u-name', u.name);
    card.append(nameRow);
    if (u.side === 'enemy' && u.intent && b.intentsVisible !== false) {
      const it = u.flags.falseIntent ? { kind: 'defend', name: '???' } : u.intent;
      card.append(h('div.intent.' + it.kind, UI.icon(INTENT_ICON[it.kind] || 'eye', 12), h('span', it.name)));
    }
    const hpPct = Math.max(0, Math.min(100, (100 * u.hp) / maxHp));
    const shPct = Math.min(100 - hpPct, (100 * sh) / maxHp);
    card.append(h('div.u-hp', h('div.u-hpfill', { style: { width: hpPct + '%' } }), sh ? h('div.u-shfill', { style: { left: hpPct + '%', width: shPct + '%' } }) : null, h('span', u.flags.hideHealth && u.side === 'enemy' ? '??' : `${u.hp}${sh ? '+' + sh : ''}/${maxHp}`)));
    if (u.isPlayer || u.kind === 'familiar') card.append(h('div.u-res', h('div.u-rbar.mp', h('div', { style: { width: Math.min(100, 100 * u.mana / Math.max(1, u.baseMana)) + '%' } }), h('span', `${Math.round(u.mana)}`)), h('div.u-rbar.sp', h('div', { style: { width: Math.min(100, 100 * u.sta / Math.max(1, u.baseSta)) + '%' } }), h('span', `${Math.round(u.sta)}`))));
    const sts = Object.entries(u.statuses).filter(([id]) => !(u.flags.hiddenStatus && u.flags.hiddenStatus[id] && u.side === 'enemy'));
    if (sts.length || u.form) {
      const row = h('div.u-sts');
      if (u.form) row.append(h('span.st.form', u.form.name));
      for (const [id, s] of sts.slice(0, 8)) row.append(h('span.st.' + (K.isBoon(id) ? 'boon' : 'affl'), { onclick: (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); UI.statusSheet(id, s); } }, UI.stAbbr(id), s.stacks > 1 ? h('sub', String(Math.round(s.stacks))) : null));
      if (sts.length > 8) row.append(h('span.st', '+' + (sts.length - 8)));
      card.append(row);
    }
    if (u.flags.flying) card.append(h('span.tag-fly', 'flying'));
    if (valid && st.pending) card.append(UI.previewBadge(b, st.actor, st.pending, u));
    return card;
  };
  UI.stAbbr = function (id) { const n = K.statusDef(id).name; return n.length <= 5 ? n : n.replace(/[aeiou]/g, '').slice(0, 4) || n.slice(0, 4); };

  UI.tapUnit = function (u) {
    const st = UI.battle; const b = st.b;
    if (st.mode === 'target' && st.validIds.has(u.id)) return UI.commit(u);
    UI.unitSheet(b, u);
  };
  UI.unitSheet = function (b, u) {
    const rows = [];
    rows.push(h('div.kv', h('span', 'Health'), h('b', `${u.hp}/${K.maxHp(u)}${K.shieldTotal(u) ? ` (+${K.shieldTotal(u)} shield)` : ''}`)));
    if (u.isPlayer || u.kind === 'familiar') rows.push(h('div.kv', h('span', 'Mana / Stamina'), h('b', `${u.mana} / ${u.sta}`)));
    rows.push(h('div.kv', h('span', 'Row'), h('b', u.row + (u.flags.flying ? ', flying' : ''))));
    rows.push(h('div.kv', h('span', 'Attributes'), h('b', K.ATTRS.map((k) => Math.round(K.attr(u, k))).join(' / '))));
    if (u.intent && u.side === 'enemy') rows.push(h('div.kv', h('span', 'Intends'), h('b', u.flags.falseIntent ? '???' : u.intent.name + (u.intent.target ? ' → ' + N(b, u.intent.target) : ''))), u.intent.desc && !u.flags.falseIntent ? h('p.small.dim', u.intent.desc) : null);
    if (u.form) rows.push(h('div.kv', h('span', 'Form'), h('b', u.form.name)));
    const sts = Object.entries(u.statuses);
    if (sts.length) { rows.push(h('h4', 'Statuses')); for (const [id, s] of sts) rows.push(h('button.strow', { onclick: () => UI.statusSheet(id, s) }, h('span.st.' + (K.isBoon(id) ? 'boon' : 'affl'), K.statusDef(id).name), h('span.small', ` ×${Math.round(s.stacks * 10) / 10}${typeof s.dur === 'number' ? `, ${s.dur}t` : ''}`))); }
    const mods = u.mods.filter((m) => m.tag !== 'gear' && m.tag !== 'exhaustion');
    if (mods.length) { rows.push(h('h4', 'Effects')); rows.push(h('p.small', mods.slice(0, 12).map((m) => `${m.stat.replace(/_/g, ' ')} ${m.amt > 0 ? '+' : ''}${Math.round(m.amt * 100)}%`).join(' · '))); }
    if (u.side === 'enemy' && u.revealed) rows.push(h('p.small.dim', 'Revealed: ' + Object.keys(u.revealed).join(', ')));
    if (u.side === 'enemy' && u.abilities.length && (u.revealed && (u.revealed.enemy_abilities || u.revealed.weaknesses))) rows.push(h('p.small', 'Abilities: ' + u.abilities.map((s) => s.def.name).join(', ')));
    UI.sheet(u.name, rows);
  };

  // ---------- action tray ----------
  UI.tray = function (st, hero) {
    const b = st.b;
    const tray = h('div.tray');
    if (b.over) { tray.append(h('div.tray-msg', b.winner === 'ally' ? 'Victory!' : b.winner === 'fled' ? 'You got away.' : 'Defeat...')); return tray; }
    if (st.mode === 'target') {
      tray.append(h('div.tray-msg', h('b', st.pending.label), ': choose a target'));
      tray.append(UI.btn('Cancel', () => { st.mode = 'choose'; st.pending = null; UI.renderCombat(); }, 'ghost wide'));
      return tray;
    }
    if (st.mode !== 'choose') { tray.append(h('div.tray-msg.dim', st.auto ? 'Auto-battle is on.' : 'Enemy turn...')); return tray; }
    const tabs = h('div.tabs.tray-tabs', ['abilities', 'actions', 'items', 'familiars'].map((t) => UI.btn(t === 'abilities' ? 'Abilities' : t === 'actions' ? 'Actions' : t === 'items' ? 'Items' : 'Familiars', () => { st.tab = t; UI.renderCombat(); }, 'tab' + (st.tab === t ? ' on' : ''))));
    tray.append(tabs);
    const grid = h('div.tray-grid');
    if (st.tab === 'abilities') {
      const fav = (UI.g && UI.g.favorites) || {};
      const order = new Map(hero.abilities.map((s, i) => [s, i]));
      const slots = hero.abilities.filter((s) => !s.def.passive && s.def.type !== 'familiar').sort((x, y) => {
        const fx = fav[x.id] ? 0 : 1, fy = fav[y.id] ? 0 : 1; if (fx !== fy) return fx - fy;
        const ux = B.blocked(b, hero, x) && !x.on ? 1 : 0, uy = B.blocked(b, hero, y) && !y.on ? 1 : 0; if (ux !== uy) return ux - uy;
        return order.get(x) - order.get(y);
      });
      const wd0 = B.weaponDef(hero);
      grid.append(UI.actBtn('Attack', wd0.name, 'sword', () => UI.choose({ def: wd0, basic: 'attack' })));
      for (const slot of slots) {
        const d = slot.def;
        const why = B.blocked(b, hero, slot);
        const cost = B.cost(hero, d);
        const costEl = h('span.cost', cost.mana ? h('i.mp', cost.mana) : null, cost.stamina ? h('i.sp', cost.stamina) : null, cost.health ? h('i.hpc', cost.health + '%') : null);
        const col = UI.abColor(C().abilities[slot.id] || { essence: (hero.essences || [])[0] });
        const card = h('button.ab' + (why && !slot.on ? '.off' : '') + (slot.on ? '.toggled' : '') + (slot.form ? '.formab' : ''), { style: { borderLeftColor: slot.form ? '#e8a33d' : col }, onclick: () => { if (why && !slot.on) return UI.toast(why, 'bad'); UI.choose(slot); } },
          h('div.ab-top', h('b', fav[slot.id] ? '★ ' : '', d.name), costEl),
          h('div.ab-meta', h('span', slot.on ? 'ON — tap to end' : why && !slot.on ? why : (d.tempo || 'standard') + (slot.unseasoned ? ' · unseasoned' : '')), slot.cd > 0 ? h('span.cd', slot.cd) : null),
          h('span.ab-i', { onclick: (e) => { e.stopPropagation(); if (C().abilities[slot.id]) UI.abilitySheet(slot.id); else UI.sheet(d.name, [h('p', d.summary || JSON.stringify(d.effects).slice(0, 200))]); } }, UI.icon('info', 14)));
        grid.append(card);
      }
    } else if (st.tab === 'actions') {
      const wd = B.weaponDef(hero);
      grid.append(UI.actBtn('Attack', wd.name + (wd.tempo !== 'standard' ? ` · ${wd.tempo}` : ''), 'sword', () => UI.choose({ def: wd, basic: 'attack' })));
      grid.append(UI.actBtn('Defend', 'Guard 2, -25% damage, recover stamina', 'shield', () => UI.doAct({ type: 'defend' })));
      grid.append(UI.actBtn('Move', `To ${hero.row === 'vanguard' ? 'Rear' : 'Vanguard'}${B.canMove(b, hero) ? '' : ' (blocked)'}`, 'move', () => B.canMove(b, hero) ? UI.doAct({ type: 'swap' }) : UI.toast('You can\'t move right now.', 'bad')));
      grid.append(UI.actBtn('Wait', 'Pass quickly', 'hourglass', () => UI.doAct({ type: 'wait' })));
      grid.append(UI.actBtn('Flee', b.env.noFlee ? 'Not this fight' : 'Try to escape', 'flee', () => b.env.noFlee ? UI.toast('There\'s no running from this one.', 'bad') : UI.confirm('Flee?', 'If it works, you escape with nothing. If not, you lose a turn.', 'Run', () => UI.doAct({ type: 'flee' }))));
    } else if (st.tab === 'items') {
      const g = UI.g;
      const usable = Object.keys(g.inv).filter((id) => W.ITEMS[id] && W.ITEMS[id].kind === 'consumable');
      if (!usable.length) grid.append(h('p.dim', 'No usable items.'));
      for (const id of usable) {
        const it = W.ITEMS[id];
        grid.append(UI.actBtn(`${it.name} ×${g.inv[id]}`, it.desc, 'bag', () => UI.choose({ def: Object.assign({ id, name: it.name, target: it.target, effects: it.effects, type: 'special_ability', cost: {}, tags: ['Item'] }), item: id })));
      }
    } else {
      const fams = b.units.filter((u) => u.alive && u.owner === hero.id && (u.kind === 'familiar' || u.kind === 'conjuration'));
      if (!fams.length) grid.append(h('p.dim', 'No familiars or summons with you.'));
      for (const f of fams) {
        const box = h('div.fambox', h('b', f.name), h('div.small.dim', f.kind === 'familiar' ? `Stance: ${f.stance}` : 'Summon'));
        if (f.kind === 'familiar') box.append(h('div.stances', ['aggressive', 'guard', 'support', 'hold'].map((s) => UI.btn(s, () => { f.stance = s; if (UI.g.familiars[f.template]) UI.g.familiars[f.template].stance = s; EB.AI.plan(b, f); UI.renderCombat(); }, 'tiny' + (f.stance === s ? ' on' : ' ghost')))));
        const ordered = hero.flags.commandedThisTurn === hero.turns;
        box.append(h('div.fam-acts', (f.abilities || []).map((s, i) => UI.btn(s.def.name + (s.cd > 0 ? ` (${s.cd})` : ''), () => {
          if (ordered) return UI.toast('You already gave an order this turn.', 'bad');
          if (s.cd > 0) return UI.toast('Not ready yet.', 'bad');
          if (B.needsTarget(s.def)) UI.choose({ def: s.def, order: { fam: f, idx: i } });
          else { B.command(b, hero, f, i, null); UI.toast(`${f.name} will use ${s.def.name}.`, 'good'); UI.renderCombat(); }
        }, 'tiny', ordered ? { disabled: true } : {}))));
        grid.append(box);
      }
      grid.append(h('p.small.dim', 'Ordering a familiar is free, once per turn. It acts on its own turn.'));
    }
    tray.append(grid);
    return tray;
  };
  UI.actBtn = (name, sub, icon, fn) => h('button.ab.basic', { onclick: fn }, h('div.ab-top', h('b', UI.icon(icon, 14), ' ', name)), h('div.ab-meta', h('span', sub)));

  // choose → (choice) → (target) → commit
  UI.choose = function (p) {
    const st = UI.battle; const b = st.b; const hero = st.actor;
    const def = p.def;
    st.pending = { label: def.name, slot: p.basic || p.item || p.order ? null : p, basic: p.basic, item: p.item, def, order: p.order };
    // abilities that make you pick an option
    let opts = null;
    K.mapEffects(def.effects || [], (e) => { if ((e.op === 'choose_one' || e.op === 'choose_vow') && !opts) opts = e.options; return e; });
    const go = () => {
      const needs = B.needsTarget(def) || p.basic === 'attack';
      if (!needs) return UI.commit(null);
      const valid = p.basic === 'attack' ? B.validTargets(b, hero, B.weaponDef(hero)) : B.validTargets(b, hero, def);
      if (!valid.length) return UI.toast('No valid targets.', 'bad');
      if (valid.length === 1 && (def.target === 'self' || def.target === 'ally_or_self' && valid[0] === hero)) return UI.commit(valid[0]);
      st.validIds = new Set(valid.map((u) => u.id));
      st.mode = 'target';
      UI.renderCombat();
    };
    if (opts && !p.order) {
      UI.sheet(def.name + ': choose', opts.map((o, i) => UI.btn(o.label || ('Option ' + (i + 1)), () => { UI.closeSheet(); st.pending.choice = i; go(); }, 'wide')));
      return;
    }
    go();
  };
  UI.commit = function (target, confirmed) {
    const st = UI.battle; const b = st.b; const hero = st.actor; const p = st.pending;
    if (!confirmed && !p.order) {
      const warn = UI.actionWarnings(b, hero, p, target);
      if (warn.length) return UI.sheet('Are you sure?', warn.map((w) => h('p', w)), [UI.btn('Back', () => { UI.closeSheet(); st.mode = 'choose'; st.pending = null; st.validIds = null; UI.renderCombat(); }, 'ghost'), UI.btn('Do it', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.commit(target, true); }, 'primary')]);
    }
    st.mode = 'idle'; st.validIds = null;
    if (p.order) { B.command(b, hero, p.order.fam, p.order.idx, target); st.mode = 'choose'; UI.toast(`${p.order.fam.name} will use ${p.def.name}.`, 'good'); return UI.renderCombat(); }
    let r;
    if (p.basic === 'attack') r = B.act(b, hero, { type: 'attack', target: target && target.id });
    else if (p.item) { G.give(UI.g, p.item, -1); r = B.useItem(b, hero, Object.assign({}, W.ITEMS[p.item], { id: p.item }), target); }
    else {
      const idx = hero.abilities.indexOf(p.slot);
      r = B.act(b, hero, { type: 'ability', slot: idx, target: target && target.id, choice: p.choice });
    }
    if (r && r.error) { UI.toast(r.error, 'bad'); st.mode = 'choose'; return UI.renderCombat(); }
    UI.afterPlayerAct();
  };
  UI.doAct = function (a) {
    const st = UI.battle; const r = B.act(st.b, st.actor, a);
    if (r && r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad');
    st.mode = 'idle';
    UI.afterPlayerAct();
  };
  UI.afterPlayerAct = async function () {
    const st = UI.battle;
    await UI.flushEvents(st, st.actor);
    UI.combatLoop();
  };

  UI.logSheet = function () {
    const st = UI.battle; const b = st.b;
    UI.sheet('Combat log', st.log.map((e) => UI.eventText(b, e)).filter(Boolean).slice(-80).reverse().map((l) => h('div.logline', l)));
  };

  // ---------- end ----------
  UI.endCombat = function () {
    const st = UI.battle; if (!st || st.ended) return; st.ended = true;
    const g = UI.g; const b = st.b;
    const sum = G.resolveBattle(g, b);
    g.pendingFight = null; g.pendingCombat = null;
    if (sum.permadeath) {
      UI.lastBattle = st;
      const card = UI.sheet('Fallen', [h('p.sysline', `[${b.enc.name}: you were defeated.]`), h('p', 'You feel the cold come up through the ground. This time, nobody carries you back to the Lodge.'), h('p.dim', 'Hardcore. This character is gone.')], [UI.btn('Continue', () => UI.permadeath(st), 'primary wide')]);
      card.querySelector('.sheet-bg').onclick = null; card.classList.add('result', 'lose');
      return;
    }
    UI.save();
    const enc = b.enc;
    const title = sum.win ? 'Victory' : sum.fled ? 'Escaped' : enc.duel || enc.trial ? 'Yielded' : 'Defeated';
    const body = [];
    if (sum.win) body.push(h('p.sysline', `[${enc.name}: defeated.]`));
    if (sum.loot.length) body.push(h('h4', 'Loot'), h('div.chips', sum.loot.map((l) => h('span.chip.good', l))));
    if (sum.training.length) {
      body.push(h('h4', 'Training'));
      for (const t of sum.training) body.push(h('div.trainline', h('div.kv.small', h('span', t.name), h('b', (t.got ? `+${t.got}% → ${Math.round(t.now)}%` : `${Math.round(t.now)}%`) + (t.seasoned ? ` · seasoned ${t.seasoned}` : ''))), UI.trainBar(g, t.id, true), t.seasoned && !t.left ? h('div.small', { style: { color: '#9be6ae' } }, 'Fully seasoned.') : null));
      if (G.trialReady(g)) body.push(h('p.sysline', '[Your chosen disciplines are ready. The Magisterium\'s trial awaits.]'));
    }
    for (const n of sum.notes) body.push(h('p.small', n));
    body.push(UI.btn('Fight summary', () => UI.fightSummary(st), 'small ghost wide'));
    if (sum.wakeAtLodge) body.push(h('p', EB.SYSLINES.defeat[Math.floor(Math.random() * EB.SYSLINES.defeat.length)]));
    const done = () => {
      const cb = st.onDone; UI.lastBattle = st; UI.battle = null;
      UI.closeSheet();
      if (sum.wakeAtLodge) { g.loc = 'town'; UI.save(); if (cb && (b.encId === 'matriarch' || b.encId === 'knight' || /hollow/.test(b.encId))) { g.loc = 'town'; } return UI.go('town'); }
      if (cb) cb(sum); else UI.resume();
    };
    st.showResult = () => {
      const card = UI.sheet(title, body, [UI.btn('Continue', done, 'primary wide')]);
      card.querySelector('.sheet-bg').onclick = null;
      card.classList.add('result', sum.win ? 'win' : 'lose');
      return card;
    };
    st.showResult();
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, K = EB.K, B = EB.B;
  UI.previewBadge = function (b, u, p, t) {
    const def = p.basic === 'attack' ? B.weaponDef(u) : p.def;
    const pv = B.preview(b, u, def, t);
    const lines = [];
    if (pv.dmg) lines.push(h('div.pv-main' + (pv.lethal ? '.lethal' : ''), `~${pv.dmg}${pv.lethal ? ' KO' : ''}`, pv.hit != null ? h('span', ` · ${Math.round(pv.hit * 100)}%`) : null));
    else if (pv.hit != null) lines.push(h('div.pv-main', `${Math.round(pv.hit * 100)}% hit`));
    if (pv.heal) lines.push(h('div.pv-main.heal', `+${pv.heal}`));
    if (pv.shield) lines.push(h('div.pv-main.shield', `+${pv.shield} shield`));
    for (const s of pv.sts.slice(0, 2)) lines.push(h('div.pv-st', `${K.statusDef(s.id).name}${s.stacks > 1 ? ' ' + s.stacks : ''}${s.chance < 0.995 ? ` ${Math.round(s.chance * 100)}%` : ''}`));
    for (const n of pv.notes.slice(0, 1)) lines.push(h('div.pv-st.warn', n));
    return h('div.preview', lines);
  };
  // Things worth a second thought before committing
  UI.actionWarnings = function (b, u, p, target) {
    const out = [];
    const def = p.basic === 'attack' ? B.weaponDef(u) : p.def;
    if (u.flags.oath && EB.OPS._oathViolation) {
      const tg = target ? [target] : B.defaultTargets(b, u, def);
      const hasDmg = JSON.stringify(def.effects || []).includes('"damage"');
      const saved = u.directThisAction; u.directThisAction = hasDmg && tg.some((t) => K.isEnemy(u, t)) ? 1 : 0;
      let why = null; try { why = EB.OPS._oathViolation(b, u, def, { targets: tg, hitTargets: tg.filter((t) => K.isEnemy(u, t)) }); } catch (e) {}
      u.directThisAction = saved;
      if (why) out.push(`This would break your oath (you'd have ${why}). You'd lose its bonus and suffer its penalty.`);
    }
    const c = B.cost(u, def);
    if (c.health && u.hp - K.maxHp(u) * c.health / 100 < K.maxHp(u) * 0.2) out.push(`This costs ${c.health}% of your health and would leave you below 20%.`);
    if ((def.cooldown || 0) >= 7 && !p.item) out.push(`${def.name} has a ${def.cooldown}-turn cooldown. You may not get it back this fight.`);
    if (p.item && (p.item === 'elixir') && u.hp > K.maxHp(u) * 0.7) out.push('You\'re above 70% health. Save the elixir?');
    return out;
  };
  // Who did what: damage by source and by ability, taken, healed
  UI.fightSummary = function (st) {
    const b = st.b; const hero = b.units.find((x) => x.isPlayer);
    const last = {}, bySrc = {}, byAb = {}, taken = {}, healed = {};
    const nm = (id) => (K.byId(b, id) || {}).name || '?';
    for (const e of st.log) {
      if (e.k === 'use') last[e.src] = e.name;
      if (e.k === 'dmg' && e.src && !e.pay) {
        const s = K.byId(b, e.src); if (!s) continue;
        const amt = e.amt + (e.abs || 0);
        if (s.side === 'ally') { bySrc[s.name] = (bySrc[s.name] || 0) + amt; if (s === hero) { const k = e.dot ? 'Over time' : last[e.src] || 'Attack'; byAb[k] = (byAb[k] || 0) + amt; } }
        else taken[s.name] = (taken[s.name] || 0) + amt;
      }
      if (e.k === 'heal' && e.amt > 0) { const s = K.byId(b, e.src); if (s && s.side === 'ally') healed[s.name] = (healed[s.name] || 0) + e.amt; }
    }
    const block = (title, obj, cls) => {
      const rows = Object.entries(obj).sort((a, c) => c[1] - a[1]);
      if (!rows.length) return null;
      const max = rows[0][1];
      return [h('h4', title), ...rows.slice(0, 8).map(([k, v]) => h('div.sumrow', h('div.kv.small', h('span', k), h('b', String(v))), h('div.sumbar.' + cls, { style: { width: Math.max(4, 100 * v / max) + '%' } })))];
    };
    const turns = b.units.find((x) => x.isPlayer).turns;
    UI.sheet('Fight summary', [h('p.dim.small', `${turns} of your turns.`), block('Your side\'s damage', bySrc, 'dmg'), block('Your damage by ability', byAb, 'abl'), block('Damage dealt to your side', taken, 'taken'), block('Healing', healed, 'heal'), UI.btn('Turn-by-turn log', () => UI.logSheet(), 'small ghost wide')].flat().filter(Boolean));
  };
  // Combat log grouped by turn
  UI.logSheet = function () {
    const st = UI.battle || UI.lastBattle; if (!st) return;
    const b = st.b; const rows = [];
    let group = null;
    for (const e of st.log) {
      if (e.k === 'turn') { group = { who: (K.byId(b, e.tgt) || {}).name, side: (K.byId(b, e.tgt) || {}).side, lines: [] }; rows.push(group); continue; }
      const t = UI.eventText(b, e); if (!t) continue;
      if (!group) { group = { who: 'Start', lines: [] }; rows.push(group); }
      group.lines.push(t);
    }
    const out = [];
    for (const r of rows.slice(-40).reverse()) { if (!r.lines.length) continue; out.push(h('div.loghead.' + (r.side || ''), r.who)); for (const l of r.lines) out.push(h('div.logline', l)); }
    UI.sheet('Combat log', out);
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;
// UI for gods, astral beings, the unsworn road, the Lantern Society's ranks, board work and hardcore.
(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  const UI = EB.UI, h = UI.h, G = EB.G, W = EB.W, S = UI.screens;

  const bar = (v, max, color) => h('div.pbar', h('i', { style: { width: Math.min(100, Math.round(100 * v / Math.max(1, max))) + '%', background: color || '#e8a33d' } }));
  const modText = (m) => Object.entries(m || {}).map(([k, v]) => `+${Math.round(v * 100)}% ${k.replace(/_/g, ' ')}`).join(', ') || 'None yet';
  const reqLine = (ok, text) => h('div.req' + (ok ? '.ok' : ''), ok ? '✓ ' : '○ ', text);

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- path panel (shared)
  UI.pathPanel = function (where) {
    const g = UI.g; const p = G.path(g); const P = W.PATHS[p.id];
    const out = [];
    out.push(h('div.path-card', { style: { borderColor: P.color } },
      h('div.card-title', { style: { color: P.color } }, P.name),
      h('div.dim.small', P.kind === 'god' ? 'Your god' : P.kind === 'astral' ? 'The astral being you venerate' : 'You follow no god'),
      h('div.kv', h('span', 'Rank'), h('b', G.pathRankName(g))),
      h('div.kv', h('span', P.pointName), h('b', String(p.pts))),
      h('div.kv.small', h('span', 'Blessing'), h('span', modText(P.mods[p.rank])))));
    const nx = G.pathNext(g);
    if (nx) {
      out.push(h('h4', `Next: ${nx.name}`));
      out.push(bar(p.pts, nx.need, P.color));
      out.push(reqLine(nx.ptsOk, `${nx.need} ${P.pointName} (${p.pts})`));
      if (nx.reqRank != null) out.push(reqLine(nx.rankOk, `${W.RANKS[nx.reqRank]}-rank`));
      if (nx.trial) out.push(reqLine(nx.trialDone, 'Pass the trial'));
      if (nx.blocked) out.push(h('p.warn.small', nx.blocked));
    } else out.push(h('p.dim', 'There is no higher rank on this path.'));
    if (p.rank >= 1) out.push(h('p.small', h('b', P.boon.name + ': '), P.boon.text, G.boonReady(g) ? '' : ' (Used today.)'));
    if (p.boon && p.boon.n > 0) out.push(h('p.small.good', `Active: ${p.boon.n} fight${p.boon.n > 1 ? 's' : ''} left.`));
    if (p.id === 'morrow' && p.ward === g.day) out.push(h('p.small.good', 'Morrow\'s grace is on you today.'));
    out.push(h('details.path-more', h('summary', 'Tenet, taboo and gift'), h('p.small', h('b', 'Tenet. '), P.tenet), h('p.small', h('b', 'Taboo. '), P.taboo), h('p.small', h('b', 'Gift. '), P.gift)));
    return out;
  };
  UI.useBoon = function () {
    const r = G.useBoon(UI.g); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad');
    UI.save(); UI.sheet(r.name, [h('p.sysline', `[${r.name}.]`), h('p', r.text)]); UI.refresh();
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- temples
  UI.TEMPLE_TEXT = {
    solenne: 'Candles, and the smell of clean linen. A choir is practising somewhere above, the same three bars over and over until they\'re right.',
    morrow: 'A long, low hall by the river, cool as a cellar. Lanterns hang from the beams, one for every soul ferried this year. There are a great many.',
    kesh: 'Green baize, brass rails, a roulette wheel the size of a cartwheel. Prayers here are said quietly, between hands.',
    vask: 'Antlers on every wall, dogs under every table, and an old woman in the corner sharpening something that doesn\'t need it.',
    loom: 'A great brass orrery fills the dome, its rings turning with no lamps lit. Silver threads hang from the rings to the floor. Some of them move.',
    eye: 'In the dark under the dome, painted eyes cover the walls. You could swear some of them are open.',
  };
  UI.templeLoc = (pid) => { const t = W.PATHS[pid].temple; return t === 'chapel' || t === 'orrery' ? t : 'temples'; };
  UI.templeHot = function (pid) {
    const g = UI.g; if (G.path(g).id !== pid) return false;
    const nx = G.pathNext(g); const d = G.templeDuty(g);
    return !!(nx && (nx.ready || nx.trialReady)) || !!(d && !d.done && d.have >= d.need);
  };

  S.temple = function (root, p) {
    const g = UI.g; const pid = p.id; const P = W.PATHS[pid]; const me = G.path(g);
    root.append(UI.header(P.templeName, P.name));
    const body = h('div.pad');
    body.append(h('p.loc-intro', UI.TEMPLE_TEXT[pid]));
    if (me.id !== pid) body.append(h('div.path-card', { style: { borderColor: P.color } }, h('div.card-title', { style: { color: P.color } }, P.name), h('p', P.blurb),
      h('p.small', h('b', 'Tenet. '), P.tenet), h('p.small', h('b', 'Taboo. '), P.taboo), h('p.small', h('b', 'Gift. '), P.gift)));
    const acts = h('div.acts');
    const act = (label, icon, fn, sub, cls) => acts.append(h('button.act' + (cls ? '.' + cls : ''), { onclick: fn }, UI.icon(icon, 20), h('div', h('div.act-name', label), sub ? h('div.act-sub', sub) : null)));
    if (me.id === pid) {
      body.append(...UI.pathPanel());
      const nx = G.pathNext(g);
      if (nx && nx.ready) act(`Be raised to ${nx.name}`, 'up', () => UI.raise(), 'You have done what was asked.', 'hot');
      if (nx && nx.trialReady) act(`Trial: ${nx.name}`, 'sword', () => UI.confirm(`Trial: ${nx.name}`, `${P.trial[nx.rank]}\n\nTrials are not lethal. If you lose, you can try again.`, 'Begin', () => UI.startCombat(`ptrial_${pid}_${nx.rank}`, () => UI.go('temple', p))), 'Prove yourself.', 'hot');
      if (G.boonReady(g)) act(P.boon.name, 'star', () => UI.useBoon(), P.boon.text);
      const d = G.templeDuty(g);
      if (d) {
        if (d.done) act('Today\'s duty: done', 'log', () => UI.toast('Come back tomorrow.'), d.title);
        else if (d.have >= d.need) act('Report your duty', 'log', () => { const r = G.finishDuty(g); UI.save(); UI.toast(`${P.pointName} +${r.got}, ${r.marks} marks.`, 'good'); UI.refresh(); }, d.title, 'hot');
        else act('Today\'s duty', 'log', () => UI.sheet('Temple duty', [h('p', `${d.title}.`), h('p.dim', `Win ${d.need} fights there today (${d.have}/${d.need}). Worth 8 ${P.pointName} and some marks.`)]), `${d.title} (${d.have}/${d.need})`);
      }
      const left = G.offerLeft(g);
      act('Offer 25 marks', 'coin', () => UI.doOffer('marks'), `+2 ${P.pointName}. ${left} offering${left === 1 ? '' : 's'} left today.`);
      act('Offer an Iron Core', 'gem', () => UI.doOffer('core'), `+5 ${P.pointName}. You have ${G.count(g, 'core')}.`);
      act('Renounce', 'x', () => UI.renounceSheet(), 'Turn away. It will not forget.', 'danger');
    } else {
      const c = G.canJoin(g, pid);
      if (c.ok) act(P.kind === 'astral' ? `Venerate ${P.short}` : `Swear yourself to ${P.short}`, 'up', () => UI.joinSheet(pid), P.kind === 'astral' ? 'It may notice you. It may not.' : 'Become a petitioner.', 'hot');
      else body.append(h('p.dim', c.error));
    }
    body.append(acts);
    root.append(body);
    root.append(h('div.pad', UI.btn('Back', () => UI.go('loc', { id: p.back || UI.templeLoc(pid) }), 'ghost wide')));
    root.append(UI.nav('explore'));
  };
  UI.doOffer = function (kind) { const r = G.offer(UI.g, kind); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad'); UI.save(); UI.toast(r.got ? `${G.pathDef(UI.g).pointName} +${r.got}.` : 'The offering is taken. Nothing answers.', r.got ? 'good' : ''); UI.refresh(); };
  UI.joinSheet = function (pid) {
    const g = UI.g; const P = W.PATHS[pid]; const p = G.path(g);
    const lines = [P.kind === 'astral'
      ? `You will begin to venerate ${P.name}. It does not answer prayers, but it may notice you.`
      : `You will swear yourself to ${P.name} as a petitioner.`,
    `Your ${W.PATHS.unsworn.pointName} (${p.pts}) does not transfer, but half of it carries over as a head start, up to just short of your first rank.`,
    `${P.gift} Nobody can tell you what it will wake in you. It takes an essence slot like any other.`];
    if (P.kind === 'astral') lines.push('Astral regard is permanent: the Dawn Choir will trust you less, and no god will accept you afterwards.');
    lines.push('You can renounce later, but that door stays shut for good, and the faithful will remember.');
    UI.confirm(P.kind === 'astral' ? `Venerate ${P.short}?` : `Swear to ${P.short}?`, lines.join('\n\n'), P.kind === 'astral' ? 'Venerate' : 'Swear', () => {
      const r = G.joinPath(g, pid); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad');
      UI.save(); UI.sheet(P.ranks[0], [h('p.sysline', `[You are now a ${P.ranks[0]} of ${P.short}.]`), h('p', P.tenet), r.carried ? h('p.dim', `${P.pointName}: ${r.carried} to start.`) : null]); UI.refresh();
    });
  };
  UI.renounceSheet = function () {
    const g = UI.g; const P = G.pathDef(g);
    UI.confirm(`Renounce ${P.short}?`, `You lose every blessing of ${P.name} and can never return. ${P.faction ? W.FACTIONS[P.faction].name + ' standing -25. ' : ''}Half your ${P.pointName} returns to you as Resolve.\n\nAny essence you were given stays in you. It just goes quiet.`, 'Renounce', () => {
      const r = G.renounce(g); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad'); UI.save(); UI.toast(`You turned away from ${r.name}.`, 'bad'); UI.go('town');
    });
  };
  UI.raise = function () {
    const g = UI.g; const P = G.pathDef(g); const r = G.pathRankUp(g); if (!r) return;
    UI.save();
    const lines = [h('p.sysline', `[${P.short}: you are now ${r.name}.]`), h('p.sysline', `[Blessing: ${modText(r.mods)}.]`)];
    if (r.rank === 1) lines.push(h('p.sysline', `[New: ${P.boon.name}. ${P.boon.text}]`));
    if (r.essence) {
      const E = G.ess(r.essence);
      lines.push(h('h4', 'A gift'), h('p', P.kind === 'astral' ? `Something vast turns its attention toward you for a moment. When it passes, there is an essence in your hands that wasn't there before: ${E.name}.` : `The priest places an essence in your hands, warm as a hearthstone: ${E.name}.`),
        h('p.dim', 'It takes a full day and an essence slot to absorb, like any other, and nobody can tell you what it will wake in you. It\'s in your bag.'));
    }
    const s = UI.sheet(`Raised: ${r.name}`, lines);
    if (r.choice) setTimeout(() => UI.unswornGiftSheet(r.choice), 50);
    UI.refresh();
    return s;
  };
  UI.unswornGiftSheet = function (choices) {
    const g = UI.g;
    UI.sheet('What the road provides', [h('p', 'Maud takes a lead box down from the top shelf. "Society keeps these for people who don\'t pray. Pick one. Nobody can tell you what it\'ll wake in you, so don\'t ask me."'),
      ...choices.map((eid) => { const E = G.ess(eid); return h('button.card.ess-pick', { style: { borderColor: UI.essColor(eid), width: '100%', marginTop: '8px' }, onclick: () => { g.essenceItems.push(eid); UI.save(); UI.closeSheet(); UI.toast(`Got: ${E.name} essence. Absorb it at the Drowned Lamp.`, 'good'); UI.refresh(); } },
        h('div.card-title', { style: { color: UI.essColor(eid) } }, E.name, h('span.rar', { style: { color: UI.RAR[E.rarity] } }, ' ' + E.rarity)), h('p.small', E.identity)); })]);
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- the unsworn road (at the Lodge)
  UI.roadSheet = function () {
    const g = UI.g; const P = W.PATHS.unsworn; const nx = G.pathNext(g);
    const body = [h('p.dim', '"No god? Good. Gods are a lot of paperwork." Maud keeps a ledger for people like you.'), ...UI.pathPanel()];
    const acts = [];
    if (nx && nx.ready) acts.push(UI.btn(`Be raised to ${nx.name}`, () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.raise(); }, 'primary'));
    if (nx && nx.trialReady) acts.push(UI.btn(`Trial: ${nx.name}`, () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.confirm(`Trial: ${nx.name}`, `${P.trial[nx.rank]}\n\nTrials are not lethal.`, 'Begin', () => UI.startCombat(`ptrial_unsworn_${nx.rank}`, () => UI.go('loc', { id: 'lodge' }))); }, 'primary'));
    if (G.boonReady(g)) acts.push(UI.btn(P.boon.name, () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.useBoon(); }));
    acts.push(UI.btn(`Spar in the yard (25 marks, ${G.offerLeft(g)} left)`, () => { const r = G.offer(g, 'marks'); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad'); UI.save(); UI.toast(`Resolve +${r.got}.`, 'good'); UI.roadSheet(); }, 'ghost'));
    UI.sheet('Your own road', body, acts);
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- Society
  UI.societySheet = function () {
    const g = UI.g; const s = G.society(g); const D = G.societyDef(g); const nx = G.societyNext(g);
    const body = [h('div.path-card', { style: { borderColor: '#e8a33d' } }, h('div.card-title', { style: { color: '#e8a33d' } }, D.name), h('p.small', D.text), h('div.kv', h('span', 'Contracts done'), h('b', String(G.contractsDone(g)))))];
    const acts = [];
    if (nx) {
      body.push(h('h4', `Next: ${nx.name}`), reqLine(nx.contractsOk, `${nx.contracts} contracts or board jobs (${nx.have})`), reqLine(nx.rankOk, `${W.RANKS[nx.reqRank]}-rank`));
      if (nx.assess) body.push(reqLine(nx.assessDone, 'Pass the assessment'));
      body.push(h('p.dim.small', W.SOCIETY[nx.rank].text));
      if (nx.ready) acts.push(UI.btn(`Take the ${nx.name} badge`, () => { const r = G.societyRankUp(g); UI.save(); UI.closeSheet(); UI.sheet('Promoted', [h('p.sysline', `[Lantern Society: ${r.name}.]`), h('p', W.SOCIETY[r.rank].text)]); UI.refresh(); }, 'primary'));
      if (nx.assessReady) acts.push(UI.btn('Take the assessment', () => { UI.closeSheet(); UI.confirm(W.ENCOUNTERS[nx.assess].name, `${W.ENCOUNTERS[nx.assess].intro}\n\nAssessments are not lethal.`, 'Begin', () => UI.startCombat(nx.assess, () => UI.go('loc', { id: 'lodge' }))); }, 'primary'));
    } else body.push(h('p.dim', 'Silver is as high as the Calderhal lodge can rank you.'));
    UI.sheet('Lantern Society', body, acts);
  };
  UI.boardSheet = function () {
    const g = UI.g; const s = G.society(g); const pay = G.societyDef(g).pay;
    const body = [h('p.dim.small', 'Repeatable work, posted fresh every day. Two jobs at a time.' + (G.path(g).id === 'unsworn' ? ' Each one is worth 4 Resolve on your own road.' : ''))];
    if (s.active.length) body.push(h('h4', 'Your jobs'));
    for (const t of s.active) {
      const done = t.have >= t.need;
      body.push(h('div.contract', h('div.card-title', t.title), h('p.small', `Win ${t.need} fights at ${W.MAP[t.node].name}: ${t.have}/${t.need}.`), h('div.row-r',
        done ? UI.btn(`Collect ${Math.round(t.marks * pay)} marks`, () => { const r = G.reportBoard(g, t.id); UI.save(); UI.toast(`+${r.marks} marks, ${r.cores} core${r.cores > 1 ? 's' : ''}${r.resolve ? `, Resolve +${r.resolve}` : ''}.`, 'good'); UI.boardSheet(); }, 'primary small') : UI.btn('Drop it', () => { G.dropBoard(g, t.id); UI.save(); UI.boardSheet(); }, 'ghost small'))));
    }
    body.push(h('h4', 'Posted today'));
    const offers = G.boardOffers(g);
    if (!offers.length) body.push(h('p.dim', 'Nothing left. Check back tomorrow.'));
    for (const o of offers) body.push(h('div.contract', h('div.card-title', o.title), h('p.small', `Win ${o.need} fights at ${W.MAP[o.node].name}.`), h('p.dim.small', `Pays ${Math.round(o.marks * pay)} marks and ${o.cores} Iron Core${o.cores > 1 ? 's' : ''}.`),
      UI.btn('Take it', () => { const r = G.takeBoard(g, o.id); if (r.error) return UI.toast(r.error, 'bad'); UI.save(); UI.boardSheet(); }, 'small')));
    UI.sheet('Board work', body);
  };

  // ---------------------------------------------------------------- hardcore
  UI.permadeath = function (st) {
    const g = UI.g; const rec = G.buryHero(g) || { name: g.name };
    UI.battle = null; UI.closeSheet();
    UI.g = null;
    UI.go('fallen', { rec });
  };
  S.fallen = function (root, p) {
    const r = p.rec || {};
    root.append(h('div.title-wrap.fallen',
      h('div.title-lantern', UI.icon('skull', 56)),
      h('h1.title', r.name || 'Fallen'),
      h('p.title-sub', 'Hardcore. This death is permanent.'),
      h('div.memorial',
        h('p', `${r.rank || 'Normal'}-rank. Fell on day ${r.day} to ${r.enc || 'something in the dark'}${r.where ? ' at ' + r.where : ''}.`),
        r.path ? h('p.dim', r.path === 'Unsworn' ? `Unsworn. ${r.pathRank}.` : `${r.pathRank} of ${r.path}.`) : null,
        r.essences && r.essences.length ? h('p.dim', `Essences: ${r.essences.join(', ')}.`) : null,
        h('p.dim', `${r.wins || 0} fights won of ${r.fights || 0}.`),
        h('p.flavor', 'Maud adds a lantern to the wall at the Lodge. She doesn\'t say anything. She lights it herself.')),
      h('div.title-btns', UI.btn('Hall of the Fallen', () => UI.hallSheet(), 'ghost'), UI.btn('Begin again', () => UI.go('create'), 'primary big'), UI.btn('Title', () => UI.go('title'), 'ghost'))));
  };
  UI.hallSheet = function () {
    const f = G.fallen();
    UI.sheet('Hall of the Fallen', f.length ? f.map((r) => h('div.row', h('div', h('b', r.name), h('div.dim.small', `${r.rank}, day ${r.day}. ${r.enc ? 'Fell to ' + r.enc : ''}${r.where ? ' at ' + r.where : ''}.${r.path && r.path !== 'Unsworn' ? ' ' + r.pathRank + ' of ' + r.path + '.' : ''}`)))) : [h('p.dim', 'No lanterns yet. Keep it that way.')]);
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

;

;(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  // Essencebound: Choice & Consequence update. No original ability/status definitions changed.
  const G=EB.G, UI=EB.UI, B=EB.B, K=EB.K, W=EB.W, U=EB.U, S=UI.screens;
  const h=UI.h, C=()=>EB.content;
  const original={};
  const STONE_COST={common:60,uncommon:110,rare:220,legendary:500};
  const DISC_KEY='essencebound.awakening.discoveries.v1';
  const FAMILIES={
    Assault:['hunter','predator','duelist','avenger','ruin','siege','feral','apex','skirmisher','adversary','ambush','arsenal','chainreaction','crucible','defiance','fuse','gauntlet','overflow','reckoning','spite','sunder','vantage','zenith','harbinger'],
    Guardianship:['bulwark','anchor','bastion','sentinel','oath','pact','beacon','intercessor','keystone','lattice','obelisk','quarantine','sanctum','talisman','paragon'],
    Sorcery:['sage','tempest','echo','catalyst','chronicle','arcane','convergence','eclipse','mnemonic','overtone','prism','ritual','tesseract','wildcard','solstice','equilibrium'],
    Renewal:['mender','wellspring','purifier','harvest','glutton','leech','martyr','alchemy','balance','brink','inheritance','reservoir','scavenger','temperance','tithe','supplicant'],
    Command:['legion','herald','sovereign','dominion','companion','avatar','forge','corruptor','chorus','imperative','lockstep','relay','tactician','debt','arbiter'],
    Wanderlust:['wanderer','trickster','veil','mirror','wayfarer','pathfinder','divergence','horizon','jinx','liminal','pilgrim','rift','undertow','cadence','null']
  };
  G.stoneFamily = key => Object.entries(FAMILIES).find(([,keys])=>keys.includes(key))?.[0] || 'Sorcery';
  G.stoneFamilies = FAMILIES;
  G.regionStoneFamily={road:'Wanderlust',ash:'Assault',mill:'Sorcery',scar:'Guardianship',marsh:'Renewal',flats:'Command',wrecks:'Sorcery',gate:'Wanderlust'};

  // 1: Stone research commissions. One active commission, earned through wins rather than rolling 1-in-100.
  G.stoneCommission = g => g.stoneCommission || null;
  G.commissionPrice = key => Math.round((STONE_COST[G.stoneDef(key).rarity]||100)*1.15);
  G.requestStone = function(g,key){
    if(!G.stoneDef(key)) return {error:'Unknown Awakening Stone.'};
    if(g.stoneCommission) return {error:'Complete or cancel the existing commission first.'};
    const price=G.commissionPrice(key);
    if(g.marks<price) return {error:`A commission requires ${price} marks.`};
    g.marks-=price;
    g.stoneCommission={key, progress:0, need:4, ready:false, placed:g.day};
    G.note(g,`Commission placed for ${G.stoneDef(key).name}. Win four worthy fights to locate it.`);
    return {price};
  };
  G.claimCommission = function(g){
    const c=g.stoneCommission;
    if(!c || !c.ready) return {error:'Your commission is not ready.'};
    const key=G.addStone(g,c.key);
    g.stoneCommission=null;
    G.note(g,`The Marrow Exchange delivered your commissioned ${G.stoneDef(key).name}.`);
    return {key};
  };
  G.cancelCommission = function(g){
    const c=g.stoneCommission;
    if(!c) return {error:'No active commission.'};
    const refund=Math.round(G.commissionPrice(c.key)*0.5);
    g.marks+=refund; g.stoneCommission=null;
    G.note(g,`Cancelled stone commission; ${refund} marks refunded.`);
    return {refund};
  };
  original.addStone=G.addStone;
  G.addStone=function(g,key){
    if(key==='random' && g.node){
      const family=G.regionStoneFamily[g.node];
      if(family){
        const rng=G.rng(g, g.stones.length+g.stats.fights*13+713);
        if(rng()<0.62){
          const pool=G.stoneKeys().filter(k=>G.stoneFamily(k)===family && G.stoneDef(k).rarity!=='legendary');
          if(pool.length) key=pool[Math.floor(rng()*pool.length)];
        }
      }
    }
    return original.addStone(g,key);
  };
  original.resolveBattle=G.resolveBattle;
  G.resolveBattle=function(g,b){
    const sum=original.resolveBattle(g,b);
    if(sum.win && !b.enc?.tutorial && !b.enc?.trial && !b.enc?.duel){
      if(g.stoneCommission && !g.stoneCommission.ready){
        g.stoneCommission.progress++;
        if(g.stoneCommission.progress>=g.stoneCommission.need){
          g.stoneCommission.ready=true;
          sum.notes.push('Stone commission located. Claim it through your Commission ledger.');
          G.note(g, `${G.stoneDef(g.stoneCommission.key).name} located: ready for collection.`);
        } else sum.notes.push(`Stone commission: ${g.stoneCommission.progress}/${g.stoneCommission.need} fights.`);
      }
      // A concrete, modest reward tied to the council alliance. Earned only on milestone victories.
      if(g.ending && g.stats.wins%4===0){
        const prize={lantern:()=>G.give(g,'draught',1), ithric:()=>{g.marks+=22;},
          selenne:()=>{g.marks+=15;}, varo:()=>G.addStone(g,'random'),
          magisterium:()=>G.give(g,'core',2)}[g.ending];
        if(prize){prize();sum.notes.push(`Council alliance (${g.ending}) grants its fourth-victory support.`);}
      }
    }
    return sum;
  };

  // 2: Limited, safe stone extraction. Essence removal remains consequential.
  G.respecAvailable = g => g.rank>=0 && !(g.safeRespecRanks||[]).includes(g.rank);
  G.respecPrice = g => 150+Math.max(0,g.rank)*90;
  G.safeExtractStone=function(g,index,key){
    if(!G.respecAvailable(g)) return {error:'You have used your one safe extraction for this rank.'};
    if(g.marks<G.respecPrice(g)) return {error:`Safe extraction requires ${G.respecPrice(g)} marks.`};
    const e=G.allEssences(g)[index];
    if(!e || !e.stones.includes(key)) return {error:'That stone is not implanted here.'};
    const A=C().abilities[G.ess(e.id).byStone[key]];
    g.marks-=G.respecPrice(g);
    g.safeRespecRanks=g.safeRespecRanks||[];
    g.safeRespecRanks.push(g.rank);
    e.stones=e.stones.filter(k=>k!==key);
    delete g.abilities[A.id];
    if(A.type==='familiar'){
      const t=B.familiarTemplate(A);
      if(t && !G.abilityIds(g).some(id=>B.familiarTemplate(C().abilities[id])===t)) delete g.familiars[t];
    }
    G.addStone(g,key);
    g.day++;g.coresToday={};
    G.note(g,`Hale safely unbound ${A.name}; the stone was recovered with no training loss or scar.`);
    return {lost:A,key};
  };

  // 3: A player-selected mastery portfolio: seven abilities, each essence represented.
  G.masteryTarget=7;
  G.focusIds=function(g){
    const all=new Set(G.abilityIds(g));
    g.masteryFocus=(g.masteryFocus||[]).filter(id=>all.has(id));
    return g.masteryFocus;
  };
  G.masteryInfo=function(g){
    const pool=G.abilityIds(g);
    const focus=G.focusIds(g);
    const ess=new Set(focus.map(id=>C().abilities[id]?.essence).filter(Boolean));
    const required=new Set(G.allEssences(g).map(e=>e.id));
    const mature=focus.filter(id=>{
      const st=G.abilityState(g,id);
      return st.train>=80 && G.unseasoned(g,id)<=20;
    });
    const covered=[...required].filter(x=>ess.has(x));
    const ready=G.fullSet(g) && focus.length===G.masteryTarget && covered.length===required.size && mature.length===focus.length;
    return {focus,mature,covered,total:required.size,ready,pool};
  };
  G.toggleFocus=function(g,id){
    if(!G.abilityIds(g).includes(id)) return {error:'You have not awakened that ability.'};
    const f=G.focusIds(g); const at=f.indexOf(id);
    if(at>=0) f.splice(at,1);
    else if(f.length<G.masteryTarget) f.push(id);
    else return {error:`Choose at most ${G.masteryTarget} abilities. Unselect one first.`};
    return {focus:f};
  };
  G.autoFocus=function(g){
    const ids=G.abilityIds(g);
    const picks=[];
    for(const e of G.allEssences(g)){
      const options=ids.filter(id=>C().abilities[id]?.essence===e.id)
        .sort((a,b)=>G.abilityState(g,b).train-G.abilityState(g,a).train);
      if(options[0]) picks.push(options[0]);
    }
    const other=ids.filter(id=>!picks.includes(id))
      .sort((a,b)=>G.abilityState(g,b).train-G.abilityState(g,a).train);
    g.masteryFocus=picks.concat(other.slice(0,Math.max(0,G.masteryTarget-picks.length)));
    return G.masteryInfo(g);
  };
  G.trialReady = g => (g.rank===0 || g.rank===1) && G.masteryInfo(g).ready;
  original.breakthrough=G.breakthrough;
  G.breakthrough=function(g){const res=original.breakthrough(g); g.masteryFocus=[];return res;};
  G.playerLevel = g => 1 + Math.max(0,g.rank)*3;

  // 4: Persistent awakening discoveries. Stored globally across characters in this browser.
  G.discoveries=function(g){
    let global={};
    try {global=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(DISC_KEY)||'{}');}catch(e){}
    return Object.assign({},global,(g && g.awakeningDiscoveries)||{});
  };
  G.recordDiscovery=function(g,essId,key){
    const E=G.ess(essId), A=E && C().abilities[E.byStone[key]];
    if(!E||!A)return;
    const tag=essId+':'+key, val={id:A.id, day:g.day};
    g.awakeningDiscoveries=g.awakeningDiscoveries||{};
    g.awakeningDiscoveries[tag]=val;
    try{const x=G.discoveries(g); x[tag]=val;localStorage.setItem(DISC_KEY,JSON.stringify(x));}catch(e){}
  };
  G.rememberExisting=function(g){
    for(const e of G.allEssences(g)) for(const key of e.stones||[]) {
      const E=G.ess(e.id),A=E&&C().abilities[E.byStone[key]];
      if(!A) continue;
      g.awakeningDiscoveries=g.awakeningDiscoveries||{};
      if(!g.awakeningDiscoveries[e.id+':'+key])G.recordDiscovery(g,e.id,key);
    }
  };
  original.absorbStone=G.absorbStone;
  G.absorbStone=function(g,uid,index){
    const e=G.allEssences(g)[index],id=e&&e.id;
    const r=original.absorbStone(g,uid,index);
    if(!r.error && id)G.recordDiscovery(g,id,r.key);
    return r;
  };

  // 5: Encounter modifiers, bounded to routine exploration (not scripted bosses/trials).
  const modList=[
    {id:'fog',name:'Blinding Fog',desc:'Visibility drops; enemies lose accuracy, allies gain evasion.'},
    {id:'vanguard',name:'Iron Vanguard',desc:'Frontline enemies are fortified and shielded.'},
    {id:'haste',name:'Frenzied Pack',desc:'Foes begin with a burst of speed and power.'},
    {id:'ambush',name:'Sudden Ambush',desc:'Enemies act sooner, but expose themselves.'},
    {id:'sanctuary',name:'Resonant Ground',desc:'Both sides gain a small protective resonance.'},
    {id:'fragile',name:'Shattered Defenses',desc:'Enemies are exposed to focused attacks.'}
  ];
  G.encounterModifiers=modList;
  original.encounterFor=G.encounterFor;
  G.encounterFor=function(g,node){
    g.pendingEncounterMod=null;
    const r=original.encounterFor(g,node);
    if(r.enc && W.ENCOUNTERS[r.enc] && !W.ENCOUNTERS[r.enc].boss && !W.ENCOUNTERS[r.enc].trial && !W.ENCOUNTERS[r.enc].tutorial && !W.ENCOUNTERS[r.enc].duel){
      const rng=G.rng(g,1129 + (g.explored[node]||0)*37 + g.stats.fights);
      if(rng()<0.76){
        const mod=modList[Math.floor(rng()*modList.length)];
        g.pendingEncounterMod=mod.id;
        r.modifier=mod;
      }
    }
    return r;
  };
  original.makeBattle=G.makeBattle;
  G.makeBattle=function(g,encId,opts){
    const b=original.makeBattle(g,encId,opts);
    const E=W.ENCOUNTERS[encId];
    const mod=modList.find(m=>m.id===g.pendingEncounterMod);
    g.pendingEncounterMod=null;
    if(!mod || !E || E.boss || E.trial || E.duel || E.tutorial) return b;
    b.modifier=mod.id;b.env.modifier=mod.name;
    const foes=b.units.filter(u=>u.side==='enemy'), allies=b.units.filter(u=>u.side==='ally');
    if(mod.id==='fog'){
      foes.forEach(u=>K.addMod(b,u,'accuracy',-0.12,3,null));
      allies.forEach(u=>K.addMod(b,u,'evasion',0.08,3,null));
    }else if(mod.id==='vanguard'){
      foes.filter(u=>u.row==='vanguard').forEach(u=>{K.applyStatus(b,null,u,'fortified',1,{dur:3,force:true});K.addShield(b,u,Math.max(5,Math.round(K.maxHp(u)*0.08)),3,null);});
    }else if(mod.id==='haste'){
      foes.forEach(u=>{K.applyStatus(b,null,u,'hasted',1,{dur:2,force:true});K.applyStatus(b,null,u,'empowered',1,{dur:2,force:true});});
    }else if(mod.id==='ambush'){
      foes.forEach(u=>{u.t=Math.max(0,u.t-15);K.applyStatus(b,null,u,'exposed',1,{dur:2,force:true});});
    }else if(mod.id==='sanctuary'){
      allies.concat(foes).forEach(u=>K.addShield(b,u,Math.max(4,Math.round(K.maxHp(u)*0.05)),3,null));
    }else if(mod.id==='fragile'){
      foes.forEach(u=>K.applyStatus(b,null,u,'exposed',1,{dur:2,force:true}));
    }
    K.say(b,`Encounter: ${mod.name}. ${mod.desc}`,'sys');
    return b;
  };

  // 6: Meaningful continuing alliance perks and reputation-aware trading.
  original.priceOf=G.priceOf;
  G.priceOf=function(g,kind,id,shop){
    let p=original.priceOf(g,kind,id,shop);
    if(shop!=='black' && shop!=='landing'){
      if(g.ending==='selenne')p=Math.round(p*0.95);
      if(g.ending==='magisterium' && kind==='stone')p=Math.round(p*0.93);
      if((g.standing.lantern||0)<-25 && kind==='stone')p=Math.round(p*1.12);
    }
    return Math.max(1,p);
  };

  // 7: Lightweight context-aware combat hints, derived from executable effect data.
  UI.synergyHints=function(b,actor,def){
    if(!def||!actor)return [];
    const statuses=new Set();
    const foes=b.units.filter(u=>u.alive && u.side!==actor.side);
    for(const u of foes)for(const id in u.statuses) if(u.statuses[id].stacks>0)statuses.add(id);
    const all=[];
    const walk=effs=>{for(const e of effs||[]){
      if(e.op==='consume_status' && e.status && statuses.has(e.status))all.push(`Consume ${K.statusDef(e.status).name} now`);
      if(e.op==='damage' && e.vs_status && statuses.has(e.vs_status))all.push(`Bonus vs ${K.statusDef(e.vs_status).name}`);
      if(e.op==='reaction' && e.trigger)all.push('Sets a reactive effect');
      if(e.op==='cleanse' && b.units.some(u=>u.side===actor.side && Object.keys(u.statuses).some(k=>!K.isBoon(k))))all.push('Ally affliction can be cleansed');
      if(e.effects)walk(e.effects);if(e.then)walk(e.then);if(e.else)walk(e.else);
      for(const x of e.options||[])if(x.effects)walk(x.effects);
    }};
    walk(def.effects);
    return [...new Set(all)].slice(0,2);
  };
  original.tray=UI.tray;
  UI.tray=function(st,hero){
    const tray=original.tray(st,hero);
    if(st.mode==='choose' && st.tab==='abilities' && hero){
      const cards=tray.querySelectorAll('button.ab:not(.basic)');
      const slots=hero.abilities.filter(s=>!s.def.passive && s.def.type!=='familiar');
      for(const card of cards){
        const title=card.querySelector('.ab-top b')?.textContent.replace(/^★ /,'');
        const slot=slots.find(s=>s.def.name===title);
        if(!slot)continue;
        const hints=UI.synergyHints(st.b,hero,slot.def);
        if(hints.length){
          const tip=h('div.synergy-tip',hints.join(' · '));
          card.append(tip);
        }
      }
    }
    return tray;
  };

  // 8: A dedicated Awakening Journal and build-planning pages.
  UI.stoneCommissionSheet=function(){
    const g=UI.g;
    const c=g.stoneCommission;
    const body=[h('p.dim','Commission a specific Awakening Stone. Ottoline will send word after four victorious fights. The price is paid upfront; cancellation refunds half. Random drops remain available.')];
    const actions=[];
    if(c){
      const sd=G.stoneDef(c.key);
      body.push(h('div.hint',h('b',sd.name),h('p.small',c.ready?'Located — ready to collect.':`${c.progress}/${c.need} qualifying victories.`)));
      if(c.ready)actions.push(UI.btn('Collect stone',()=>{const r=G.claimCommission(g);if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');UI.save();UI.closeSheet();UI.toast('Collected '+G.stoneDef(r.key).name,'good');UI.refresh();},'primary'));
      actions.push(UI.btn('Cancel & refund half',()=>UI.confirm('Cancel commission?','You will receive half the original commission price.', 'Cancel',()=>{G.cancelCommission(g);UI.save();UI.stoneCommissionSheet();}),'ghost'));
    }else{
      const filters=h('div.tabs',{style:{flexWrap:'wrap'}});
      const results=h('div',{style:{maxHeight:'44vh',overflowY:'auto'}});
      const draw=fam=>{
        results.replaceChildren();
        const stones=G.stoneKeys().filter(k=>G.stoneFamily(k)===fam).sort((a,b)=>G.stoneDef(a).name.localeCompare(G.stoneDef(b).name));
        for(const key of stones){
          const sd=G.stoneDef(key),price=G.commissionPrice(key);
          results.append(h('div.row',h('div.grow',h('b',sd.name),h('div.dim.small',sd.theme)),UI.btn(`${price} marks`,()=>UI.confirm('Commission '+sd.name,`Pay ${price} marks to guarantee this stone after four victories.`, 'Commission',()=>{const r=G.requestStone(g,key);if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');UI.save();UI.stoneCommissionSheet();}), 'small',g.marks<price?{disabled:true}:{})));
        }
      };
      Object.keys(FAMILIES).forEach((fam,i)=>filters.append(UI.btn(fam,()=>{filters.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(b=>b.classList.remove('on'));draw(fam);},'tiny')));
      draw(G.regionStoneFamily[g.node]||'Assault');
      body.push(h('p.small','Browse by stone family:'),filters,results);
    }
    UI.sheet('Stone commissions',body,actions);
  };
  const oldShop=S.shop;
  S.shop=function(root,p){oldShop(root,p);
    if(p.shop==='landing'||p.sell)return;
    const box=h('div.pad',UI.btn(gStoneLabel(),()=>UI.stoneCommissionSheet(),'wide primary'),h('p.dim.small','Browse a stone family and order a specific stone. Win four fights to fulfill the commission.'));
    function gStoneLabel(){const c=UI.g.stoneCommission;return c?(c.ready?'Collect your stone commission':'View stone commission '+c.progress+'/'+c.need):'Commission a specific stone';}
    const nav=root.querySelector('nav.bottom');if(nav)root.insertBefore(box,nav);else root.append(box);
  };
  const oldContracts=UI.contractsSheet;
  UI.contractsSheet=function(){
    if(!UI.g?.flags.joined_lantern){
      return UI.sheet('Lantern Society membership',[
        h('p','Maud looks at the pin in your hand. \"You can take contracts once you wear it. Your choice.\"'),
        h('p.dim.small','Joining grants Society standing and unlocks Society board work. You may continue exploring without joining.')
      ],[UI.btn('Pledge and join',()=>{
        UI.g.flags.joined_lantern=true;delete UI.g.flags.lantern_considering;
        G.addStanding(UI.g,'lantern',8);G.note(UI.g,'You joined the Lantern Society.');
        UI.save();UI.contractsSheet();
      },'primary'),UI.btn('Not yet',UI.closeSheet,'ghost')]);
    }
    return oldContracts();
  };
  const oldBoardSheet=UI.boardSheet;
  UI.boardSheet=function(){if(!UI.g?.flags.joined_lantern)return UI.contractsSheet();return oldBoardSheet();};
  const oldLoc=S.loc;
  S.loc=function(root,p){oldLoc(root,p);
    if(p.id==='exchange'){
      const body=root.querySelector('.pad');
      const btn=UI.btn('Awakening stone commissions',()=>UI.stoneCommissionSheet(),'wide');
      if(body)body.append(btn);
    }
    if(p.id==='lodge' && !UI.g.flags.joined_lantern){
      const body=root.querySelector('.acts');
      if(body)body.append(UI.btn('Pledge to the Lantern Society',()=>UI.confirm('Take the lantern pin?','Maud will record your commitment. This is voluntary.','Join',()=>{
        UI.g.flags.joined_lantern=true;delete UI.g.flags.lantern_considering;
        G.addStanding(UI.g,'lantern',8);G.note(UI.g,'You pledged yourself to the Lantern Society.');UI.save();UI.refresh();
      }),'wide primary'));
    }
    if(p.id==='magisterium'){
      const body=root.querySelector('.acts');if(body)body.append(UI.btn('Review safe stone respecialization',()=>UI.surgerySheet('magisterium'),'wide'));
    }
  };
  const oldSurgery=UI.surgerySheet;
  UI.surgerySheet=function(where){
    if(where!=='magisterium')return oldSurgery(where);
    const g=UI.g;
    const rows=[h('p','Once per rank, Hale can safely unbind one Awakening Stone. The stone is recovered, other abilities retain training, and you suffer no strain or scar. The operation takes one day.')];
    rows.push(h('div.hint',G.respecAvailable(g)?`Available at ${G.rankName(g)}-rank · ${G.respecPrice(g)} marks.`:'Your safe unbinding for this rank is already used.'));
    if(G.respecAvailable(g)) G.allEssences(g).forEach((e,i)=>{
      const E=G.ess(e.id);
      for(const key of e.stones){
        const A=C().abilities[E.byStone[key]];
        rows.push(h('div.row',h('div.grow',h('b',A.name),h('div.dim.small',E.name+' · '+G.stoneDef(key).name)),UI.btn('Safely unbind',()=>UI.confirm('Recover '+G.stoneDef(key).name,`Cost ${G.respecPrice(g)} marks and one day. ${A.name} will be unlearned. No other training is lost.`, 'Unbind',()=>{const r=G.safeExtractStone(g,i,key);if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');UI.save();UI.surgerySheet(where);UI.toast('Stone recovered.','good');}),'small')));
      }
    });
    rows.push(h('p.dim.small','Ordinary extraction remains available: it destroys the stone and reduces other training, but costs less.'));
    UI.sheet('Magisterium · Safe unbinding',rows,[UI.btn('Destructive surgery',()=>oldSurgery(where),'ghost'),UI.btn('Close',UI.closeSheet)]);
  };
  const oldTrain=S.train;
  S.train=function(root){
    oldTrain(root);
    const g=UI.g;
    const info=G.masteryInfo(g);
    const extra=h('div.rankbox');
    extra.append(h('h3','Mastery portfolio'),h('p.small.dim','Select seven abilities, including at least one from each of your five essences. Train all seven to 80% and season core-fed training to 20% or less; then face the Magisterium trial.'));
    extra.append(h('div.kv',h('span','Selected'),h('b',info.focus.length+'/7')),h('div.kv',h('span','Essences covered'),h('b',info.covered.length+'/'+info.total)),h('div.kv',h('span','Mastered'),h('b',info.mature.length+'/7')));
    extra.append(UI.btn('Suggest balanced portfolio',()=>{G.autoFocus(g);UI.save();UI.refresh();},'small ghost wide'));
    for(const e of G.allEssences(g)){
      const E=G.ess(e.id);
      extra.append(h('h4',E.name));
      const ids=G.abilityIds(g).filter(id=>C().abilities[id]?.essence===e.id);
      for(const id of ids){
        const A=C().abilities[id],st=G.abilityState(g,id),on=info.focus.includes(id);
        extra.append(h('div.row',h('div.grow',h('b',A.name),h('div.dim.small',Math.round(st.train)+'% trained · '+Math.round(G.unseasoned(g,id))+'% unseasoned')),UI.btn(on?'✓ Focus':'Focus',()=>{const r=G.toggleFocus(g,id);if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');UI.save();UI.refresh();},on?'small on':'small ghost')));
      }
    }
    const pane=root.querySelector('div.pad');
    if(pane)pane.insertBefore(extra,pane.firstChild);else root.append(extra);
  };
  const oldChar=S.char;
  S.char=function(root){oldChar(root);
    const g=UI.g,info=G.masteryInfo(g);
    const pane=root.querySelector('div.pad');
    if(pane&&G.fullSet(g))pane.insertBefore(h('div.hint',info.ready?'Mastery portfolio complete. Your trial is ready.':`Mastery focus: ${info.mature.length}/${G.masteryTarget} trained · ${info.covered.length}/${info.total} essences covered. Choose your focus in Training.`),pane.firstChild);
  };
  const oldJournal=S.journal;
  S.journal=function(root,p){
    if(p.tab!=='awakening'){
      oldJournal(root,p);
      const tabs=root.querySelector('.tabs');if(tabs)tabs.append(UI.btn('Awakening',()=>UI.go('journal',{tab:'awakening'}),'tab'));
      return;
    }
    const g=UI.g;
    G.rememberExisting(g);
    const records=G.discoveries(g), count=Object.keys(records).length;
    root.append(UI.header('Awakening Journal',`${count} of ${G.stoneKeys().length*Object.keys(C().essences).length} combinations discovered`));
    const wrap=h('div.pad');
    wrap.append(h('p.dim','Discoveries persist across your characters in this browser. An unknown pairing stays hidden until you awaken it. Stone themes are clues, not guaranteed outcomes.'),UI.btn('Stone commissions',()=>UI.stoneCommissionSheet(),'wide primary'));
    const chosen=h('select',{style:{width:'100%',padding:'11px',background:'var(--card)',color:'var(--text)',border:'1px solid var(--line2)',borderRadius:'9px'}});
    const eids=Object.keys(C().essences).sort((a,b)=>G.ess(a).name.localeCompare(G.ess(b).name));
    for(const id of eids)chosen.append(h('option',{value:id},G.ess(id).name));
    chosen.value=UI.awakeEssence||G.allEssences(g)[0]?.id||eids[0];
    const catalog=h('div');
    const render=()=>{
      const eid=chosen.value;UI.awakeEssence=eid;
      catalog.replaceChildren();
      for(const family of Object.keys(FAMILIES)){
        catalog.append(h('h4',family));
        for(const key of G.stoneKeys().filter(k=>G.stoneFamily(k)===family)){
          const sd=G.stoneDef(key),record=records[eid+':'+key];
          const ab=record&&C().abilities[G.ess(eid)?.byStone[key]];
          const label=ab?ab.name:'Unknown resonance';
          catalog.append(h('div.row',h('div.grow',h('b',sd.name),h('div.dim.small',label)),ab?UI.btn('Inspect',()=>UI.abilitySheet(ab.id),'tiny ghost'):h('span.dim.small','?')));
        }
      }
    };
    chosen.onchange=render;render();
    wrap.append(h('label.lbl','Essence'),chosen,catalog);
    root.append(wrap,UI.nav('journal'));
  };
  const oldResume=UI.resume;
  UI.resume=function(){
    if(UI.g)G.rememberExisting(UI.g);
    if(UI.g?.pendingCombat && UI.g.pendingFight && !UI.battle)return UI.resumeBattle();
    return oldResume();
  };

  // 9: Battle recovery by deterministic replay of accepted player actions.
  // This avoids serializing closures in hundreds of reaction hooks and boss phases.
  UI._replayingCombat=false;
  UI._battleCallbacks=function(g,snap){
    if(snap.context?.scene){
      return sum=>{
        const scene=snap.context.scene;
        const node=EB.SCENES[scene.id]?.nodes[scene.node];
        let fight=node?.d?.fight;
        if(!fight && node?.o)fight=node.o.map(o=>o.d?.fight).find(x=>x && x.enc===snap.id);
        const dest=fight&&(sum.win?fight.win:fight.lose);
        if(dest){g.scene={id:scene.id,node:dest};UI.save();return UI.go('scene');}
        g.scene=null;UI.save();return UI.resume();
      };
    }
    if(snap.context?.loc==='hollow'){
      return sum=>{
        const did=snap.context.dungeon||'glass';const D=W.DUNGEONS[did];const ds=UI.dstate(g,did);
        const room=D?.rooms[ds.room];
        if(!sum.win)return UI.go(sum.wakeAtLodge?'town':'hollow');
        if(room?.enc===D.boss){ds.cleared[room.id]=true;UI.save();return UI.playScene(D.after,null,()=>UI.go('map'));}
        if(room){ds.cleared[room.id]=true;if(ds.room<D.rooms.length-1)ds.room++;}
        UI.save();return UI.go('hollow');
      };
    }
    return ()=>UI.go(snap.context?.loc==='hollow'?'hollow':snap.context?.loc==='town'?'town':'map');
  };
  const oldStart=UI.startCombat;
  UI.startCombat=function(encId,onDone){
    const g=UI.g;
    const ctx={loc:g.loc,node:g.node,dungeon:g.dungeon,scene:g.scene?{id:g.scene.id,node:g.scene.node}:null};
    const triggers={scoutAmbush:g.flags.scoutAmbush,cinder_ambush:g.flags.cinder_ambush,pendingEncounterMod:g.pendingEncounterMod};
    const r=oldStart(encId,onDone);
    // Original startCombat saves before the battle exists; checkpoint immediately afterwards.
    g.pendingCombat={version:1,id:encId,actions:[],context:ctx,triggers,created:Date.now()};
    UI.save();
    return r;
  };
  const indexOfTarget=(b,id)=>b.units.findIndex(u=>u.id===id);
  const oldAct=B.act;
  B.act=function(b,u,a){
    const st=UI.battle;
    const record=!UI._replayingCombat && u?.isPlayer && st?.b===b && UI.g?.pendingCombat;
    const cmd=record?{type:a.type,slot:a.slot,targetIndex:indexOfTarget(b,a.target),choice:a.choice}:null;
    const r=oldAct(b,u,a);
    if(record && !r?.error){UI.g.pendingCombat.actions.push(cmd);UI.save();}
    return r;
  };
  const oldUseItem=B.useItem;
  B.useItem=function(b,u,item,target){
    const st=UI.battle;
    const record=!UI._replayingCombat && u?.isPlayer && st?.b===b && UI.g?.pendingCombat;
    const cmd=record?{type:'item',item:item?.id,targetIndex:target?b.units.indexOf(target):-1}:null;
    const r=oldUseItem(b,u,item,target);
    if(record&&!r?.error){UI.g.pendingCombat.actions.push(cmd);UI.save();}
    return r;
  };
  const oldCommand=B.command;
  B.command=function(b,u,fam,idx,target){
    const st=UI.battle;
    const record=!UI._replayingCombat && u?.isPlayer && st?.b===b && UI.g?.pendingCombat;
    const cmd=record?{type:'command',famIndex:b.units.indexOf(fam),idx,targetIndex:target?b.units.indexOf(target):-1}:null;
    const r=oldCommand(b,u,fam,idx,target);
    if(record&&!r?.error){UI.g.pendingCombat.actions.push(cmd);UI.save();}
    return r;
  };
  UI.resumeBattle=function(){
    const g=UI.g,snap=g.pendingCombat;
    if(!snap||!W.ENCOUNTERS[snap.id]){g.pendingFight=null;g.pendingCombat=null;UI.save();return oldResume();}
    try{
      const oldFlags={scoutAmbush:g.flags.scoutAmbush,cinder_ambush:g.flags.cinder_ambush,pendingEncounterMod:g.pendingEncounterMod};
      if(snap.triggers?.scoutAmbush)g.flags.scoutAmbush=true;
      if(snap.triggers?.cinder_ambush)g.flags.cinder_ambush=true;
      g.pendingEncounterMod=snap.triggers?.pendingEncounterMod||null;
      const st={queue:[],onDone:UI._battleCallbacks(g,snap),mode:'idle',pending:null,busy:false,tab:'abilities',auto:false,log:[],speed:UI.prefSpeed||'fast'};
      UI._replayingCombat=true;
      st.b=G.makeBattle(g,snap.id,{onEvent:e=>{st.log.push(e);}});
      UI.battle=st;
      Object.assign(g.flags,oldFlags);
      g.pendingEncounterMod=null;
      let pos=0,iterations=0;
      const actions=snap.actions||[];
      while(!st.b.over && iterations++<8000){
        const r=B.stepOne(st.b);
        if(!r)break;
        if(!r.awaitPlayer)continue;
        const u=r.unit;
        while(actions[pos]?.type==='command'){
          const a=actions[pos++];
          B.command(st.b,u,st.b.units[a.famIndex],a.idx,a.targetIndex>=0?st.b.units[a.targetIndex]:null);
        }
        if(pos>=actions.length){st.mode='choose';st.actor=u;break;}
        const a=actions[pos++];
        const target=a.targetIndex>=0?st.b.units[a.targetIndex]:null;
        let res;
        if(a.type==='item')res=B.useItem(st.b,u,Object.assign({},W.ITEMS[a.item],{id:a.item}),target);
        else res=B.act(st.b,u,{type:a.type,slot:a.slot,target:target?.id,choice:a.choice});
        if(res?.error)throw Error(`Replay action ${pos}: ${res.error}`);
      }
      UI._replayingCombat=false;
      if(iterations>=8000)throw Error('Battle replay exceeded safety limit');
      UI.go('combat');
      if(st.b.over){UI.endCombat();return;}
      if(st.mode!=='choose')UI.combatLoop();
      UI.toast(`Battle recovered (${pos} actions replayed).`,'good');
    }catch(err){
      UI._replayingCombat=false;
      UI.battle=null;
      // Never quietly discard a Hardcore battle. Leave the record available for another load.
      UI.go('town');
      UI.sheet('Battle recovery needs attention',[h('p','The saved combat could not be safely replayed. No fight rewards or losses were applied.'),h('p.dim.small',String(err.message||err)),h('p.dim.small','Your in-progress battle has been kept in the save. You can retry by returning to the title screen and continuing.')]);
    }
  };

  // Consequences, quest visibility and collection progress appear in the journal without exposing undiscovered abilities.
  const originalJournal=S.journal;
  S.journal=function(root,p){
    originalJournal(root,p);
    if(p.tab==='factions'){
      const pane=root.querySelector('.pad');
      if(pane){
        const g=UI.g;
        pane.append(h('h3','Decisions remembered'),h('p.small',g.flags.joined_lantern?'You pledged to the Lantern Society.':g.flags.lantern_considering?'You took a pin but did not join the Lantern Society.':'You have not pledged to the Lantern Society.'),
          h('p.small',g.ending?`Glasswater Hollow was entrusted to ${g.ending}. That choice affects trading and periodic alliance support.`:'Glasswater Hollow has not yet been assigned.'),
          h('p.dim.small',g.stoneCommission?`Stone commission: ${G.stoneDef(g.stoneCommission.key).name} (${g.stoneCommission.progress}/${g.stoneCommission.need}).`:'No active stone commission.'));
      }
    }
  };
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

    caspian: {place:'lodge', minTrust:10, text:'Caspian equips you as a reliable comrade.', reward:'Fire Flasks and a Healing Draught.', item:{flask:2,draught:1}, line:'"A Varo does not abandon someone who stood beside him," Caspian says, pretending the supplies are a casual gift.'},

    jory: {place:'lamp', minTrust:10, text:'Jory offers an old friend’s kindness.', reward:'A Healing Draught.', item:{draught:1}, line:'Jory left a draught and a crooked note: "You still owe me a pie."'}
