  W.SOCIAL_NPC_FAVORS = MAP;
  const factionByLoc = {lodge:'lantern',magisterium:'magisterium',chapel:'choir',undercroft:'velvet',exchange:'selenne',steps:'ithric'};
  const political = new Set(['lantern','magisterium','choir','velvet','varo','ithric','selenne']);
    const thresholdNames = {'-40':'Wary','-10':'Neutral','15':'Friendly','45':'Trusted','80':'Sworn'};
  const significant = {
    ledger_velvet:{velvet:5,ithric:-5},ledger_ithric:{ithric:6,velvet:-5},
    gannet_choir:{choir:5,varo:-6},gannet_varo:{varo:5,choir:-6},
    petra_bribed:{ithric:-10,magisterium:-5},
    petra_exposed:{ithric:5,velvet:3},
    freed_acolyte:{choir:4,ashen:-8}
  };
  const endings = {
    lantern:{choir:5,velvet:-5}, ithric:{selenne:-8,varo:-5}, selenne:{velvet:4,ithric:-8},
    varo:{selenne:-5,ithric:-8},magisterium:{choir:3,velvet:-5}
  };

  G.socialEnsure = function(g){
    if(!g) return;
    g.flags ||= {}; g.standing ||= {}; g.npc ||= {}; g.journal ||= []; g.stats ||= {fights:0,wins:0,losses:0};
    g.socialHistory ||= []; g.socialFavors ||= {}; g.socialNPCFavors ||= {};
    for(const n in W.NPCS) if(!g.npc[n]) g.npc[n]={aff:0,trust:0};
    for(const f in W.FACTIONS) if(!Number.isFinite(g.standing[f])) g.standing[f]=0;
    if(!g.socialMigrationV1){
      // A limited, non-additive recognition of past deeds for old saves.
      const evidence = {maud:[g.flags.maud_ally,8],caspian:[g.flags.caspian_ally,10],
        hale:[g.flags.hale_lore,5], anselm:[g.flags.pilgrim_turned,6],
        sixpence:[g.flags.crate_paid,6],gannet:[g.flags.gannet_landing,10]};
      for(const n in evidence) if(evidence[n][0] && g.npc[n]) g.npc[n].trust=Math.max(g.npc[n].trust,evidence[n][1]);
      g.socialMigrationV1=true;
    }
  };
  G.socialRecord = function(g,title,detail){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    g.socialHistory.unshift({day:g.day,title:String(title),detail:String(detail||'')});
    if(g.socialHistory.length>36)g.socialHistory.length=36;
  };
  G.socialTier = function(v){return G.tier(v||0);};
  G.socialStandingInfo = function(g,f){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    const val=g.standing[f]||0;
    const next=[-40,-10,15,45,80].find(x=>val<x)??null;
    const perks=PROF[f]||{};
    return {value:val,tier:G.tier(val),next,nextName:next?thresholdNames[next]:null,perks,
      active:val>=15, trusted:val>=45, sworn:val>=80, negative:val<-10, hostile:val<-40};
  };
  G.socialRelationship = function(g,n){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    const s=g.npc[n]||{aff:0,trust:0}, a=s.aff||0, t=s.trust||0;
    const tone=a>=10?(t>=10?'Friend and confidant':'Warm but cautious'):(t>=10?'Professional respect':'Distant or strained');
    const detail=a>=10?(t>=10?'They like you and rely on your word.':'They enjoy your company, but keep sensitive matters private.'):(t>=10?'They trust your judgement even if they do not like you much.':'You have not yet earned their affection or confidence.');
    return {aff:a,trust:t,tone,detail};
  };
  G.socialChangeNPC = function(g,n,changes,source){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(!g.npc[n])return;
    const before={...g.npc[n]},x=g.npc[n];
    if(changes.aff) x.aff=clamp((x.aff||0)+changes.aff,-100,100);
    if(changes.trust) x.trust=clamp((x.trust||0)+changes.trust,-100,100);
    const msg=[];
    if(x.aff!==before.aff)msg.push(`affinity ${x.aff-before.aff>0?'+':''}${x.aff-before.aff}`);
    if(x.trust!==before.trust)msg.push(`trust ${x.trust-before.trust>0?'+':''}${x.trust-before.trust}`);
    if(msg.length)G.socialRecord(g,npcName(n),`${source||'An interaction'}: ${msg.join(', ')}`);
  };
  const prevStanding=G.addStanding;
  G.addStanding=function(g,f,n){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    const start=g.standing[f]||0;
    let amount=Number(n)||0;
    // Repeatable jobs restore relations, but cannot build Trusted/Sworn status by grinding.
    if(g._socialRoutine && amount>0)amount=Math.max(0,Math.min(amount,44-start));
    prevStanding(g,f,amount);
    const delta=(g.standing[f]||0)-start;
    if(delta)G.socialRecord(g,factionName(f),`${delta>0?'+':''}${delta} standing → ${G.tier(g.standing[f])} (${g.standing[f]})`);
    return delta;
  };
  const prevApply=G.apply;
  G.apply=function(g,d){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(!d)return prevApply(g,d);
    const preFlags=new Set(Object.keys(g.flags).filter(x=>g.flags[x]));
    const preEnding=g.ending;
    const preNpc={};
    for(const n in d.trust||{})preNpc[n]=g.npc[n]?.trust||0;
    let effect=d;
    if(d.socialRepeat){
      g.socialRepeatLast ||= {};
      const last=g.socialRepeatLast[d.socialRepeat];
      effect={...d};
      // Repeatable donations, healing and carousing offer relationship gains at most once every seven days.
      if(last!=null && g.day-last<7){delete effect.standing;delete effect.affinity;delete effect.trust;}
      else {
        g.socialRepeatLast[d.socialRepeat]=g.day;
        if(effect.affinity){const affinity={};for(const n in effect.affinity){const v=effect.affinity[n];affinity[n]=v>0?Math.max(0,Math.min(v,25-(g.npc[n]?.aff||0))):v;}effect.affinity=affinity;}
      }
    }
    if((g._socialRoutine||d.socialRepeat) && effect.standing){
      const standing={};
      for(const f in effect.standing){const v=effect.standing[f];standing[f]=v>0?Math.max(0,Math.min(v,44-(g.standing[f]||0))):v;}
      effect=Object.assign({},effect,{standing});
    }
    const out=prevApply(g,effect);
    for(const n in effect.trust||{}){
      const delta=(g.npc[n]?.trust||0)-preNpc[n];
      if(delta){G.socialRecord(g,npcName(n),`Trust ${delta>0?'+':''}${delta}: ${G.socialRelationship(g,n).tone}`);out.notes.push({m:`${npcName(n)} trust ${delta>0?'+':''}${delta}`,cls:delta>0?'good':'bad'});}
    }
    // Political changes happen only for named, one-time story decisions—not every reputation gain.
    for(const key of [d.flag,d.flag2])if(typeof key==='string' && !preFlags.has(key) && significant[key]){
      for(const f in significant[key]){const actual=G.addStanding(g,f,significant[key][f]);if(actual)out.notes.push({m:`Political ripple: ${factionName(f)} ${actual>0?'+':''}${actual}`,cls:actual>0?'good':'bad'});}
      G.socialRecord(g,'News in Calderhal',`Word spreads about ${key.replaceAll('_',' ')}.`);
    }
    if(d.ending && d.ending!==preEnding && endings[d.ending]){
      for(const f in endings[d.ending]){const actual=G.addStanding(g,f,endings[d.ending][f]);if(actual)out.notes.push({m:`Political ripple: ${factionName(f)} ${actual>0?'+':''}${actual}`,cls:actual>0?'good':'bad'});}
      G.socialRecord(g,'A public allegiance',`The Glasswater rights went to ${d.ending}. Other factions take notice.`);
    }
    return out;
  };
  const prevPay=G.payContract;
  G.payContract=function(g,id){
    g._socialRoutine=true;
    try{return prevPay(g,id);}finally{delete g._socialRoutine;}
  };
  const prevReport=G.reportBoard;
  G.reportBoard=function(g,id){
    g._socialRoutine=true;
    try{return prevReport(g,id);}finally{delete g._socialRoutine;}
  };
  // A member keeps their earned badge, but their current reputation affects access to its privileges.
  const prevSocietyDef=G.societyDef;
  G.societyDef=function(g){
    const base=prevSocietyDef(g);if(!g)return base;
    const rep=(g.standing||{}).lantern||0;
    const factor=rep<-40?.3:rep<-10?.65:1;
    const member=!!(g.flags?.joined_lantern || (g.society&&g.society.rank>0));
    const bonus=member?(rep>=80?.12:rep>=45?.06:0):0;
    const rebate=member?(rep>=80?.025:rep>=45?.012:0):0;
    return {...base,pay:Math.max(1,1+((base.pay||1)-1)*factor+bonus),discount:clamp((base.discount||0)*factor+rebate,0,.25),
      text:base.text+(factor<1?' Your badge remains earned, but strained relations reduce its benefits.':bonus?' Trusted colleagues receive additional support.':'')};
  };
  const prevPrice=G.priceOf;
  G.priceOf=function(g,kind,id,shop){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    let p=prevPrice(g,kind,id,shop);
    const rep=f=>(g.standing[f]||0);
    if(shop==='black'){
      if(rep('velvet')>=80)p*=.82;else if(rep('velvet')>=45)p*=.9;else if(rep('velvet')>=15)p*=.96;
      if(rep('velvet')< -40)p*=1.28;else if(rep('velvet')< -10)p*=1.11;
    }else if(shop==='exchange'||!shop){
      if(rep('selenne')< -40)p*=1.22;else if(rep('selenne')< -10)p*=1.10;
      if(kind==='stone'){
        if(rep('magisterium')>=80)p*=.91;else if(rep('magisterium')>=45)p*=.95;
        if(rep('magisterium')< -40)p*=1.12;
      }
      if(rep('varo')< -40 && kind==='item' && /^(wpn_|arm_)/.test(id))p*=1.09;
    }
    return Math.max(1,Math.round(p));
  };
  const prevSell=G.sellPrice;
  G.sellPrice=function(g,id){const p=prevSell(g,id);return Math.max(1,Math.round(p*((g.standing?.selenne||0)>=80?1.12:(g.standing?.selenne||0)>=45?1.06:1)));};
  const prevMake=G.makeBattle;
  G.makeBattle=function(g,enc,opts){const b=prevMake(g,enc,opts);if(b&&b.env){
    // Mutually respected, trusted relationships provide modest synergy in bond-based abilities.
    const close=Object.entries(g.npc||{}).filter(([n,x])=>!W.NPCS[n]?.system&&n!=='you'&&(x.aff||0)>=10&&(x.trust||0)>=10).length;
    b.env.bonds=Math.max(b.env.bonds||0,Math.min(5,2+close));
  }return b;};
  const prevAfter=G.afterBattle;
  G.afterBattle=function(g,b,sum){
    const result=prevAfter(g,b,sum);
    const isTrial=!!(b.enc?.trial || b.enc?.tutorial || /^assess_|^ptrial_/.test(b.encId||''));
    if(sum.win&&!isTrial&&g.socialOath&&!g.socialOath.ready){
      const o=g.socialOath;o.progress=Math.min(o.need,o.progress+1);o.ready=o.progress>=o.need;
      sum.notes.push(o.ready?`Your service pledge to ${factionName(o.f)} is complete. Report to their representative.`:`Pledged service (${factionName(o.f)}): ${o.progress}/${o.need} victories.`);
    }
    return result;
  };
  G.socialClaimReady=function(g,f){return (g.standing[f]||0)>=15;};
  G.socialFavorReady=function(g,f){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(!political.has(f)||!G.socialClaimReady(g,f))return false;
    return !own(g.socialFavors,f)||g.day-g.socialFavors[f]>=5;
  };
  G.socialFavor=function(g,f){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(!G.socialFavorReady(g,f))return {error:'Your faction support is not available yet. It renews five days after collection.'};
    const rep=g.standing[f]||0,rank=rep>=80?3:rep>=45?2:1;
    const notes=[];
    const give=(id,n)=>{G.give(g,id,n);notes.push(`${n} ${W.ITEMS[id]?.name||id}`);};
    const heal=p=>{g.hp=Math.min(G.maxHp(g),Math.max(g.hp,Math.round(G.maxHp(g)*p)));notes.push('medical attention');};
    const suspect=n=>{const before=g.suspicion||0;g.suspicion=Math.max(0,before-n);notes.push(`suspicion −${before-g.suspicion}`);};
    switch(f){
      case 'lantern':give('draught',1+(rank===3?1:0));if(rank>=2)give('core',1);break;
      case 'magisterium':give(rank===3&&g.rank>=1?'bcore':'core',rank===1?1:rank===2?2:rank===3&&g.rank>=1?1:3);break;
      case 'choir':heal(rank===1?.55:rank===2?.8:1);suspect(rank===1?8:rank===2?14:22);break;
      case 'velvet':give('smoke',rank);if(rank===3)give('salts',1);break;
      case 'varo':give('flask',rank);if(rank>=2)give('draught',1);break;
      case 'ithric':suspect(rank===1?10:rank===2?18:28);if(rank===3)give('salts',1);break;
      case 'selenne':g.marks+=rank*12;notes.push(`${rank*12} marks of merchant credit`);give('tonic',1);break;
    }
    g.socialFavors[f]=g.day;
    G.note(g,`${factionName(f)} extended ${PROF[f].name}: ${notes.join(', ')}.`);
    G.socialRecord(g,factionName(f),`${PROF[f].name}: ${notes.join(', ')}`);
    return {ok:true,notes,rank};
  };
  G.socialNPCFavorReady=function(g,n){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    const d=MAP[n];if(!d || (d.flagNot&&g.flags[d.flagNot]))return false;
    const rel=G.socialRelationship(g,n);
    return (rel.trust>=d.minTrust || rel.aff>=15) && (!own(g.socialNPCFavors,n)||g.day-g.socialNPCFavors[n]>=6);
  };
  G.socialNPCFavor=function(g,n){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(!G.socialNPCFavorReady(g,n))return {error:'This contact has no new favour to offer yet.'};
    const def=MAP[n],rel=G.socialRelationship(g,n), trusted=rel.trust>=def.minTrust;
    const notes=[];let line;
    if(trusted){
      for(const [id,count] of Object.entries(def.item||{})){G.give(g,id,count);notes.push(`${count} ${W.ITEMS[id]?.name||id}`);}
      if(def.marks){g.marks+=def.marks;notes.push(`${def.marks} marks`);}
      if(def.suspicion){const before=g.suspicion||0;g.suspicion=clamp(before+def.suspicion,0,100);notes.push(`suspicion ${g.suspicion-before}`);}
      if(def.heal){g.hp=Math.max(g.hp,Math.round(G.maxHp(g)*def.heal));notes.push('healing');}
      line=def.line;
    }else{
      const before=g.hp||0;g.hp=Math.max(g.hp,Math.round(G.maxHp(g)*.35));
      notes.push(g.hp>before?'healing and a warm meal':'a warm meal');
      line=`${npcName(n)} is glad to see you, but does not yet share their confidential business. You spend a comfortable hour together.`;
    }
    g.socialNPCFavors[n]=g.day;
    G.note(g,`Personal favour: ${npcName(n)} — ${notes.join(', ')}.`);
    G.socialRecord(g,npcName(n),`${trusted?'Trusted contact':'Friendly visit'}: ${notes.join(', ')}`);
    return {ok:true,line,notes,trusted};
  };
  G.socialMendReady=function(g,f){G.socialEnsure(g);return political.has(f)&&(g.standing[f]||0)<0&&g.marks>=30&&(!own(g.socialMends||{},f)||g.day-g.socialMends[f]>=4);};
  G.socialMend=function(g,f){
    if(!G.socialMendReady(g,f))return {error:'Reconciliation is unavailable right now.'};
    g.marks-=30;g.socialMends ||= {};g.socialMends[f]=g.day;
    const amt=Math.min(12,-(g.standing[f]||0));G.addStanding(g,f,amt);
    G.note(g,`Made amends with ${factionName(f)} through a costly public service.`);
    return {ok:true,amount:amt};
  };
  G.socialOathEligible=function(g,f){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(!political.has(f)||(g.standing[f]||0)<45)return false;
    if(f==='lantern'&&!g.flags.joined_lantern)return false;
    if(f==='velvet'&&!g.flags.undercroft_open)return false;
    if(['varo','ithric','selenne'].includes(f)&&!g.flags.slice_done)return false;
    return true;
  };
  G.socialStartOath=function(g,f){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(!G.socialOathEligible(g,f))return {error:'Earn Trusted standing and complete the required introductions before pledging.'};
    if(g.socialOath)return {error:'Finish the current service pledge first.'};
    if(g.socialAllegiance===f)return {error:'You have already taken this pledge.'};
    g.socialOath={f,progress:0,need:3,ready:false};
    G.note(g,`Began a service pledge to ${factionName(f)}: prove your commitment in three real fights.`);
    G.socialRecord(g,factionName(f),'A formal three-victory service pledge has begun.');
    return {ok:true};
  };
  G.socialFinishOath=function(g){
    G.socialEnsure(g);const o=g.socialOath;
    if(!o||!o.ready)return {error:'The pledge is not complete.'};
    const old=g.socialAllegiance, f=o.f;
    if(old&&old!==f){G.addStanding(g,old,-35);for(const [id,N]of Object.entries(W.NPCS))if(N.faction===old)G.socialChangeNPC(g,id,{trust:-8},'A broken oath');}
    const gain=Math.max(0,80-(g.standing[f]||0));
    if(gain)G.addStanding(g,f,gain);
    for(const [id,N]of Object.entries(W.NPCS))if(N.faction===f&&g.npc[id])G.socialChangeNPC(g,id,{trust:5},'The fulfilled oath');
    g.socialAllegiance=f;g.socialOath=null;
    G.note(g,`You formally swore service to ${factionName(f)}${old&&old!==f?`, abandoning ${factionName(old)}`:''}.`);
    G.socialRecord(g,factionName(f),'A formal allegiance was sworn.');
    return {ok:true,f,old};
  };
  G.socialWorldText=function(g){
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    const lines=[];
    const rep=f=>g.standing[f]||0;
    if(rep('lantern')>=45)lines.push('Lantern recruits lift their lamps when you pass; a veteran gives you a nod.');
    if(rep('lantern')<-40)lines.push('Outside the Lodge, hunters fall quiet when they notice your badge.');
    if(rep('choir')>=45)lines.push('The Dawn Choir names you among those who have helped Calderhal endure.');
    if(rep('choir')<-40)lines.push('Choir acolytes whisper your name in warning when you enter the square.');
    if(rep('velvet')>=45)lines.push('A sign in chalk near the alley tells you the Velvet Hand is watching out for you.');
    if(rep('velvet')<-40)lines.push('A black-market messenger turns away at the sight of you.');
    if(rep('selenne')>=45)lines.push('A Selenne merchant waves you toward the preferred customers’ table.');
    if(rep('selenne')<-40)lines.push('Selenne clerks suddenly have no records of your previous purchases.');
    if(rep('varo')>=45)lines.push('Varo guards recognize your face and make room at the wharf gate.');
    if(rep('varo')<-40)lines.push('Varo dockhands stop their conversation until you have passed.');
    if(rep('ithric')>=45)lines.push('An Ithric clerk greets you as a person of recognised standing.');
    if(rep('ithric')<-40)lines.push('An Ithric bailiff makes a conspicuous note of your arrival.');
    if(rep('magisterium')>=45)lines.push('A student from the Annex asks if you will share your field observations.');
    if(rep('magisterium')<-40)lines.push('Magisterium assistants keep their notebooks closed while you are nearby.');
    if(g.socialAllegiance)lines.push(`Word of your oath to ${factionName(g.socialAllegiance)} has spread through Calderhal.`);
    return lines.length?lines[(g.day+g.stats.fights)%lines.length]:null;
  };

  // Repeatable conversations may still offer services, but cannot manufacture infinite loyalty.
  const healOption=SC.anselm?.nodes.a.o.find(o=>o.d?.heal===true);
  const donationOption=SC.anselm?.nodes.a.o.find(o=>o.d?.marks===-25);
  const carouseOption=SC.nell?.nodes.a.o.find(o=>o.d?.marks===-20);
  if(healOption)healOption.d.socialRepeat='choir_heal';
  if(donationOption)donationOption.d.socialRepeat='choir_donation';
  if(carouseOption)carouseOption.d.socialRepeat='nell_carouse';
  if(SC.maud_chat?.nodes.her?.d)SC.maud_chat.nodes.her.d.flag='maud_confided';
  if(SC.council_chat?.nodes.sera?.d)SC.council_chat.nodes.sera.d.flag='seraphine_conversation';

  // Deliberate branching: affection may win warmth, trust is what opens sensitive doors.
  const addChoice=(scene,node,choice)=>{if(SC[scene]?.nodes[node]?.o)SC[scene].nodes[node].o.splice(Math.max(0,SC[scene].nodes[node].o.length-1),0,choice);};
  const addNode=(scene,id,node)=>{if(SC[scene])SC[scene].nodes[id]=node;};
  addChoice('maud_chat','a',{t:'Ask Maud for a private patrol recommendation.',if:{trust:{maud:12},not:'maud_recommended'},d:{flag:'maud_recommended',standing:{lantern:7},items:{draught:1},affinity:{maud:2}},g:'recommend'});
  addNode('maud_chat','recommend',{s:'maud',t:'Maud pulls a battered sheet from her desk. "I trust your judgement. Take the patrol routes the novices are not ready for. And take this, because your judgment is not the same as your hide."',n:'a'});
  addChoice('maud_chat','a',{t:'Ask Maud to share a meal after the shift.',if:{affinity:{maud:15},trustMax:{maud:11},not:'maud_meal'},d:{flag:'maud_meal',heal:.55,affinity:{maud:1}},g:'meal'});
  addNode('maud_chat','meal',{s:'maud',t:'She offers a stool and a very bad stew. "You are good company," she says. "Do not confuse that with me giving you the patrol keys."',n:'a'});
  addChoice('hale','a',{t:'Ask Hale for confidential research notes.',if:{trust:{hale:10},not:'hale_research'},d:{flag:'hale_research',cores:2,affinity:{hale:2}},g:'research'});
  addNode('hale','research',{s:'hale',t:'"I know what you can be trusted with," Hale says, passing you a sealed packet. "It is much less than everything. Still, more than most."',n:'a'});
  addChoice('anselm','a',{t:'Ask Anselm to attest to your conduct.',if:{trust:{anselm:10},not:'anselm_vouched'},d:{flag:'anselm_vouched',suspicion:-18,standing:{choir:5}},g:'attest'});
  addNode('anselm','attest',{s:'anselm',t:'He writes a careful note for the wardens. "This is my word, not a pardon. Keep deserving it."',n:'a'});
  addChoice('nell','a',{t:'Ask Nell about trouble nobody will say aloud.',if:{trust:{nell:10},not:'nell_warning'},d:{flag:'nell_warning',items:{smoke:1},standing:{velvet:3}},g:'warning'});
  addNode('nell','warning',{s:'nell',t:'Nell pushes a small packet across the table. "Someone has been asking where you sleep. Do not make it easy for them. And do not quote me."',n:'a'});
  addChoice('sixpence','a',{t:'Ask Sixpence to put a good word in with the Hand.',if:{trust:{sixpence:10},not:'sixpence_reference'},d:{flag:'sixpence_reference',standing:{velvet:6},items:{smoke:1}},g:'reference'});
  addNode('sixpence','reference',{s:'sixpence',t:'"You deliver what you promise. That is a better reference than a hundred stolen seals," Sixpence says, and marks your name in a private ledger.',n:'a'});
  addChoice('council_chat','a',{t:'Ask Seraphine to intervene with the watch.',if:{trust:{seraphine:10},not:'seraphine_writ'},d:{flag:'seraphine_writ',suspicion:-15,standing:{ithric:4}},g:'writ'});
  addNode('council_chat','writ',{s:'seraphine',t:'Seraphine signs a narrow writ. "You have earned a hearing. Do not mistake it for immunity."',n:'a'});
  // Ottoline's short explanatory dialogue originally ended without options.
  if(SC.ottoline_stones?.nodes.c){
    SC.ottoline_stones.nodes.c.o=[
      {t:'Return her appraisal with an honest account of the flaws.',if:{flag:'met_ottoline',not:'otto_truth'},d:{flag:'otto_truth',trust:{ottoline:10},affinity:{ottoline:2}},g:'truth'},
      {t:'Ask about the private reserve.',if:{trust:{ottoline:10},not:'otto_reserve'},d:{flag:'otto_reserve',items:{tonic:1},marks:15},g:'reserve'},
      {t:'Thank her and return to the Exchange.',g:'end'}
    ];
    addNode('ottoline_stones','truth',{s:'ottoline',t:'You point out the cracked bezel that makes the stone less valuable. Ottoline looks from it to you. "You could have sold me an inflated estimate. Very well. I trust you with a little more than numbers."',n:'c'});

  }
  // One-time social choices that actually establish trust through observed choices.
  addChoice('council_chat','a',{t:'Give Petra a truthful accounting of the Heart-Shard.',if:{flag:'matriarch_dead',not:'petra_accounts'},d:{flag:'petra_accounts',trust:{petra:10},affinity:{petra:2}},g:'accounts'});

  addChoice('caspian_debt','a',{t:'Promise to judge Caspian by his own deeds, not his father’s.',if:{not:'caspian_fair'},d:{flag:'caspian_fair',trust:{caspian:9},affinity:{caspian:3}},g:'b'});

  const prevCheck=G.check;
  G.check=function(g,c){
    if(!prevCheck(g,c))return false;
    if(!c)return true;
    G.socialEnsure(g);
    if(c.trust)for(const n in c.trust)if((g.npc[n]?.trust||0)<c.trust[n])return false;
    if(c.trustMax)for(const n in c.trustMax)if((g.npc[n]?.trust||0)>c.trustMax[n])return false;
    if(c.affinityMax)for(const n in c.affinityMax)if((g.npc[n]?.aff||0)>c.affinityMax[n])return false;
    if(c.standingMax)for(const f in c.standingMax)if((g.standing[f]||0)>c.standingMax[f])return false;
    return true;
  };

  // UI: interactive faction and NPC panels, in-world access, and a readable ledger.
  const fTitle=f=>W.FACTIONS[f]?.name||f;
  UI.socialFactionSheet=function(f){

    }),'ghost'));

    }else if(p.tab==='people'){
      const cards=[...pane.querySelectorAll('.person')];let i=0;
      for(const [n,N]of Object.entries(W.NPCS)){
        if(N.system||N.narrator||n==='you')continue;

        const r=G.socialRelationship(g,n),d=MAP[n];
        card.append(h('div.social-chip',r.tone),h('p.social-detail',r.detail),
          ...(d?[h('p.dim.small',`Trusted contact (${d.minTrust} trust): ${d.reward}`)]:[]),
          UI.btn('Relationship details',()=>UI.socialNPCSheet(n),'tiny ghost'));
      }
      pane.append(h('p.dim.small','Trust and affinity do different things. Talk honestly and finish personal stories to earn trust; friendliness alone will not unlock confidences.'));
    }
  };
  const prevSocietySheet=UI.societySheet;
  UI.societySheet=function(){
    prevSocietySheet();
    const card=document.querySelector('#sheet .sheet-body');if(!card)return;
    const rep=(UI.g.standing||{}).lantern||0,x=G.societyDef(UI.g);

      ...(rep<-10?[h('p.warn.small','Strained standing reduces your badge privileges. Report for work or make amends to restore goodwill.')]:[]));
  };

  const prevBreakthrough=G.breakthrough;
  G.breakthrough=function(g){
    if(g.rank!==2)return prevBreakthrough(g);
    g.rank=3;g.masteryFocus=[];g.lastChange=G.changeFor(g,G.allEssences(g).map(e=>e.id));
    for(const id of G.abilityIds(g)){
      const st=G.abilityState(g,id);st.hollow=Math.round(((st.hollow||0)+(st.cored||0))*.6);st.cored=0;st.rank=3;st.train=0;
    }
    G.fullHeal(g);G.note(g,'Gold-rank achieved: your powers have changed again.');
    g.legacyGoldTraitPending=true;
    return {rank:3,change:g.lastChange};
  };
  G.act3Stage=g=>g.quests?.act3_horizons?.stage||0;
  G.act3Advance=function(g,stage){
    G.legacyEnsure(g); if(!g.quests.act3_horizons)G.startQuest(g,'act3_horizons');
    if(stage>G.act3Stage(g)){
      G.setStage(g,'act3_horizons',stage);
      G.note(g,W.QUESTS.act3_horizons.stages[stage]);
    }
  };
  G.act3Resolve=function(g,decision){
    if(g.rank<3||G.act3Stage(g)<5)return {error:'The ending is not yet ready.'};
    if(!['seal','share','study'].includes(decision))return {error:'Unknown choice.'};
    g.flags.act3_done=true;g.act3Ending=decision;
    if(decision==='seal'){G.addStanding(g,'choir',14);G.addStanding(g,'lantern',10);}
    else if(decision==='share'){G.addStanding(g,'varo',8);G.addStanding(g,'ithric',7);G.addStanding(g,'velvet',5);}
    else{G.addStanding(g,'magisterium',18);G.addStanding(g,'choir',-10);}
    G.act3Advance(g,6);G.finishQuest(g,'act3_horizons');
    G.note(g,'Act III complete: '+({seal:'The seed was sealed beneath the Dawn Choir.',share:'The seed was placed under a council of rival factions.',study:'The seed was given to the Magisterium for guarded study.'}[decision]));
    return {ending:decision};
  };

  // ---- Part II: AI personalities and better tactical target selection ----
  const prevEnemy=G.enemyUnit;
  G.enemyUnit=function(g,b,id,index,level){
    const u=prevEnemy(g,b,id,index,level),d=W.ENEMIES[id]||{};
    u.tacticalRole=d.role||((d.tags||[]).includes('leader')?'commander':(d.tags||[]).includes('beast')?'predator':d.a?.[2]>d.a?.[0]+2?'caster':'skirmisher');
    return u;
  };
  const prevTarget=AI.pickEnemyTarget;
  AI.pickEnemyTarget=function(b,u,list,opts){
    if(u.isPlayer||!u.tacticalRole||u.kind==='familiar')return prevTarget(b,u,list,opts);
    const candidates=list.filter(t=>t.alive&&!t.flags.untargetableByAI&&t.id!==opts?.avoid);
    if(!candidates.length)return prevTarget(b,u,list,opts);
    const role=u.tacticalRole;
    const rate=t=>{
      const hp=K.hpPct(t);let v=0.25+b.rng()*.32;
      if(role==='predator')v+=(1-hp)*2.5+(K.hasStatus(t,'exposed')?.8:0)+((t.row==='rear')?.4:0);
      else if(role==='caster')v+=(K.hasStatus(t,'silenced')?-.25:0)+((K.hasStatus(t,'marked')||K.hasStatus(t,'rattled'))?.7:0)+(t.isPlayer?.25:0);
      else if(role==='commander')v+=(t.isPlayer?.6:0)+(K.hasStatus(t,'exposed')?.5:0);
      else if(role==='hunter')v+=(1-hp)*1.3+((K.hasStatus(t,'marked')||K.hasStatus(t,'spotted'))?1:0)+(t.isPlayer?.4:0);
      else v+=(1-hp)*1.1;
      if(t.kind==='decoy')v-=.5;
      return v;
    };
    return candidates.map(t=>({t,value:rate(t)})).sort((a,b)=>b.value-a.value)[0].t;
  };
  const prevScore=AI.score;
  AI.score=function(b,u,slot,opts){
    const r=prevScore(b,u,slot,opts);if(!r||u.isPlayer||!u.tacticalRole)return r;
    const role=u.tacticalRole,p=AI.profile(slot.def),n=K.enemies(b,u).length,allies=K.allies(b,u,true);
    if(role==='predator')r.score+=p.dmg*.5+(r.target&&K.hpPct(r.target)<.45?.6:0)-p.setup*.3;
    else if(role==='commander')r.score+=p.shield*(allies.some(x=>K.hpPct(x)<.6)?1.7:1)+p.buff*(slot.uses?0:.65)+p.control*.35;
    else if(role==='caster')r.score+=p.debuff*(n>1?.7:.35)+p.control*.55+p.setup*(slot.uses?-.4:.65);
    else if(role==='hunter')r.score+=p.dmg*.27+p.control*.25+(r.target&&K.hasStatus(r.target,'marked')?.5:0);
    return r;
  };

  // ---- Part III: Companion bond progression with choices, effects, and consequences ----
  const COMP={
    caspian:{threshold:10,title:'The Varo Ultimatum',left:'Duty to the docks',right:'Duty to the house',desc:'Caspian must choose between the people of the wharves and the name on his father’s ring.',leftText:'Caspian chooses to protect the docks. He becomes a more tenacious defender.',rightText:'Caspian commits to his family. His strikes become sharper.',leftMods:{armor:.16,damage_reduction:.08},rightMods:{damage:.16,crit:.06},leftAb:'abl_shield_starter',rightAb:'abl_blade_avenger'},
    maud:{threshold:8,title:'The Warden’s Lantern',left:'Save the recruits',right:'Hold the breach',desc:'Maud receives two calls: a stranded squad of recruits and a breach that threatens the city.',leftText:'Maud goes after the recruits. Her protection becomes steadier.',rightText:'Maud holds the breach. Her counters grow stronger.',leftMods:{max_health:.18,armor:.08},rightMods:{damage:.13,crit:.05},leftAb:'abl_lantern_mender',rightAb:'abl_shield_avenger'},
    gannet:{threshold:8,title:'The Weatherwitch’s Debt',left:'Shelter the landing',right:'Hunt the captors',desc:'Gannet can turn the coming storm toward the sick village or the surviving circle that chained her.',leftText:'Gannet saves the landing. Her healing and defenses deepen.',rightText:'Gannet hunts her captors. Her stormcraft becomes lethal.',leftMods:{spell_power:.09,magic_resist:.13},rightMods:{spell_power:.22,crit:.04},leftAb:'abl_rain_mender',rightAb:'abl_lightning_tempest'}
  };
  G.bondState=(g,id)=>{G.legacyEnsure(g);return g.legacy.companion[id]|| (g.legacy.companion[id]={wins:0,route:null});};
  G.bondAvailable=(g,id)=>!!COMP[id]&&G.bondState(g,id).wins>=3&&(g.npc[id]?.trust||0)>=COMP[id].threshold &&!G.bondState(g,id).route;
  G.completeBond=function(g,id,route){
    if(!['left','right'].includes(route)||!G.bondAvailable(g,id))return {error:'Fight alongside this companion three times and earn their trust first.'};
    const st=G.bondState(g,id);st.route=route;
    G.addStanding(g,W.NPCS[id]?.faction||'lantern',route==='left'?4:3);
    G.note(g,COMP[id][route+'Text']);
    return {message:COMP[id][route+'Text']};
  };
  const priorAlly=G.allyUnit;
  G.allyUnit=function(g,b,id){
    const u=priorAlly(g,b,id),def=COMP[id];if(!def)return u;
    const st=G.bondState(g,id);
    if(st.wins>=2)K.addMod(b,u,'max_health',Math.min(.12,st.wins*.015),'combat',null);
    if(st.route){
      const mods=def[st.route+'Mods']||{};for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(mods))K.addMod(b,u,k,v,'combat',null);
      const ability=def[st.route+'Ab'];if(C().abilities[ability]&&!u.abilities.some(a=>a.id===ability))K.learn(u,ability,Math.max(1,g.rank));
    }
    return u;
  };
  const priorBattle=G.resolveBattle;
  G.resolveBattle=function(g,b){
    G.legacyEnsure(g);
    const practice=!!g.legacy.practiceSnapshot && !!W.ENCOUNTERS[b.encId]?.practice;
    const oldPractice=practice?g.legacy.practiceSnapshot:null;
    const sum=priorBattle(g,b);
    if(practice){
      // Exact economic/story rollback: the hall never grants drops, mastery, reputation, vows or death.
      const before=JSON.parse(oldPractice);
      for(const k of Object.keys(g))if(k!=='pendingFight'&&k!=='pendingCombat')delete g[k];
      Object.assign(g,before);
      g.pendingFight=null;g.pendingCombat=null;
      G.legacyEnsure(g);g.legacy.practiceSnapshot=null;g.legacy.practiceCount=(g.legacy.practiceCount||0)+1;
      sum.loot=[];sum.training=[];sum.notes=['Training Hall simulation. Health, supplies, progression, reputation, and rewards restored.'];
      return sum;
    }
    if(sum.win && !b.enc?.trial && !b.enc?.tutorial && !b.enc?.duel && g.party && COMP[g.party]){
      const st=G.bondState(g,g.party);st.wins++;
      if(st.wins===3)sum.notes.push(`${W.ALLIES[g.party].name}: three battles together. A bond quest may now be possible if trust is sufficient.`);
    }
    if(sum.win){
      if(b.encId==='wake_outpost')G.act3Advance(g,1);
      if(b.encId==='wake_pact' && g.legacy.act3SideFaction&&!g.legacy.act3SideComplete){
        g.legacy.act3SideComplete=true;g.flags.cantor_weakness=true;
        G.addStanding(g,g.legacy.act3SideFaction,10);
        sum.notes.push(`Cantor dispatch recovered. ${W.FACTIONS[g.legacy.act3SideFaction].name} +10; the final boss begins wounded.`);
      }
      if(['wake_bridge','wake_ruse'].includes(b.encId))G.act3Advance(g,3);
      if(b.encId==='wake_cantor'){g.flags.act3_engine_stilled=true;G.act3Advance(g,4);sum.notes.push('The last engine is silenced. A Gold trial now awaits your mastery.');}
      if(b.encId==='trial_gold'&&G.act3Stage(g)>=4&&g.rank===2){G.breakthrough(g);G.act3Advance(g,5);sum.notes.push('Gold-rank reached. Your last physical change and a new Gold trait are available.');}
      if(g.pendingHollowSide && b.encId===g.pendingHollowSide.enc){
        const side=g.pendingHollowSide;g.legacy.hollowChoices[side.key]=side.route;
        G.legacyMaterial(g,side.material||'silver_filament',2);sum.loot.push('2 '+G.materialName(side.material||'silver_filament'));g.pendingHollowSide=null;
      }
      const mat={wake_outpost:'silver_filament',wake_bridge:'silver_filament',wake_ruse:'silver_filament',wake_approach:'void_glass',wake_choir:'void_glass',wake_cantor:'void_glass',prior:'ember_dust',matriarch:'glass_shard',cinderknight:'ember_dust',gulls:'storm_essence',tidecaller:'storm_essence'}[b.encId];
      if(mat){G.legacyMaterial(g,mat,b.enc?.boss?4:1);sum.loot.push((b.enc?.boss?4:1)+' '+G.materialName(mat));}
      // Regional salvage has a bounded chance rather than a guaranteed payout per fight.
      else if(!b.enc?.trial&&!b.enc?.tutorial&&!b.enc?.duel && G.rng(g,99001+g.stats.wins)()<.3){
        const name=g.node==='ash'?'ember_dust':g.node==='scar'?'glass_shard':g.node==='flats'||g.node==='wrecks'?'storm_essence':'iron_scrap';
        G.legacyMaterial(g,name,1);sum.loot.push(G.materialName(name));
      }
    }
    return sum;
  };

  // An announced, slow row attack that can be interrupted or mitigated, rather than pure stat inflation.
  const CANTICLE={id:'eb_boss_last_canticle',name:'Canticle of Unmaking',type:'special_ability',target:'enemy_row',tempo:'slow',cooldown:4,cost:{mana:8},tags:['Spell'],summary:'Slow, telegraphed spirit blast against a row, inflicting Rattled.',effects:[
    {op:'damage',type:'spirit',power:.78,scale:{spirit:1}},
    {op:'apply_status',status:'rattled',stacks:2,duration:2}
  ]};
  const prevMakeBattle=G.makeBattle;
  G.makeBattle=function(g,id,options){
    if(g.party && (g.npc?.[g.party]?.trust||0)<-25){
      G.note(g,`${W.ALLIES[g.party]?.name||'Your companion'} has withdrawn from your party after the breach of trust.`);g.party=null;
    }
    const b=prevMakeBattle(g,id,options);
    if(id==='wake_cantor'){
      const boss=b.units.find(u=>u.fid==='wake_cantor');
      if(boss){
        K.learn(boss,CANTICLE,Math.max(1,g.rank));
        if(g.flags.cantor_weakness){boss.hp=Math.round(boss.hp*.76);K.say(b,'The recovered dispatch exposed a weakness in the Cantor’s ritual. The seed flickers.','good');}
        AI.plan(b,boss);
      }
    }
    return b;
  };
  // ---- Part IV: Build laboratory (true practice: save-exact economic and progression rollback) ----
  for(const id of ['practice_strike','practice_guard','practice_control','practice_boss'])W.ENCOUNTERS[id].practice=true;
  G.startPractice=function(g,id){
    if(!W.ENCOUNTERS[id]?.practice)return {error:'Unknown training drill.'};
    if(g.pendingFight||g.pendingCombat)return {error:'Finish your current encounter first.'};
    G.legacyEnsure(g);
    const clone=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(g));delete clone.legacy.practiceSnapshot;
    g.legacy.practiceSnapshot=JSON.stringify(clone);
    return {ok:true};
  };

  // ---- Part V: Crafting and salvage; real equipment effects through existing engine operations ----
  const MAT={iron_scrap:'Forgebound Scrap',ember_dust:'Ember Dust',glass_shard:'Glasswater Shard',storm_essence:'Stormglass Salt',void_glass:'Void Glass',silver_filament:'Silver Filament'};
  G.materialName=k=>MAT[k]||k;
  G.legacyMaterial=function(g,key,n){G.legacyEnsure(g);if(!MAT[key])return;g.legacy.materials[key]=(g.legacy.materials[key]||0)+(n||1);};
  Object.assign(W.ITEMS,{
    wpn_coalfang:{name:'Coal-Fang Saber',kind:'weapon',price:560,desc:'Fire-linked weapon: +31% weapon power; hits kindle Burning (1).',mods:{weapon_power:.31},onHit:[{op:'apply_status',status:'burning',stacks:1}]},
    wpn_verdic:{name:'Thornwake Seeker',kind:'weapon',price:680,desc:'A hunter’s edge: +25% weapon power and +15% critical chance; hits apply Marked.',mods:{weapon_power:.25,crit:.15},onHit:[{op:'apply_status',status:'marked',stacks:1}]},
    wpn_sunglass:{name:'Dawn-Glass Staff',kind:'weapon',price:580,desc:'A refracting focus: +24% spell power, +8% critical chance.',mods:{spell_power:.24,crit:.08},wtype:'light'},
    arm_hollowguard:{name:'Hollowguard Harness',kind:'armor',price:630,desc:'+19% armor, +12% max health and +12% affliction resistance.',mods:{armor:.19,max_health:.12,affliction_resist:.12}},
    arm_stormweave:{name:'Stormweave Mantle',kind:'armor',price:550,desc:'+10% speed, +15% magic resistance, +12% lightning resistance.',mods:{speed:.10,magic_resist:.15,lightning_resist:.12}},
    charm_vowthread:{name:'Vowthread Charm',kind:'charm',price:490,desc:'+10% max health, +9% spell power, +5% evasion.',mods:{max_health:.10,spell_power:.09,evasion:.05}}
  });
  const RECIPES={
    wpn_coalfang:{marks:145,core:6,mat:{ember_dust:3,iron_scrap:2}},
    wpn_verdic:{marks:210,bcore:2,mat:{silver_filament:4,iron_scrap:2}},
    wpn_sunglass:{marks:185,bcore:1,mat:{glass_shard:3,storm_essence:2}},
    arm_hollowguard:{marks:200,bcore:2,mat:{void_glass:3,iron_scrap:3}},
    arm_stormweave:{marks:175,core:5,mat:{storm_essence:3,silver_filament:2}},
    charm_vowthread:{marks:125,bcore:1,mat:{silver_filament:3,void_glass:1}}
  };
  G.craftRecipes=RECIPES;
  G.craftCheck=(g,id)=>{
    G.legacyEnsure(g);const r=RECIPES[id];if(!r)return 'Unknown recipe';
    if(g.marks<r.marks)return 'Not enough marks';
    if(G.count(g,'core')<(r.core||0))return 'Not enough Iron Cores';
    if(G.count(g,'bcore')<(r.bcore||0))return 'Not enough Bronze Cores';
    for(const [mat,n] of Object.entries(r.mat))if((g.legacy.materials[mat]||0)<n)return 'Missing '+G.materialName(mat);
    return null;
  };
  G.craft=function(g,id){const error=G.craftCheck(g,id);if(error)return {error};const r=RECIPES[id];
    g.marks-=r.marks;G.give(g,'core',-(r.core||0));G.give(g,'bcore',-(r.bcore||0));
    for(const [m,n] of Object.entries(r.mat))g.legacy.materials[m]-=n;
    G.give(g,id,1);G.note(g,'Crafted '+W.ITEMS[id].name+'.');return {item:id};
  };

  // ---- Part VI: Optional dungeon passages and non-combat utility ----
  const PASSAGES={
    glass:{1:{name:'Prismatic Gallery',battle:'salt1',material:'glass_shard'},3:{name:'Behind the Mirror',battle:'salt2',material:'glass_shard'}},
    cinder:{1:{name:'Beneath the Penitents',battle:'cloister1',material:'ember_dust'},4:{name:'The Cold Ash Stairs',battle:'cloister2',material:'ember_dust'}},
    thornwake:{0:{name:'The Door of Names',battle:'wake_ruse',material:'silver_filament'},1:{name:'The Sealed Archive',battle:'wake_choir',material:'void_glass'}}
  };
  G.hollowSide=function(g,did,room,route){
    G.legacyEnsure(g);const p=PASSAGES[did]?.[room];if(!p)return {error:'No passage here.'};
    const key=did+':'+room;if(g.legacy.hollowChoices[key])return {error:'This passage has already been explored.'};
    if(route==='scout'){
      if(!G.hasUtil(g,'scout')&&!G.socialTags(g).has('Lore'))return {error:'Requires Scout or Lore.'};
      g.legacy.hollowChoices[key]='scout';G.legacyMaterial(g,p.material,1);G.note(g,`You studied ${p.name}, finding ${G.materialName(p.material)} without combat.`);return {message:'A hidden route yields '+G.materialName(p.material)+'.'};
    }
    if(route==='safe'){
      g.legacy.hollowChoices[key]='safe';g.hp=Math.min(G.maxHp(g),g.hp+Math.round(G.maxHp(g)*.15));
      G.note(g,`You took the patient route through ${p.name}.`);return {message:'You pass safely and recover 15% health.'};
    }
    if(route==='fight'){g.pendingHollowSide={key,route,enc:p.battle,material:p.material};return {enc:p.battle};}
    return {error:'Unknown passage.'};
  };

  const SIGILS={
    glass:{name:'The Reflecting Lock',options:['The Mirror','The Flame','The Crown'],solution:'The Mirror',hint:'The seal answers only to a true reflection.',mat:'glass_shard'},
    cinder:{name:'The Penitent Cipher',options:['The Oath','The Ash','The Dawn'],solution:'The Dawn',hint:'A fire can end without ending the morning.',mat:'ember_dust'},
    thornwake:{name:'The Final Name',options:['Silence','Memory','Power'],solution:'Memory',hint:'A forgotten name is restored by remembrance, not force.',mat:'void_glass'}
  };
  G.solveHollowSigil=function(g,did,choice){
    G.legacyEnsure(g);const p=SIGILS[did];if(!p)return {error:'No sigil in this Hollow.'};
    const key=did+':sigil';if(g.legacy.hollowChoices[key])return {error:'This lock has been solved.'};
    if(choice!==p.solution){g.hp=Math.max(1,g.hp-Math.round(G.maxHp(g)*.08));G.note(g,`A mistaken answer to ${p.name} drew a warding pulse.`);return {message:'The wrong symbol burns your hand: −8% maximum health.'};}
    g.legacy.hollowChoices[key]='solved';G.legacyMaterial(g,p.mat,2);G.note(g,`You deciphered ${p.name}; you recovered a pair of rare materials.`);
    return {message:'The sigil opens. You recover 2 '+G.materialName(p.mat)+'.'};
  };
  // ---- Part VII: World changes, reflecting allegiances and origins ----
  G.cityState=function(g){
    const e=g.act3Ending,al=g.socialAllegiance||g.ending;
    const flags=g.flags||{};
    const line=e==='seal'?'The Choir has sealed the last seed. Pilgrims place candles in windows at night.':e==='share'?'A mixed council now holds the last seed. The city is full of arguments; for once, they are public.':e==='study'?'Magisterium wards glow over the quays. Scholars and protestors argue beside the bridges.':
      flags.act2_done?'With the Ashen Circle broken here, traders are reopening routes beyond Calderhal.':flags.petra_exposed?'The council is uneasy. People speak more freely about old secrets.':'The city is uncertain whether its powerful protect it or profit from it.';
    const background={dockhand:'At the docks, old friends still call you by the name you had before awakening.',foundling:'The chapel children greet you by your old household nickname.',heir:'The noble clerks remember the scandal even when they bow.',smuggler:'The customs officers study you twice before looking away.',dropout:'A few apprentices still whisper about the laboratory accident.',hunter:'At the Lodge, the new trackers ask which paths are safe.'}[g.origin]||'';
    const pledge=al==='velvet'?'Velvet informants move quietly through the crowds.':al==='choir'?'Dawn Choir volunteers are visible around the sick ward.':al==='magisterium'?'Magisterium students have begun cataloguing old blooms.':al==='lantern'?'Lantern recruits gather beneath fresh banners.':al==='ithric'?'Watch patrols carry House Ithric colours.':al==='selenne'?'The Exchange has new stalls bearing the Selenne seal.':al==='varo'?'Varo dock-workers have returned to the warehouses.':'';
    return [line,pledge,background].filter(Boolean);
  };

  // Final political decision changes access to goods and recovery, not only flavour text.
  const oldPrice=G.priceOf;
  G.priceOf=function(g,kind,id,shop){
    let value=oldPrice(g,kind,id,shop);
    if(g.act3Ending==='share'&&shop!=='black')value=Math.round(value*.95);
    if(g.act3Ending==='study'&&kind==='stone'&&shop!=='black')value=Math.round(value*.93);
    if(g.origin==='dropout'&&['wpn_sunglass','charm_vowthread'].includes(id))value=Math.round(value*.94);
    return Math.max(1,value);
  };
  const oldShopStock=G.shopStock;
  G.shopStock=function(g,where){
    const stock=oldShopStock(g,where);
    if(where==='exchange' || !where){
      if(g.act3Ending==='seal')stock.items=Array.from(new Set([...stock.items,'salts','charm_ward']));
      if(g.act3Ending==='share')stock.items=Array.from(new Set([...stock.items,'arm_hollowguard']));
      if(g.act3Ending==='study')stock.items=Array.from(new Set([...stock.items,'wpn_sunglass','bcore']));
    }
    return stock;
  };
  const oldRest=G.rest;
  G.rest=function(g){oldRest(g);if(g.act3Ending==='seal')g.suspicion=Math.max(0,g.suspicion-4);};
  // ---- UI: new fully integrated screens ----
  UI.frontier=function(){UI.go('frontier');};
  S.frontier=function(root){
    const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);
    root.append(UI.header('Thornwake March',`Act III · ${G.rankName(g)}`));
    const body=h('div.pad'),stage=G.act3Stage(g);
    if(!g.flags.act2_done||g.rank<2){body.append(h('p.dim','Complete Act II and reach Silver rank before the outer March can be opened.'));}
    else {
      if(!g.quests.act3_horizons)G.startQuest(g,'act3_horizons');
      body.append(h('p.flavor','Beyond the Saltgrave, another cloister is burning without flame. People who go there come back having forgotten their own names.'),h('div.hint',W.QUESTS.act3_horizons.stages[stage]));
      if(stage===0)body.append(act('Search the abandoned watch','Find the missing surveyors; three hostile scouts remain.',()=>UI.startCombat('wake_outpost',()=>UI.go('frontier')),'eye'));
      if(stage===1){
        body.append(h('h3','The watchtower evidence'),h('p','The missing surveyors left three conflicting reports. Choose whom you believe; the route will change, and so will your allies.'));
        body.append(act('Trust the Magisterium charts','A methodical passage. Magisterium +6.',()=>{g.legacy.act3Route='charts';G.addStanding(g,'magisterium',6);G.act3Advance(g,2);next(g);},'book'));
        body.append(act('Trust the smugglers’ bearings','A hidden passage. Velvet Hand +6.',()=>{g.legacy.act3Route='smugglers';G.addStanding(g,'velvet',6);G.act3Advance(g,2);next(g);},'eye'));
        if((g.npc.maud?.trust||0)>=10||g.origin==='hunter')body.append(act('Follow Maud’s patrol route','A direct march. Lantern +6.',()=>{g.legacy.act3Route='lantern';G.addStanding(g,'lantern',6);G.act3Advance(g,2);next(g);},'shield'));
      }
      if(stage===2){const enc=g.legacy.act3Route==='smugglers'?'wake_ruse':'wake_bridge';body.append(act('Cross the disputed causeway',`Encounter: ${W.ENCOUNTERS[enc].name}`,()=>UI.startCombat(enc,()=>UI.go('frontier')),'sword'));}
      if(stage>=2&&stage<=3&&!g.legacy.act3SideComplete){
        body.append(h('h3','Optional: The Cantor’s dispatch'),h('p.dim.small','A missing courier carries the engine’s weakness. Choose which faction receives the evidence before confronting its escort. Completing this mission weakens the final boss.'));
        for(const [f,title] of [['choir','Dawn Choir: safeguard the wounded'],['velvet','Velvet Hand: intercept the dispatch'],['magisterium','Magisterium: decode its cipher']]){
          if(!g.legacy.act3SideFaction)body.append(act(title,`Recover the courier’s dispatch; ${W.FACTIONS[f].name} will remember it.`,()=>{
            g.legacy.act3SideFaction=f;UI.save();UI.startCombat('wake_pact',()=>UI.go('frontier'));
          },'book'));
        }
        if(g.legacy.act3SideFaction)body.append(act('Continue the dispatch mission',`Chosen ally: ${W.FACTIONS[g.legacy.act3SideFaction].name}`,()=>UI.startCombat('wake_pact',()=>UI.go('frontier')),'sword'));
      }
      if(stage===3)body.append(act('Enter the Hollow of Last Names','Three chambers, optional side passages, and the last Cantor.',()=>{g.dungeon='thornwake';g.loc='hollow';UI.save();UI.go('hollow');},'eye'));
      if(stage===4){const info=G.masteryInfo(g);body.append(h('p.small',`Gold mastery: ${info.mature.length}/${G.masteryTarget} mature abilities. Cover all five essences.`));
        if(G.trialReady(g))body.append(act('Return to the Magisterium for the Gold Trial','The Gold Reflection awaits in the Annex.',()=>UI.go('loc',{id:'magisterium'}),'star'));
        else body.append(UI.btn('Choose mastery portfolio',()=>UI.go('train'),'wide'));
      }
      if(stage===5){
        body.append(h('h3','The final seed'),h('p','The seed is yours, but the city will live with your decision.'));
        const endingOptions=[['seal','Seal it with the Dawn Choir','Safer, but it becomes a holy relic.'],['share','Place it under joint council custody','Risk political conflict, prevent exclusive control.'],['study','Entrust it to the Magisterium','Knowledge grows, alongside dangerous ambition.']];
        for(const [id,title,sub] of endingOptions)body.append(act(title,sub,()=>UI.confirm(title,sub+' This is a permanent ending choice.','Decide',()=>{const r=G.act3Resolve(g,id);if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');UI.save();UI.go('frontier');}),'star'));
      }
      if(stage===6){body.append(h('div.rankbox',h('h3','Act III complete'),h('p','The last seed has passed into history. Calderhal remembers your choice.')),
        h('p.small',`Final decision: ${{seal:'Sealed beneath Choir protection',share:'Shared between the city’s factions',study:'Entrusted to guarded research'}[g.act3Ending]||'Unrecorded'}.`));}
    }
    body.append(UI.btn('Return to Calderhal',()=>{g.loc='town';UI.save();UI.go('town');},'wide ghost'));
    root.append(body,UI.nav('explore'));
  };
  UI.legacyHallSheet=function(){
    const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);
    const body=[h('p','These encounters are practice illusions. You may test combinations, targeting, reactions, and resource efficiency without permanently spending supplies or changing your character.'),h('p.dim.small',`Practice battles completed: ${g.legacy.practiceCount||0}. The ordinary fight summary and combat log are available afterward.`)];
    for(const [id,description] of Object.entries({practice_strike:'Aggressive pursuer — positioning and damage',practice_guard:'Fortified sentinel — armor and attrition',practice_control:'Caster and scout — interruption and control',practice_boss:'Mirror champion — endurance and counterplay'}))
      body.push(UI.btn(W.ENCOUNTERS[id].name,()=>{const r=G.startPractice(g,id);if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');UI.closeSheet();UI.startCombat(id,()=>UI.go('loc',{id:'magisterium'}));},'wide ghost'),h('p.dim.small',description));
    UI.sheet('Magisterium Combat Hall',body);
  };
  UI.legacyCraftSheet=function(){
    const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);
    const body=[h('p','Use salvaged Hollow materials, cores, and marks to forge equipment with tactical properties.'),h('h4','Your materials')];
    for(const [m,name] of Object.entries(MAT))body.push(h('div.kv',h('span',name),h('b',String(g.legacy.materials[m]||0))));
    body.push(h('h4','Recipes'));
    for(const [id,r] of Object.entries(RECIPES)){
      const item=W.ITEMS[id],parts=Object.entries(r.mat).map(([m,n])=>`${n} ${MAT[m]}`);
      if(r.core)parts.push(`${r.core} Iron Cores`);if(r.bcore)parts.push(`${r.bcore} Bronze Cores`);parts.push(`${r.marks} marks`);
      body.push(h('div.card',h('b',item.name),h('p.small',item.desc),h('p.dim.small',parts.join(' · ')),UI.btn(G.craftCheck(g,id)||'Forge',()=>{
        const x=G.craft(g,id);if(x.error)return UI.toast(x.error,'bad');UI.save();UI.legacyCraftSheet();UI.toast(item.name+' crafted. Equip it from your bag.','good');
      },'small primary',G.craftCheck(g,id)?{disabled:true}:{})));
    }
    UI.sheet('Anvil Workshop',body);
  };
  UI.legacyBondSheet=function(){
    const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);
    const body=[h('p','Fighting beside a companion builds shared experience. At three victories together and sufficient trust, a personal decision unlocks a combat specialization.')];
    for(const [id,d] of Object.entries(COMP)){
      const st=G.bondState(g,id),ready=G.bondAvailable(g,id);
      body.push(h('div.card',h('b',W.ALLIES[id].name),h('p.dim.small',`${st.wins} victories together · Trust ${g.npc[id]?.trust||0}/${d.threshold}`),
        h('p.small',st.route?d[st.route+'Text']:d.desc),
        ready?UI.btn('Resolve '+d.title,()=>{
          UI.sheet(d.title,[h('p',d.desc)],[UI.btn(d.left,()=>{G.completeBond(g,id,'left');UI.save();UI.legacyBondSheet();},'primary'),UI.btn(d.right,()=>{G.completeBond(g,id,'right');UI.save();UI.legacyBondSheet();},'ghost')]);
        },'small primary'):null));
    }
    UI.sheet('Companion Bonds',body);
  };
  const prevHollow=S.hollow;
  S.hollow=function(root){prevHollow(root);const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);const id=g.dungeon||'glass',ds=UI.dstate(g,id),p=PASSAGES[id]?.[ds.room];
    if(ds.room>=1&&!g.legacy.hollowChoices[id+':sigil']&&!g.flags[W.DUNGEONS[id].doneFlag]&&SIGILS[id]){
      const lock=SIGILS[id],known=G.socialTags(g).has('Lore')||G.hasUtil(g,'scout');
      const puzzle=h('div.pad legacy-side',h('h3','Optional puzzle: '+lock.name),h('p.dim.small',known?lock.hint:'An old lock offers three symbols. Choosing incorrectly causes a warding shock.'),
        ...lock.options.map(opt=>UI.btn(opt,()=>{const r=G.solveHollowSigil(g,id,opt);if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');next(g);UI.toast(r.message,r.message.startsWith('The wrong')?'bad':'good');},'small ghost wide')));
      const nav=root.querySelector('nav.bottom');if(nav)root.insertBefore(puzzle,nav);else root.append(puzzle);
    }
    if(!p||g.flags[W.DUNGEONS[id].doneFlag]||g.legacy.hollowChoices[id+':'+ds.room])return;    if(!p||g.flags[W.DUNGEONS[id].doneFlag]||g.legacy.hollowChoices[id+':'+ds.room])return;
    const box=h('div.pad legacy-side',h('h3','Optional passage: '+p.name),h('p.dim.small','The main route remains available. Choose one alternative; the choice cannot be repeated.'),
      UI.btn('Keep to the safe route · recover health',()=>{const r=G.hollowSide(g,id,ds.room,'safe');if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');next(g);UI.toast(r.message,'good');},'wide ghost'),
      UI.btn('Study the hidden passage · Scout/Lore',()=>{const r=G.hollowSide(g,id,ds.room,'scout');if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');next(g);UI.toast(r.message,'good');},'wide ghost'),
      UI.btn('Explore the guarded cache · fight',()=>{const r=G.hollowSide(g,id,ds.room,'fight');if(r.error)return UI.toast(r.error,'bad');UI.save();UI.startCombat(r.enc,()=>UI.go('hollow'));},'wide primary'));
    const nav=root.querySelector('nav.bottom');if(nav)root.insertBefore(box,nav);else root.append(box);
  };
  const prevLoc=S.loc;
  S.loc=function(root,p){prevLoc(root,p);const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);
    const actions=root.querySelector('.acts');if(!actions)return;
    if(p.id==='magisterium'){
      if(G.trialReady(g)&&g.rank===2&&G.act3Stage(g)>=4)actions.append(act('Gold Trial — The Last Reflection','Prove that your seven mastered disciplines can stand together.',()=>UI.startCombat('trial_gold',()=>UI.go('loc',{id:'magisterium'})),'star'));
      actions.append(act('Combat Hall · no-risk drills','Practice real fights; review damage, resources and tactics.',()=>UI.legacyHallSheet(),'sword'));
      actions.append(act('Anvil Workshop · crafting','Forge build-defining equipment from expedition salvage.',()=>UI.legacyCraftSheet(),'gem'));
    }
    if(p.id==='lodge')actions.append(act('Companion bonds & loyalty','Personal decisions and combat specializations.',()=>UI.legacyBondSheet(),'chat'));
  };
  const prevTown=S.town;
  S.town=function(root,p){prevTown(root,p);const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);
    const pad=root.querySelector('.pad'),nav=root.querySelector('nav.bottom');if(!pad)return;
    const block=h('div.rankbox',h('h3','Horizons of Calderhal'),h('p.small',G.cityState(g).join(' ')));
    if(g.flags.act2_done&&g.rank>=2)block.append(UI.btn('Act III · The Thornwake March',()=>UI.frontier(),'primary wide'),h('p.dim.small',W.QUESTS.act3_horizons.stages[G.act3Stage(g)]));
    else block.append(h('p.dim.small','Thornwake expeditions open after Act II and Silver rank.'));
    block.append(UI.btn('Combat Hall / crafting',()=>UI.go('loc',{id:'magisterium'}),'ghost wide'));
    pad.append(block);
  };
  const prevJournal=S.journal;
  S.journal=function(root,p){prevJournal(root,p);const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);const pane=root.querySelector('.pad');if(!pane)return;
    if(p.tab==='quests'){
      if(g.flags.act2_done&&!g.quests.act3_horizons)G.startQuest(g,'act3_horizons');
      const st=G.act3Stage(g);if(g.quests.act3_horizons)pane.append(h('div.qline',h('b','Act III — Beyond the Ash'),h('div.dim.small',W.QUESTS.act3_horizons.stages[st]),UI.btn('Visit the March',()=>UI.frontier(),'small')));
    }
    if(p.tab==='factions')pane.append(h('h3','The city responds'),...G.cityState(g).map(t=>h('p.small',t)));
    if(p.tab==='people')pane.append(UI.btn('Companion loyalty stories',()=>UI.legacyBondSheet(),'wide ghost'));
  };
  const prevCharacter=S.char;
  if(prevCharacter)S.char=function(root,p){prevCharacter(root,p);const g=UI.g;if(g.rank<2||!G.fullSet(g))return;
    if(g.rank===2){const pane=root.querySelector('.pad');if(pane){const m=G.masteryInfo(g);pane.append(h('div.rankbox',h('h3','Gold mastery'),h('p.small',g.flags.act3_engine_stilled?`Mastery ${m.mature.length}/7. ${G.trialReady(g)?'Gold trial ready in Thornwake.':'Choose a training portfolio covering all five essences.'}`:'Confront the Last Cantor in Act III to unlock the Gold trial.')));}}
  };
  // Gold trait selection: non-destructive one-time sheet following breakthrough, mirrored by an entry in the character menu.
  UI.legacyGoldTraitSheet=function(){const g=UI.g;if(g.rank<3||!g.legacyGoldTraitPending)return UI.toast('No Gold trait choice is pending.');
    const choices=Object.keys(W.TRAITS).filter(id=>W.TRAITS[id].rank===3);
    UI.sheet('Gold transformation',[h('p',g.lastChange?.text||'Your essence settles into a new shape.'),h('p','Choose one permanent Gold trait:'),...choices.map(id=>UI.btn(W.TRAITS[id].name+' — '+W.TRAITS[id].text,()=>UI.confirm('Choose '+W.TRAITS[id].name,W.TRAITS[id].text,'Choose',()=>{G.takeTrait(g,id);g.legacyGoldTraitPending=false;UI.save();UI.closeSheet();UI.go('char');}),'wide ghost'))]);
  };
  const char=S.char;
  if(char)S.char=function(root,p){char(root,p);if(UI.g?.legacyGoldTraitPending){const pane=root.querySelector('.pad');if(pane)pane.prepend(UI.btn('Select your Gold breakthrough trait',()=>UI.legacyGoldTraitSheet(),'wide primary'));}};

  // ---- Part VIII: accessible settings, desktop layout, and maintenance-friendly metadata ----
  UI.applyLegacyPrefs=function(){const prefs=UI.g?.uiPrefs||{};const el=document.documentElement;
    el.classList.toggle('eb-large',!!prefs.large);el.classList.toggle('eb-reduced',!!prefs.reduced);el.classList.toggle('eb-wide',!!prefs.wide);
  };
  const prevGo=UI.go;
  UI.go=function(screen,p){UI.applyLegacyPrefs();return prevGo(screen,p);};
  const prevSettings=S.settings;
  S.settings=function(root,p){prevSettings(root,p);const g=UI.g;G.legacyEnsure(g);const pad=root.querySelector('.pad');if(!pad)return;
    const v=h('div.rankbox',h('h3','Accessibility and layout'),h('p.dim.small','These settings are saved per character. Reduced motion also applies to combat animations.'));
    for(const [key,name,desc] of [['large','Larger text','Increase reading and interface text size.'],['reduced','Reduce motion','Disable nonessential pulsing and transition animations.'],['wide','Wider layout','Use more screen width on desktop displays.']])v.append(UI.btn((g.uiPrefs[key]?'✓ ':'○ ')+name,()=>{g.uiPrefs[key]=!g.uiPrefs[key];UI.applyLegacyPrefs();next(g);},'small ghost wide'),h('p.dim.small',desc));
    v.append(h('p.dim.small',`Content: ${Object.keys(C().abilities).length.toLocaleString()} abilities · ${Object.keys(C().essences).length} essences · ${Object.keys(C().stones).length} Awakening Stones.`));
    pad.append(v);
  };
  // Browser keyboard access: native Tab/Enter continue to work; these shortcuts ease desktop navigation.
  document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
    if(e.key==='Escape'&&document.querySelector('#sheet')){UI.closeSheet();return;}
    if(!e.altKey||e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||!/^[1-5]$/.test(e.key))return;
    if(['INPUT','TEXTAREA','SELECT'].includes(document.activeElement?.tagName))return;
    if(!UI.g||UI.screen==='combat'||UI.screen==='scene'||UI.g.pendingFight)return;
    e.preventDefault();const dest={1:'town',2:'char',3:'bag',4:'journal',5:'settings'}[Number(e.key)];
    if(document.querySelector('#sheet'))UI.closeSheet();UI.go(dest);
  });
  const title=S.title;
  S.title=function(root){title(root);const t=root.querySelector('.title-sub');if(t)t.textContent='A Calderhal saga. Iron to Gold.';};
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});

(function (EB) {
  'use strict';
  function boot() {
    EB.ensureFX();
    // resume an interrupted fight? We drop it and return to the last location (the fight is forfeit, no penalty).
    EB.UI.go('title');
    window.addEventListener('beforeunload', () => EB.UI.save());
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => { if (document.hidden) EB.UI.save(); });
  }
  if (document.readyState === 'loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot); else boot();
  window.addEventListener('error', (e) => { try { EB.UI.toast('Something broke: ' + (e.message || 'error'), 'bad'); } catch (x) {} });
})(globalThis.EB = globalThis.EB || {});
</script>
</body></html>
